Method: 在三个 MFM（Gemma-4-12B-it, MiniCPM-o-4.5, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B）中，以语音情感识别和面部表情识别作为互补探针，识别与情感类别选择性关联的稀疏 decoder 神经元（即情感敏感神经元 ESNs）。通过消融（deactivation）和激活增强（steering）实验验证视觉 ESNs 的因果作用，并通过跨模态干预测试因果迁移。

Result: 视觉 ESNs 具有因果意义：消融会选择性损害对应面部情感的识别，激活增强则选择性提升该情感的识别。声学与视觉 ESNs 表现出情感匹配的重叠和相似的逐层分布，表明情感表征部分对齐。跨模态干预显示双向因果迁移：来自一个模态的 ESNs 施加到另一模态产生情感特异性效果。

Conclusion: 语音和面部情感识别在 MFMs 中部分汇聚到稀疏的 decoder 级共享成分上，这些成分可以被定位并在无需训练的情况下进行操控，为多模态模型情感表征的结构理解提供了新的激活级视角。

Abstract: Modern multimodal foundation models (MFMs) have made rapid progress on tasks requiring integrated perception across speech, vision, and language, including emotion recognition. However, it remains unclear whether they recognize speech and facial emotion through shared affective functional units or modality-specific pathways. We explore emotion-sensitive neurons (ESNs), sparse decoder neurons selectively associated with emotion categories, in three MFMs: Gemma-4-12B-it, MiniCPM-o-4.5, and Qwen2.5-Omni-7B. Using speech emotion recognition and facial expression recognition as complementary probes, we identify acoustic and visual ESNs. Visual ESNs are causally meaningful: deactivating them selectively impairs recognition of the associated facial emotion, whereas steering their activations selectively enhances recognition of that emotion relative to other emotion categories. Acoustic and visual ESNs further show emotion-matched overlap and similar layer-wise distributions, indicating partial structural alignment between affective representations across speech and faces. Finally, cross-modal interventions reveal bidirectional causal transfer: ESNs identified from one modality produce emotion-specific effects when applied to the other. Our findings provide one of the first cross-modality activation-level analyses of affective functional units in MFMs, suggesting that speech and facial emotion recognition partially converge onto sparse decoder-level components that can be localized and manipulated without training.

</details>


### [98] [Children, but not language models, show accelerating returns in word learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17120)
*Michael C. Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 儿童词汇学习呈现加速累积模式（每单位语言经验的学习收益递增），而语言模型（包括基于儿童导向语言训练的模型）仅呈现恒定比例收益（符合缩放定律）。儿童能以远少于语言模型的数据量实现词汇学习，其输入利用效率随经验增长而提高可能是关键原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往模型将词汇增长描述为随时间推移的证据累积（通常隐含线性或递减收益），但本文旨在揭示儿童词汇学习的真实动态模式，并对比儿童与语言模型在学习效率上的本质差异，从而解释为何儿童能以少得多的数据实现词汇习得。

Method: 通过对比儿童词汇学习过程与语言模型（包括以儿童导向语言训练的语言模型）的词汇增长曲线，分析两者的学习效率模式——检验儿童学习是否符合加速累积模型（边际收益递增）而非恒定收益模型，同时对照语言模型的缩放定律行为。

Result: 发现儿童的词汇增长呈现加速累积特征：每额外增加一个单位的语言输入，儿童从中获得的词汇学习收益高于此前单位输入带来的收益。相比之下，语言模型无论是否使用儿童导向语言训练，都表现为恒定比例收益，符合典型的缩放定律，即学习效率不随数据量增加而改善。

Conclusion: 儿童与其训练数据之间日益增长的利用效率（加速累积）是解释其能在极少训练数据下实现词汇习得的关键因素，而当前语言模型缺乏这种随经验递增的学习效率，因此依赖海量数据。这一发现提示未来语言模型设计应朝向更具效率的"递增收益"学习机制发展。

Abstract: Children learn hundreds of words over the first years of their lives, in a process that begins slowly but quickly picks up speed. Prior models describe vocabulary growth as evidence accumulation over time. Here we show that the process is best characterized as accelerating accumulation: children learn more from each additional unit of linguistic experience than they did from the one before. In contrast to children, language models -- even those trained on child-directed speech -- do not accelerate. Instead, they show constant proportional returns on new data, consistent with scaling laws. Children learn using many orders of magnitude less training data than language models; their increasingly efficient use of their learning input is a candidate explanation.

</details>


### [99] [Towards Safer RAG: Only Agents Capable of System 2 Thinking may Access Untrusted Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17153)
*Mehrdad Ghassabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的RAG安全原则：只有具备System 2深思熟虑推理能力的智能体才能访问不可信文档，并引入新指标量化"错误信息检测"与"下游影响"之间的差异，实验证明推理型语言模型比标准模型对污染证据更具鲁棒性，且不需要Cordon原则的严格隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统易受知识投毒攻击，模型即使能检测到文档中的错误信息，仍可能被其影响输出。已有的Cordon原则通过隔离原始证据来防御，但带来大量计算开销，需要更实用的安全方案。

Method: 提出"仅允许具备System 2审慎推理能力的智能体访问不可信文档"的精炼安全原则；设计新的评估指标来量化错误信息检测与下游影响之间的差异；在提出的指标上对比最先进的推理语言模型与标准语言模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 推理能力强的模型在面对被污染的检索证据时表现出显著更强的鲁棒性，且无需Cordon原则所要求的严格隔离机制。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持所提出的精炼安全原则，为RAG系统的安全设计提供了更实用、更低开销的替代方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has significantly enhanced the performance of large language models (LLMs), yet these systems remain vulnerable to knowledge-poisoning attacks, in which misinformation in retrieved documents can influence the model's final outputs. Notably, an LLM may correctly detect that a document contains incorrect information while nevertheless being influenced by it. Prior work has addressed this vulnerability through the Cordon Principle, which prevents models responsible for final answer synthesis from directly accessing raw evidence. Although effective, this strict isolation can introduce substantial computational overhead. In this work, we propose a refined security principle: only agents capable of deliberative System 2 reasoning may access untrusted documents. To evaluate this principle, we introduce novel metrics that quantify the discrepancy between misinformation detection and downstream influence. We then empirically compare state-of-the-art reasoning language models with standard language models across these metrics. Our results show that reasoning-capable models are substantially more robust to corrupted evidence, without requiring the strict isolation imposed by the Cordon Principle. These findings provide empirical support for our refined principle and suggest a more practical foundation for secure RAG system design.

</details>


### [100] [Can LLMs Reason in a Legally Meaningful Manner? A Small-scale Study on European Court of Human Rights Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17168)
*Amogh Raina,Ilias Chalkidis,Daniel Hershcovich,Henrik Palmer Olsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在法律案件预测任务中的推理能力，以欧洲人权法院案件为测试集，评估了GPT 5.4在不同提示策略下的表现。研究发现模型在法律推理上远未达到理想水平，其分析结构性完整但实质浅薄，且LLM评估虽内部一致但与人工评估弱对齐，专家提示可提升推理全面性但无助于预测准确率，呼吁社区不要仅依赖LLM自动评估或将任务准确率作为推理质量的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理已成为现代LLM的标准能力和特征，但其在法律导向型任务（如法律案件预测）中的应用和质量仍未被充分探索。作者希望通过实证研究揭示LLM在专业法律推理场景下的真实表现，并评估不同提示策略对推理质量的影响。

Method: 以欧洲人权法院（ECtHR）的法律案件为测试平台，采用多种提示策略（从较少暗示到高度暗示法律有意义推理的标准），评估OpenAI GPT 5.4的表现。通过人工标注者和LLM-as-a-Judge两种方式对模型输出进行评估，并对比两者的一致性。

Result: 研究发现：1）模型在法律推理得分上远未达理想水平；2）模型产出结构完整但实质分析浅薄；3）LLM-as-a-Judge评估内部一致但与训练有素的标注者弱对齐（可靠但非有效替代）；4）专家策划的提示比普通提示产生更全面的推理，但并未带来更准确的预测。

Conclusion: 不应仅依赖自动化LLM评估来评判法律推理质量，也不应将任务准确率作为推理质量的合适代理指标。建议社区结合人工评估并区分推理质量与任务表现两个维度。

Abstract: Reasoning has become a standard technique and feature for contemporary LLMs; however, its application and quality in the context of demanding legal-oriented tasks, such as legal case forecasting, remain under explored. We investigate how LLMs reason in the context of legal case forecasting, using legal cases from the European Court of Human Rights (ECtHR) as a testbed. We evaluate OpenAI GPT 5.4, a recent top-tier LLM, by exploring alternative prompting strategies that are more or less suggestive of what counts as legally meaningful reasoning in the context of ECtHR jurisprudence. We present our findings derived from assessing the model's responses with both human and LLM evaluation. We find that the examined model scores far from ideal in legal reasoning, the model produces structurally complete but substantively shallow analyses, and that LLM-as-a-Judge evaluators are internally consistent yet align only weakly with our trained annotators, i.e., reliable but not a valid substitute for human evaluation. Overall, the expert-curated prompt leads to more comprehensive reasoning, which does not result in more accurate predictions compared to the other examined settings. Based on our findings, we urge the community not to rely solely on automated LLM-based evaluation and to avoid using task accuracy as an appropriate proxy for reasoning quality.

</details>


### [101] [Polaris: Learning to Generate Table Descriptions from Retrieval Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17171)
*Ting Cai,Tuan Minh Phan,AnHai Doan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Polaris 通过直接偏好优化（DPO）训练 LLM 生成面向检索效果的表描述，显著提升表格检索性能，超越了现有最优的 AutoDDG 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法用 LLM 生成自然语言表描述来提升表格检索，但这些描述通常针对流畅性而非检索效果进行优化，导致检索性能不是最优。作者希望直接从检索反馈中训练 LLM 生成检索导向的表描述。

Method: 核心洞察是现有的表格检索基准已包含训练所需的监督信号：给定查询-表相关性判断，为每张表生成多个候选描述，按 BM25 检索效果排序形成偏好对，使用 DPO 对 LLM 进行微调。此外，Polaris 在生成前会展开缩写的表名和列名以减少词汇不匹配。

Result: 大量实验表明 Polaris 在多个基准上显著优于现有的 SOTA 方案 AutoDDG。

Conclusion: 研究证明检索基准可以被重新用作监督信号来训练 LLM 生成面向检索的元数据（表描述），为表格检索领域提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: Many table-centric NLP tasks such as NL2SQL first retrieve relevant tables from large collections using keyword search. Recent work uses LLMs to generate natural-language table descriptions to improve retrieval, but they are typically optimized for fluency rather than retrieval effectiveness. We present Polaris, a system that trains an LLM to generate table descriptions directly from retrieval feedback. Our key insight is that existing table retrieval benchmarks already contain the supervision needed for this task: given query-table relevance judgments, we generate multiple candidate descriptions for each table, rank them by their BM25 retrieval effectiveness, and use the resulting preference pairs to fine-tune the LLM with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Polaris further expands abbreviated table and column names before generation to reduce vocabulary mismatch. Extensive experiments show that Polaris outperforms the state-of-the-art AutoDDG solution, often by a significant margin. More broadly, our results demonstrate that retrieval benchmarks can be repurposed as supervision for training LLMs to generate retrieval-oriented metadata.

</details>


### [102] [AISA: AI Safety Assistant Framework for Continuous Improvement of Highway Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17184)
*Mason Smetana,Trevor Neece,Lev Khazanovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以大型语言模型（LLMs）为核心的高速公路施工安全报告与规划框架，旨在通过本地推理实现事故叙事的分类与质量评分，以及历史事故、图像和行业文档的检索，为未来agentic应用奠定基础。OIICS分类达到75%的留出集准确率，事故检索效果显著优于随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作业安全分析（JSA）和任务前规划可以从历史事故记录中获益，但现有事故数据通常以非结构化叙事文本存储，难以在规划时点直接查阅。需要一个既能处理现有非结构化数据又能支撑未来智能化应用的框架。

Method: 提出基于LLM的框架，包含两大核心：1）训练神经探针（neural probes）对事故叙事进行四类多分类和两类二分类的OIICS分类，并生成整体质量评分；2）利用文本嵌入模型对历史事故、参考图像和行业文档进行检索，服务于日常安全计划。使用超过15,000条叙事作为测试集，100条作者标注记录作为留出集，并以多数投票LLM集成作为基准。

Result: OIICS分类在留出集上达到75%准确率，但两个二元标志退化；质量评分在单一数据库上有意义，但在留出集的分布外死亡案例上失真；事故检索显著优于随机水平，在词汇差异较大的施工活动上表现最佳；文档问答中，开源权重解码器嵌入模型优于专有模型。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个以本地推理和文本嵌入模型为核心的新框架，为未来agentic应用奠定基础，重点在于将外部数据桥接到JSA报告中。

Abstract: Job Safety Analysis (JSA) and pre-task planning can benefit from prior incident records, yet historical accident data is often stored as unstructured narratives that are difficult to consult at the point of planning. A novel framework centered on large language models (LLMs) for highway construction safety reporting and planning is proposed as a foundation for future agentic applications, prioritizing deterministic, local inferencing. The first aim is to enable classification and quality scoring of incident narratives for existing and future reporting purposes. The second is to evaluate retrieval of relevant historical accidents, related imagery, and trusted industry documents for incorporation into daily safety plans. Neural probes were trained to classify incidents along four multiclass and two binary Occupational Injury and Illness Classification System (OIICS) fields and to derive an overall quality score, evaluated on a test set of over 15,000 narratives and a held-out set of 100 author-labeled records, benchmarked against a majority-vote LLM ensemble. The retrieval of historical accidents, reference imagery, and industry documents was benchmarked across embedding models using standard information retrieval metrics. OIICS classification reached 75% held-out accuracy, though the two binary flags were degenerate. The quality score, while meaningful on one database, was distorted on out-of-distribution fatalities in the held-out dataset. Accident retrieval recovered relevant incidents far above chance, performing best on lexically distinct construction activities. On document question answering, an open-weight decoder embedding model surpassed proprietary models. Overall, this work provides a new framework rooted in local inferencing and text embedding models for future agentic applications, with emphasis on bridging external data to JSA reports.

</details>


### [103] [Token Optimization and Context Window Management in Multi-Agent AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17188)
*Dvir Shamay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向多智能体AI工作流的token优化与上下文窗口管理实践框架，包含六种工程模式，并在生产环境中实现冷加载延迟从3.5-10.5分钟降至61-116秒，estimated token消耗减少60-70%。同时通过2,420次受控试验发现"相关性对比上下文"效应：在固定10条提示项下，混入同域低相关项比仅用高相关项能提升模型的目标项相关性评分一致性（+0.077, Cohen's d=0.49）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI工作流不仅受限于模型质量，还受限于token成本、延迟和上下文窗口质量。作者旨在补齐模型研究与生产部署之间的工程层，提供可重复的模式和评估方法，以实现更快、更便宜、更可靠的工作流。

Method: 基于内部生产仪表盘（从会议、邮件、聊天中提取结构化工作项并路由摘要），提出六种模式：上下文分层（context stratification）、一次获取/本地处理架构（fetch-once/process-locally）、模式压缩提示（schema-contracted prompts）、token感知回退链（token-aware fallback chains）、语义缓存（semantic caching）、智能体间通信压缩。通过受控上下文构成研究（2,420次试验、11种模型配置、661个匿名工作项）测试相关性对比上下文假设。

Result: 生产环境：冷加载延迟降至61-116秒（6次计时），基线为3.5-10.5分钟；token减少60-70%。受控研究：50:50信号/噪声条件下相比100%高相关条件，目标项相关性评分一致性提升+0.077（95% CI [+0.056,+0.098], d=0.49, p<.001）；9个模型族层面效应为+0.084（[+0.064,+0.103]）。Fusion-of-N后续实验显示学习式融合未超越机械集合取并集。

Conclusion: 论文贡献是一个可测量的工程层，介于模型研究与生产智能体实践之间。相关性对比上下文是一种稳健的上下文构成效应，但需注意统计独立性限制，作者将多模型族分析明确定位为语料内描述性比较而非总体推断。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI workflows are limited not only by model quality but by token cost, latency, and context-window quality. This paper presents a practitioner framework for token optimization and context-window management, grounded in an internal production dashboard that extracts structured work items from meetings, email, and chat with LLMs and routes summaries across workstreams. Six patterns are described: context stratification, fetch-once/process-locally architecture, schema-contracted prompts, token-aware fallback chains, semantic caching, and inter-agent communication compression. In production they cut measured cold-load latency to 61-116 seconds (six timed runs) from an operational baseline of roughly 3.5-10.5 minutes, with an estimated 60-70% token reduction. It also reports a controlled context-composition study: 2,420 confirmatory trials across 11 model configurations, using 661 anonymized workplace items scored for relevance. Holding the prompt at a fixed ten items, replacing some high-relevance items with same-domain low-relevance items improves the model's relevance-score concordance on the target items, versus high-relevance items only; we call this relevance-contrast context. In the all-11 paired analysis, the 50:50 signal/noise condition improved relevance accuracy by +0.077 over the 100% condition (naive 95% CI [+0.056, +0.098], Cohen's d = 0.49, Holm-adjusted p < .001, n = 220). These cells are not independent; by the nine model families the effect is +0.084 (95% interval [+0.064, +0.103]), reported as a within-corpus descriptive comparison, not a population inference. A Fusion-of-N follow-up found that learned synthesis did not beat the mechanical set union of item IDs. The contribution is a measured engineering layer between model research and production agent practice: repeatable patterns and evaluation methods for faster, cheaper, more reliable workflows.

</details>


### [104] [Which Source Wins? Task-Dependent Reliance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17205)
*Rodela Ghosh,Aviral Gupta,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在图像与文本冲突时如何重新分配模态依赖。作者在GSM8K和SVAMP上构建算术问题冲突对，并引入手动审核的ChartQA-Conflict基准（229个图表-报告冲突样本），评估六个开源VLM。结果发现：在算术任务中，五个模型对退化文本的规避强于退化图像；而在ChartQA-Conflict上，六个模型均表现出相反模式（更强地规避退化视觉源）。这一反转在校准后仍然存在，两个前沿API模型也复现了该现象，表明VLM的模态依赖并非固定，而是随任务、证据结构、模型和评估设置而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对VLM的模态依赖缺乏系统性的受控研究，当图像与文本内容冲突且一方可读性退化时，模型如何在两种模态间重新分配依赖尚不清楚。作者希望通过可控实验量化这种模态重分配行为。

Method: 构建受控冲突设置：将GSM8K和SVAMP中一个算术问题的渲染图像与另一个问题的文本配对，使两种来源支持不同答案；引入ChartQA-Conflict手动审核基准（229例图表-报告冲突，含匹配的图表和表格图像表示）。在四种可读性退化水平下，分别退化图像或文本，使用生成答案和长度归一化条件对数似然边际两种方式评估六个开源VLM。

Result: 在GSM8K和SVAMP上，六个模型中五个对退化文本的偏移强于退化图像；在ChartQA-Conflict上，全部六个采用似然评分的模型呈现相反模式（对退化视觉源的偏移更强）。校准单模态准确率损失后反转仍存在，替换为纯表格图像后亦然。GPT-5.6-Luna和Gemini-3.5-Flash两个API模型复现了ChartQA-Conflict的反转，GPT-5.6-Luna还匹配了算术任务的方向。

Conclusion: VLM的模态依赖不是固定属性，而是随任务类型、证据结构（图像vs文本）、具体模型和评估方法而动态变化。该发现对多模态系统的鲁棒性和安全部署具有重要启示，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) combine images and text, but when the two conflict and one becomes harder to read, it is unclear how a model shifts its reliance between them. We study this modality reallocation with a controlled setup: we degrade either the image or the text across four levels of legibility while keeping the other clean, and track how the model's preference changes. We build conflicts from GSM8K and SVAMP by pairing the rendered image of one arithmetic problem with the text of another, so the two sources support different answers. We also introduce ChartQA-Conflict, a manually reviewed benchmark of 229 chart-report conflicts with matched chart and table-image representations. We evaluate six open-weight VLMs using both generated answers and a length-normalized conditional log-likelihood margin. On GSM8K and SVAMP, five of six models shift more strongly away from degraded text than from degraded images. On ChartQA-Conflict, all six likelihood-scored models exhibit the opposite pattern, shifting more strongly away from the degraded visual source. This reversal persists after calibrating for unimodal accuracy loss and after replacing charts with plain table images. Two frontier API models, GPT-5.6-Luna and Gemini-3.5-Flash, behaviorally replicate the ChartQA-Conflict reversal, with GPT-5.6-Luna also matching the arithmetic direction. These results show that modality reliance in VLMs is not fixed, but varies across tasks, evidence structures, models, and evaluation settings. The source code is available at https://github.com/Ro-netizen004/multimodal-arbitration-artifact.

</details>


### [105] [The Plot Thins: Uniformity and Linearity in Literary Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17218)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Sil Hamilton,David Mimno,Ross Deans Kristensen-McLachlan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个将150部小说摘要中的句子映射到其来源章节的数据集，发现该任务对人类和模型标注者都异常困难，并基于此衡量摘要的线性度（保持原始事件顺序的程度）和均匀度（在各章节间均匀分配注意力的程度），从而揭示文学作品与摘要表达情节的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学作品平衡了情节、悬念、惊喜和艺术表达等多个要素，而摘要优先关注情节，因此可能偏离原作。作者希望系统地量化摘要与原著之间的偏差，理解摘要在多大程度上保持了原作的事件顺序（线性度）和注意力分布（均匀度），从而揭示文学表达与摘要表达之间的结构性差异。

Method: 结合人工标注和基于LLM的标注方法，构建了一个数据集，将150部小说摘要中的句子映射到其对应的来源章节。基于这些句子到章节的映射，测量摘要的线性度（保持原作事件顺序的程度）和均匀度（摘要对各个章节注意力分配的均衡程度），并分析摘要何时以及如何破坏线性和均匀性。

Result: 发现句-章映射任务对人类和模型标注者都出乎意料地困难；通过测量线性度和均匀度，识别出文学作品与摘要表达情节的差异，特别是叙事细节在清晰度和显著性方面的处理方式不同。

Conclusion: 摘要与文学作品在表达情节的方式上存在本质差异：摘要倾向于更清晰、更显著地呈现叙事细节，这导致其在保持线性度和均匀度方面与原著产生系统性偏差；该研究为理解自动摘要和人工摘要与长篇文学之间的结构关系提供了新视角。

Abstract: Works of literature are complicated; they balance plot, suspense, surprise, and artistic expression. Summaries of literature prioritize plot, and therefore may deviate from their sources. Using a combination of manual and LLM-based annotation, we construct a dataset mapping sentences from 150 novel summaries to their respective source chapters. We find the task unexpectedly difficult for both human and model annotators. Using the sentence-to-chapter mappings, we then measure summary linearity, the degree to which it maintains the source's order of events, and uniformity, the degree to which a summary spreads attention equally across a source. By examining when and how summaries break linearity and uniformity, we identify differences in how literary works and summaries express plot, particularly with regard to the clarity and prominence with which narrative details are described.

</details>


### [106] [Temporal Leakage in Financial News NLP: A Multi-Architecture Audit with a Regime-Specific M&A Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17223)
*Chenhao Xue,Raslen Guesmi,Siwei Feng,Yucheng Gong,Jacob Xavier Sundram,Jordan Pang,Lan Wang,Julian Kaljuvee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了金融新闻方向预测中时序泄漏的影响，使用49,799篇文章语料和16种特征-模型组合发现：随机划分相比时序划分会使MCC膨胀1.1至6.5倍，且膨胀程度随模型能力和特征丰富度递增；端到端微调不仅未缩小反而放大了该差距。在近时序评估下，并购（M&A）事件是唯一具有显著正信号的类别（TF-IDF MCC=0.138），但该信号无法迁移至FNSPID的2009-2020美国语料，说明其局限于2024-2025欧洲偏向的并购语义而非通用预测因子。作者主张将泄漏审计作为金融NLP基准的强制披露项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融新闻方向预测已成为广受欢迎的NLP基准任务，但报告的收益高度依赖于训练-测试划分是时序的还是随机的，即存在时间泄漏问题。然而此前缺乏对该依赖性的系统性量化审计，作者旨在量化这一影响并定位可泛化的真实信号。

Method: 在49,799篇文章语料上，对16种特征-模型组合（涵盖TF-IDF、MiniLM、FinBERT、微调RoBERTa-large/DeBERTa-v3-large）以及Llama-3和Qwen2.5 LLM的zero/few-shot与LoRA探针，分别比较随机划分与时序划分下的MCC表现；进一步按事件类型分类评估时序锁定测试信号，并通过跨语料（FNSPID 2009-2020美国数据）验证信号的通用性；使用三个独立角色标注器定位信号来源。

Result: 随机划分使MCC膨胀1.1至6.5倍，膨胀倍数随模型能力与特征丰富度递增；端到端FinBERT微调的膨胀比率为1.75倍（规模匹配后），说明微调放大而非弥合差距。在近时序评估下，并购事件是唯一具有显著正信号（MCC=0.138）的类别，但其无法迁移至FNSPID语料，信号局限于2024-2025欧洲偏向的并购语义。三个独立标注器收敛于"收购方"标记文章为信号定位点。

Conclusion: 时序划分对于金融NLP的作用类似于特征净化（characteristics-purging）对于资产定价的作用，它剥离了新闻中可预测的陈旧成分，留下的是微小、事件局部化且词汇浅层的残差信号。作者据此倡导将泄漏审计作为金融NLP基准的强制性披露要求。

Abstract: Financial-news direction prediction has become a popular NLP benchmark, yet reported gains depend critically on whether the train-test split is chronological or random, i.e., on temporal leakage. We audit this dependence on a 49,799-article corpus across 16 feature-model combinations spanning TF-IDF, MiniLM, FinBERT, and fine-tuned RoBERTa-large / DeBERTa-v3-large, plus separate zero/few-shot and LoRA probes of Llama-3 and Qwen2.5 LLMs: random splits inflate MCC by $1.1\times$ to $6.5\times$, tracking model capacity and feature richness, and end-to-end FinBERT fine-tuning re-amplifies rather than closes the gap (size-matched ratio $1.75\times$). Conditioning on event type, mergers and acquisitions (M&A) is the only audited category with a positive locked-test signal under near-temporal chronological evaluation (TF-IDF MCC $= 0.138$ train-only, $0.068$ under train$\cup$val refit; 10,000-permutation $p < 10^{-3}$); the signal does not transfer to FNSPID's 2009-2020 U.S. corpus, localising the headline to our 2024-2025 European-tilted M&A semantics rather than a universal predictor. Three independent role labellers converge on acquirer-tagged articles as the signal locus, a power-limited qualitative convergence rather than a hypothesis-tested asymmetry. Chronological splitting plays for financial NLP the role characteristics-purging plays for asset pricing: it strips the predictable, stale component of news and leaves a residual that is small, event-localized, and lexically shallow. We advocate leakage audits as a required disclosure for financial-NLP benchmarks.

</details>


### [107] [Q-Interference: Memory-Efficient Phase-Aware Quantum-Inspired Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17288)
*Emama Nahid,Tahmid Imtiaz Imu,Huayue Gu,Liran Ma,Zhipeng Cai,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Interference，一种受量子启发的完全经典注意力机制，为每个query和key特征附加振幅和相位，使注意力分数具有相位感知能力（相位对齐增强、冲突抵消）。针对朴素实现需要大规模中间张量导致内存密集的问题，提出精确三角分解，将其化简为两次标准矩阵乘法，无需物化中间张量，从而在标准GPT框架中实现内存高效的相位感知干扰注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT的标准点积注意力仅通过点积相似性度量token兼容性，但无法显式建模强token特征间是相互增强还是抑制的关系。研究者希望引入更丰富的交互规则（相位感知的增强/抵消），同时保持内存效率，使这种机制在标准GPT管道中实用。

Method: Q-Interference为每个query和key特征引入振幅（amplitude）和可学习相位（phase）。注意力分数由相位感知的干扰规则决定：对齐相位构建性叠加，冲突相位破坏性叠加。为解决朴素实现需要大型token-pair-feature交互张量的内存瓶颈，提出精确三角分解（trigonometric factorization），将同一分数计算转化为两次标准矩阵乘法，避免物化中间大张量。该方法直接嵌入Transformer块，不改变其余模型架构和下一token预测目标。

Result: 在公开基准数据集和基线模型上的实验表明：该重配方在受控GPT风格设置下能够稳定训练，相比朴素相位感知干扰注意力具有一致的内存优势。这证实了精确内存高效分解方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Q-Interference的主要贡献在于提供了一种精确且内存高效的数学分解，使得相位感知干扰注意力能够在标准GPT流水线中实际部署，同时保留更丰富的token交互建模能力。实验结果支持该方法的训练稳定性和内存效率优势。

Abstract: GPT attention measures token compatibility through dot-product similarity. This mechanism is simple, effective, and memory-efficient. But it does not explicitly model whether strong token features should reinforce or suppress one another. We introduce Q-Interference, a fully classical quantum-inspired attention mechanism for autoregressive language modeling that augments each query and key feature with an amplitude and a learned phase. The resulting attention score is phase-aware which aligned phases contribute constructively while conflicting phases contribute destructively. Although Q-Interference yields a richer interaction rule than similarity alone, a naive implementation of Q-Interference requires a large token-pair-feature interaction tensor, making it memory-intensive and often impractical. To address this limitation, we propose an exact trigonometric factorization that computes the same score using two standard matrix multiplications avoiding materialization of the large intermediate tensor. Q-Interference fits directly into a Transformer block in GPT and leaves the remainder of the model architecture and next-token prediction objective unchanged. Experiments on public benchmark datasets and baseline models show that the proposed reformulation trains stably in a controlled GPT-style setting and provides a consistent memory advantage over naive phase-aware interference attention. These results support the specific contribution of this work: an exact memory-efficient reformulation that makes phase-aware interference attention practical within a standard GPT pipeline.

</details>


### [108] [What Tokens are Learned when Tokenization is Optimized Jointly with Language Modeling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17325)
*Saketh Reddy Vemula,Parameswari Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了当分词与语言建模联合优化时，模型会学习到什么样的token。通过在18种语言上对比无分词器方法（SSLMs和H-Nets）与固定分词器，发现联合优化会从根本上改变token结构：SSLMs恢复形态对齐且上下文高效的token，H-Nets则偏向字节级效率。不同语言类型表现出不同的分词行为，且基于SSLM的预分词在困惑度和下游任务上均具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词是语言建模流程的基础组件，但目前分词器通常是固定的，即使它显著影响模型在不同语言上的性能。作者希望探究当分词与语言建模联合优化时，模型究竟会学到什么样的token，以理解tokenizer-free方法的内在机制。

Method: 在18种类型学和文字体系多样的语言上开展实验，系统比较无分词器方法（SSLMs和H-Nets）与固定分词器。通过联合优化分词和语言建模，分析学习到的token结构，并对黏着语等不同语言类型进行分类分析，同时进行基于SSLM的预分词+微调BERT的下游评估。

Result: 联合优化从根本上改变了token结构。SSLMs恢复形态对齐且上下文高效的token；H-Nets追求字节级效率，产生更长且与标准子词词汇表重叠度极低的token。黏着语在训练中表现出更动态的分割模式。在预训练-微调BERT的下游评估中，SSLM预分词持续降低语言建模困惑度，并取得有竞争力的下游性能。

Conclusion: 无分词器方法优化的是上下文和计算效率而非严格的形态结构，从而生成与固定分词器根本不同但同样有效的词汇表，为下游NLP提供了可行且高效的分词替代方案。

Abstract: Tokenization is a fundamental component of language modeling pipelines. Despite its importance, it is often fixed, even though it significantly impacts model performance across languages. In this work, we analyze what tokens are learned when tokenization is jointly optimized with language modeling. We compare tokenizer-free approaches such as SSLMs and H-Nets with fixed tokenizers across 18 typologically and script-diverse languages. Our results show that joint optimization fundamentally alters token structure. SSLMs recover morphologically aligned and contextually efficient tokens, whereas H-Nets prioritize byte-level efficiency, producing longer tokens with very low overlap with standard subword vocabularies. We further show that tokenization behavior varies across language typologies. Agglutinative languages exhibit more dynamic segmentation patterns while learning. Through downstream evaluation, with pretrained-then-finetuned BERT models, we find that SSLM-based pretokenization consistently reduces language modeling perplexity and achieves competitive downstream performance despite distinct vocabularies. Overall, tokenizer-free approaches optimize for contextual and computational efficiency rather than strict morphological structure, resulting in fundamentally different yet effective vocabularies for downstream NLP.

</details>


### [109] [ArguLens: An Open-Source System for Automated Essay Scoring and Label-Aware Feedback Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17356)
*Weiran Wang,Hongxiang Shi,Huitao Tang,Wenjuan Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ArguLens是一个开源、本地可部署的自动作文评分系统，将评分任务分解为话语移动分类器（Qwen2.5-7B，LoRA微调）、基于LightGBM的评分器（31个语言学与话语特征）和基于vLLM的反馈生成器三个解耦组件。在PERSUADE 2.0数据集上，分类器达到82.6%准确率/0.727宏F1，评分器在oracle话语特征协议下平均QWK为0.813，gold话语注释带来+0.055 QWK增量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动作文评分系统只能输出单一的总体分数，缺乏可解释性证据；同时依赖封闭API，带来数据隐私和成本问题。作者旨在构建一个开源、本地部署、可解释的评分系统来应对这些问题。

Method: 系统分为三个解耦组件：(1) 基于Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct并用LoRA在PERSUADE 2.0上微调的话语移动分类器；(2) 基于31个语言学和话语特征、与年级无关的LightGBM评分器；(3) 以Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct为骨干、通过vLLM服务的标签感知反馈生成器。系统通过Gradio UI提供可插拔推理后端，支持单篇和批量评分。

Result: 在作文不重叠的PERSUADE 2.0测试集上，logitprobe分类器准确率为82.6%、宏F1为0.727；在按提示词分组的5折交叉验证下，oracle话语特征协议的平均QWK为0.813；消融实验表明加入gold话语注释相比仅词法+句法配置提升+0.055 QWK（配对t检验，p=0.010）。

Conclusion: ArguLens证明了组件级解耦的AES架构的可行性，话语结构信息对评分有显著贡献。但作者明确指出这是组件级诊断而非端到端结果，人类评分员评估留待未来工作。系统已以Apache 2.0协议开源。

Abstract: Most automated essay scoring (AES) systems output a single holistic score without interpretable evidence and rely on closed APIs that introduce data privacy and cost barriers. We present ArguLens, an opensource, locally deployable system that decomposes AES into three decoupled components: a discourse-move classifier (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct fine-tuned with LoRA on PERSUADE 2.0), a grade-independent LightGBM scorer over 31 linguistic and discourse features, and a label-aware feedback generator served through vLLM with a Qwen2.5-14BInstruct backbone. A Gradio web UI exposes pluggable inference backends and supports single-essay and batch scoring with downloadable per-essay breakdowns. On an essaydisjoint PERSUADE 2.0 test split, the logitprobe classifier achieves 82.6% accuracy and 0.727 macro-F1; under prompt-grouped 5-fold cross-validation the scorer reaches a mean QWK of 0.813 under an oracle discoursefeature protocol, and an ablation shows that adding gold discourse annotations yields an increment of +0.055 QWK over the lexical+syntactic configuration (paired t-test, p = 0.010). This is a component-level diagnostic rather than an end-to-end classifier-to-scorer result. The feedback generator ships with a structured evaluation protocol; its human-rater study is left to future work. The system is released under Apache 2.0 at https://github.com/wwrwbs/AI_AWE.

</details>


### [110] [PTXBench: Benchmark and Adapt LLMs for GPU Kernel Optimization with Architecture-specific PTX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17379)
*Genghan Zhang,Yixin Dong,Chengze Fan,Zhichen Zeng,Yueming Yuan,Shaowei Zhu,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PTXBench基准，用于评估和训练LLM使用架构特定PTX指令进行GPU内核优化，涵盖GEMM和注意力工作负载，并验证了监督微调在提升LLM的PTX能力方面的效果与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在生成CUDA内核方面已有一定能力，但针对具体GPU架构的PTX级优化能力未得到充分评估。随着GPU架构快速演进，需要一种可审计的基准来衡量和提升LLM利用架构特定指令进行内核优化的能力，以弥合模型能力与前沿库性能之间的差距。

Method: 构建PTXBench基准，从三个维度评估模型：(1)功能正确性，(2)选定的目标指令是否在运行时实际执行，(3)相对于前沿GPU库在GEMM和注意力负载上的加速比。测试平台涵盖H100和B200 GPU。并在此基础上对Qwen3.6-27B进行监督微调，采用修复条件训练策略。

Result: 评估显示，LLM的架构特定PTX能力分布不均：复杂注意力反向传播任务的成功率显著下降，且目标指令的运行时执行并不保证获得有竞争力的性能。没有模型能在整个基准上稳定匹敌前沿库。监督微调提升部分任务表现，但泛化仍然不均衡，数据覆盖度、平衡性和推理教师质量与数据集规模同样重要。

Conclusion: PTXBench为衡量和提升LLM利用不断演进的GPU架构的能力提供了可审计的测试平台，同时揭示了当前LLM在PTX内核优化方面能力的局限性和改进方向。

Abstract: We introduce PTXBench, a benchmark for evaluating and adapting large language models (LLMs) to use architecture-specific PTX for GPU kernel optimization. PTXBench measures functional correctness, whether selected target instructions execute at runtime, and speedup over frontier libraries across GEMM and attention workloads on H100 and B200 GPUs. Our evaluation shows that architecture-specific PTX capability remains uneven: success rates fall substantially on complex attention backward workloads, and executing the target instructions does not necessarily translate into competitive performance. No evaluated model consistently matches frontier libraries across the suite. We further adapt Qwen3.6-27B using supervised fine-tuning. Repair-conditioned training improves several tasks, but generalization remains uneven; data coverage, balance, and the quality of the reasoning teacher matter in addition to dataset size. PTXBench provides an auditable testbed for measuring and improving LLMs' ability to exploit evolving GPU architectures.

</details>


### [111] [An Investigation of Translationese in the Generations of Multilingual Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17399)
*Maria Valentini,Téa Wright,Julisa Granados,Eliana Colunga,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言大语言模型（MLLMs）生成的文本是否类似于"翻译腔"（translationese），并比较了MLLM生成的翻译腔与直接翻译产生的翻译腔之间的差异。通过分类模型、方差分析和人类标注，在五种语言中评估了MLLM生成文本的翻译腔含量及其关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs能够生成多种语言的文本，但目前尚不清楚这些生成文本是否从英语或其他语言进行内部翻译而产生翻译腔。理解这一点对于评估MLLM生成文本的自然度和质量非常重要。

Method: 作者利用已建立的翻译文本指标，在五种语言中评估了最先进的MLLM生成的文本，并将其与非翻译文本和人类撰写的基线进行比较。研究结合高精度分类模型、对个体语言特征进行方差分析，以及在德语和西班牙语两种语言中收集人类标注，以评估MLLM生成文本的翻译腔含量并识别关键区分特征。

Result: 通过高精度分类模型、个体语言特征的方差分析以及德语和西班牙语两种语言的人类标注，成功评估了MLLM生成文本中的翻译腔内容，并发现了区分MLLM生成文本与典型翻译相关干扰的关键特征。

Conclusion: MLLM生成的多语言文本确实表现出翻译腔特征，但其翻译腔与直接翻译产生的翻译腔有所区别，并且可以通过特定语言特征进行区分。这一发现对理解MLLM的多语言生成机制及其文本的自然度具有重要启示。

Abstract: Text which has been translated from another language tends to carry with it evidence of translation$\unicode{x2014}$hence, it is often referred to as $\textit{translationese}$. Multilingual large language models (MLLMs) generate text in a variety of languages. However, it is still unclear if MLLMs' generations resemble internal translation (from English or, potentially, other languages) and, thus, result in translationese. Here, we ask the following research questions: (1) Does text generated by MLLMs resemble translationese? (2) How does translationese produced by MLLMs differ from translationese produced through direct translation? We leverage established indicators of translated text to evaluate text generated by state-of-the-art MLLMs in five languages, comparing to both non-translated and human-written baselines in order to isolate translationese from other kinds of interference. Through the use of high-accuracy classification models, analyses of variance on individual linguistic features, and the collection of human annotations in a subset of two languages (German and Spanish), we assess the translationese content of MLLM generations and examine the key features that distinguish MLLM-generated text from typical translation-related interference.

</details>


### [112] [From Entity Mentions to Tone: An LLM-Based Pipeline for Media Bias Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17454)
*Klesti Hoxha,Olti Qirici*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分析在线新闻媒体偏见与框架的流水线，将文章进行主题和事件聚类，添加命名实体和情感标注，并通过人物提及、来源级语气和事件级覆盖模式比较新闻来源。在8,358条阿尔巴尼亚语新闻上验证，结果显示与GDELT自动化标注有中等程度的一致性，并提出了两种提示词方案的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在手动验证数据集或专门语言工具有限的场景下，缺乏有效的媒体偏见分析手段。现有自动化工具（如GDELT）的标注质量有限，需要一种能够灵活比较不同新闻来源在人物提及、语气和事件覆盖方面差异的流水线，特别是在低资源语言环境中。

Method: 构建一个新闻分析流水线：(1) 将文章按主题和事件聚类；(2) 添加命名实体和情感标注；(3) 通过人物提及、来源级语气和事件级覆盖模式进行跨来源比较。比较两种LLM提示词方案（简单版与严格情感验证版），并与GDELT的自动化标注进行一致性评估。

Result: 流水线在8,358条阿尔巴尼亚语新闻上运行，与GDELT标注在情感和实体提取上呈现中等一致性，并发现额外的可支持偏见分析的人物-实体对。严格情感验证提示词消除了标签-分数的矛盾但增加了执行时间并降低了覆盖范围，因此选择简单提示词用于后续分析。提供了来源级框架画像、跨来源人物语气差异和事件级把关/覆盖指标的示例分析。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够利用同一新闻语料库考察来源覆盖内容、如何描述公众人物以及覆盖集中区域，在缺乏手动验证数据集或专门语言工具的低资源环境中具有特别价值，为媒体偏见和框架分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a pipeline for analyzing media bias and framing in online news. The pipeline groups articles into topics and events, adds named-entity and sentiment annotations, and compares news sources through people mentions, source-level tone, and event-level coverage patterns. We apply it to 8,358 Albanian news articles collected from GDELT and compare the resulting annotations with GDELT's automated annotations. The results show moderate agreement for sentiment and entity extraction, as well as additional person-entity pairs that can potentially support the bias analysis. We compare two annotation prompts and find that stricter sentiment-validation rules remove label-score inconsistencies but increase execution time and reduce annotation coverage. Based on these results, the simpler prompt is used for the rest of the analysis. We have provided sample analysis on source-level framing pro les, person-level tone differences across sources, and event-level gatekeeping and coverage indicators. These outputs show how the same news collection can be used to examine what sources cover, how they describe public figures, and where coverage is concentrated. The approach is particularly useful in settings where manually verified datasets or specialized language tools are limited.

</details>


### [113] [Effects of Answer Format Variation on Gender Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17516)
*Ksenia Merzlyakova,Sebastian Padó,Franziska Weeber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了答案格式（封闭式、李克特量表式、开放式）对大型语言模型性别偏见测量结果的影响。在BBQ基准和OpinionQA调查数据上评估三个指令微调模型，发现答案格式会实质性改变偏见测量结果，甚至导致排序反转，各格式会引发不同的响应行为（如强制选择、量表分布、拒绝回答），因此应将答案格式视为LLM评估的实质性组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM性别偏见评估通常使用预定义答案格式的问答或调查基准，但调查科学领域早已证明答案格式会对回答产生实质影响，正如LLM对提示措辞敏感一样。然而此前尚无研究探讨答案格式变化如何影响LLM中性别偏见的测量及其与人类响应分布的匹配度，这一空白需要填补。

Method: 在BBQ基准和OpinionQA调查数据上，对三个指令微调模型，在完全相同的条件下跨封闭式、李克特量表式和开放式三种答案格式进行评估，比较偏见测量结果和分布对齐情况。

Result: 答案格式确实会实质性改变测量结果，包括排序反转（reversals in order rankings）。不同格式会引发不同的响应行为，如强制选择行为、量表式分布以及自由文本生成中的拒绝回答。

Conclusion: 答案格式应被视为LLM评估中的实质性组成部分而非无关紧要的细节，研究结果支持采用多格式设计以实现更稳健的模型评估。

Abstract: Gender bias or other social biases in large language models (LLMs) are frequently evaluated with question answering or survey benchmarks where the LLM needs to give a response in a predefined answer format. It is well known in survey science that the answer format has a substantial impact on answers, just as LLMs are sensitive to the prompt wording. However, to our knowledge it has not been studied yet how changes in answer format impact the measurement of gender bias in LLMs and their alignment with human response distributions. We evaluate three instruction-tuned models on the BBQ benchmark and OpinionQA survey data across closed-ended, Likert-scaled and open-ended formats, comparing bias measurement and distributional alignment under otherwise identical conditions. We find that answer format does substantially alter measured outcomes, including reversals in order rankings. These differences arise because each format elicits distinct response behaviours, such as forced-choice selection, scale-based distributions and refusal in free-text generation. Our findings highlight the importance of treating answer format as a substantive component of LLM evaluation and motivate multi-format designs for more robust model assessment.

</details>


### [114] [ArborMem: Navigating Interaction States with Memory Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17534)
*Zongwei Lv,Yuemeng Xu,Yilun Yao,Siyi Ding,Xinyu Tan,Yaoming Li,Guangxiang Zhao,Weihong Lin,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ArborMem，一种将长期对话表示为可导航的交互状态森林的在线记忆框架，每个分支保留局部连贯的轨迹，森林维护多条可随时恢复的轨迹。对于新输入，ArborMem定位相关状态、恢复其分支上下文，并跨分支检索可复用证据。同时引入BranchMemEval诊断基准来隔离分支结构挑战。在LongMemEval、LoCoMo、BEAM 100K和BranchMemEval上分别超过最强基线3.36至10.31个百分点和5.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期对话记忆系统仅将记忆访问视为检索相关历史信息，而没有首先确定当前对话延续的是哪个先前的交互状态。当对话涉及多个任务、人物和计划交织且可能被打断后恢复时，这种局限性尤为突出。需要一种能够表示和恢复分支化交互轨迹的记忆框架。

Method: ArborMem是一个在线记忆框架，将长期对话表示为交互状态的森林（forest）结构。每个分支保留一条局部连贯的轨迹，森林整体维护多条可能被恢复的轨迹。对于每个新输入，ArborMem执行三个步骤：(1) 定位（localize）相关状态；(2) 恢复（restore）该分支的局部上下文；(3) 用跨分支检索到的可复用证据进行增强（augment），以保持交互连续性而不混淆语义相关但结构不同的轨迹。此外，文章构建了BranchMemEval，一个受控的诊断基准，专门用于评估交织和可恢复的交互轨迹。

Result: 在LongMemEval、LoCoMo和BEAM 100K三个既有的长期记忆基准上，ArborMem比最强基线高出3.36至10.31个百分点；在BranchMemEval上高出5.0个百分点。在受限的读取预算下优势更加明显，且完整记忆查询时间保持在半秒以内。

Conclusion: ArborMem通过森林结构的分支化状态表征，有效解决了现有长期对话记忆系统在识别和恢复分支交互轨迹方面的不足。实验结果验证了该方法在多项基准上的优越性，尤其在读取预算受限时优势更显著，同时保持了较低的查询延迟，表明其作为持久对话助手的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly serve as persistent conversational assistants, requiring memory that preserves relevant experience and maintains continuity across interactions. Existing methods improve access to conversational history through long-context processing, selective retrieval, and structured memory organization. However, most systems treat memory access as retrieving relevant past information without first determining which prior interaction state the current turn resumes. This limitation becomes particularly important when conversations interleave multiple tasks, people, and plans that may be interrupted and later revisited. We introduce ArborMem, an online memory framework that represents a long-running conversation as a navigable forest of interaction states. Each branch preserves a locally coherent trajectory, while the forest maintains multiple trajectories that may later be resumed. For each new input, ArborMem localizes the relevant state, restores its branch-local context, and augments it with reusable evidence retrieved across branches, preserving interaction continuity without conflating semantically related but structurally distinct trajectories. Existing long-term memory benchmarks cover diverse memory and reasoning capabilities but do not explicitly isolate branch-structured challenges. We therefore introduce BranchMemEval, a controlled diagnostic benchmark for interleaved and resumable interaction trajectories. Experiments on LongMemEval, LoCoMo, BEAM 100K, and BranchMemEval show that ArborMem outperforms the strongest baselines by 3.36 to 10.31 percentage points on the three established benchmarks and by 5.0 points on BranchMemEval. Its advantage grows under constrained read budgets, while complete memory queries remain below half a second.

</details>


### [115] [CoAL-RAG: A Complexity-Aware Legal Retrieval-Augmented Generation Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17536)
*Jin Su,Zhuofeng Zhao,Huanhuan Wang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoAL-RAG，一种复杂度感知的法律检索增强生成方法，通过"问题本质"和"检索一致性"的多维评估机制实现检索策略的自适应路由，在中文法律基准（SocialLawQA、LawBench）和英文基准（LexGLUE、CaseHold）上均显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律咨询问题具有多层次复杂度，单一检索策略无法兼顾简单问题的效率与复杂问题的可解释性，不能满足高风险场景下答案质量和效率的双重要求。

Method: 构建基于"问题本质"和"检索一致性"的多维评估机制：首先根据问题逻辑结构量化推理需求，然后利用语义检索与关键词检索之间的差异间接反映问题复杂度，据此自适应选择最合适的检索策略并动态过滤上下文信息。

Result: 在中文数据集上BLEU得分提升42.5%，ROUGE-L达到基于知识图谱方法的3.6倍；在英文基准上保持高度竞争力的准确率，在不同法域间展现了良好的跨司法辖区泛化能力。

Conclusion: CoAL-RAG在生成质量、深层逻辑推理和系统效率之间实现了最优平衡，验证了复杂度感知检索策略在不同法律体系下的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Legal consultation questions exhibit multi-level complexity. A single retrieval strategy often leads to over-reasoning for simple questions and poor interpretability for complex ones, making it difficult to meet the requirements for both answer quality and efficiency in high-risk scenarios. To address this issue, this paper proposes CoAL-RAG, a complexity-aware legal retrieval-augmented generation method, which constructs a multi-dimensional evaluation mechanism based on ``question essence'' and ``retrieval consistency'' to enable adaptive routing of retrieval strategies. First, the reasoning demand is quantified according to the logical structure of the question. Then, the discrepancy between semantic retrieval and keyword retrieval is utilized to indirectly reflect problem complexity, thereby selecting the most appropriate retrieval strategy and dynamically filtering contextual information. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms baseline models not only on Chinese legal benchmarks (SocialLawQA, LawBench) but also demonstrates strong cross-jurisdictional generalization on English datasets (LexGLUE, CaseHold). Specifically, on Chinese datasets, the BLEU score improves by 42.5\% and ROUGE-L reaches 3.6 times that of knowledge graph-based methods. On English benchmarks, CoAL-RAG maintains highly competitive accuracy, achieving an optimal balance between generation quality, deep logical reasoning, and system efficiency across different legal systems.

</details>


### [116] [Auditing Exposure to Harmful Content on TikTok using Multimodal Language Models: A Cross-National, Age-Stratified Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17583)
*Hamidreza Saffari,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的可扩展跨语言青少年视频平台审计方法。研究者在法国、意大利和瑞典对TikTok进行审计，使用代表四种年龄人设（13、16、19、40岁）的"袜偶"账户，被动刷信息流和主动搜索危害关键词两种方式共收集36,971个视频。通过验证四个多模态LLM模型，发现Gemini 2.5 Flash（8帧+文本）在300视频参考集上表现最佳（聚合kappa=0.42），且成本比原生视频上传减半。关键词搜索返回35-56%的有害内容，是滚动基线的1.5-7.5倍（12个国别-年龄组合中的10个）；但在意大利，被动滚动下19岁用户的有害率最高达48.6%。研究还发现平台安全过滤器（1.1%拒绝率）严重低估了最明显的有害内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线视频平台可能向年轻用户展示有害内容，但由于视频标注成本高昂且不同语言的审核判断存在差异，独立审计仍然困难。需要一种可扩展、跨语言且经济可行的审计方法来评估平台对青少年用户的保护程度。

Method: 在法国、意大利、瑞典三国使用袜偶账户（代表13/16/19/40岁四种年龄人设）对TikTok进行审计，收集被动信息流滚动和主动危害关键词搜索两类会话共36,971个视频。验证了四个多模态LLM模型，以母语标注者在300视频参考集上的标签为基准，选出最优模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash，8采样帧+文本，聚合kappa=0.42），并将其应用于10%样本，总API花费约50美元。

Result: 关键词搜索在10/12的国别-年龄组合中出现有害内容率1.5-7.5倍的显著提升（35-56%），但该尖峰是暂时性的，且抹平了法国和瑞典各年龄人设之间的差异。被动滚动下意大利在所有年龄的有害率最高，意大利19岁达48.6%。平台安全过滤器仅有1.1%拒绝率，严重低报了最明显的有害内容。

Conclusion: 基于MLLM的审计方法为跨国青少年安全审计提供了可扩展的路径，能够在低成本下实现规模化标注。同时研究揭示平台现有的安全过滤机制严重不足，关键词搜索暴露的危害尖峰虽然短暂但也反映平台未能及时拦截主动搜索有害内容的年轻用户。

Abstract: Online video platforms can expose young users to harmful content, but independent audits remain difficult because video annotation is costly and moderation judgments vary across languages. We audit TikTok in France, Italy, and Sweden with sockpuppet accounts representing four age personas (13, 16, 19, 40), collecting 36,971 videos from passive For-You-page scrolling and active sessions that scroll, search for harm keywords, and scroll again. To scale annotation, we validate four multimodal LLMs against native-speaker labels on a 300-video reference set. Gemini 2.5 Flash with eight sampled frames plus text performs best (aggregate kappa = 0.42), at half the per-call cost of native-video upload, and we apply it to a 10% sample for approximately \$50 in total API spend across both modalities. Keyword search returns 35-56% harmful content, a 1.5-7.5x increase over the scrolling baseline in ten of twelve country-age combinations; the spike is temporary and flattens the age differences observed in France and Sweden. Under passive scrolling, Italy has the highest harm rate at every age, with Italian age-19 reaching 48.6%. Overall, MLLM-based auditing offers a scalable approach for cross-national youth-safety audits, while provider safety filters (1.1% refusal rate) under-count the most explicit harms.

</details>


### [117] [Write, Execute, Refine: From Skill Followers to Skill Optimizers via Reinforcement Learning from Execution Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17587)
*Kang Peng,Zhiwei Zhang,Yichen Zhang,Zezhong Wang,Yiming Du,Geng Tu,Baojun Wang,Bin Liang,Ruifeng Xu,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出WER（Write, Execute, and Refine）多阶段框架，通过训练一个专门的技能优化器（Skill Optimizer）来生成和改进工具使用智能体的技能。该框架在BFCL v4多轮和tau2-bench基准上分别比无技能基线提升7.80和3.85个Pass@1百分点，同等工作流下比未训练优化器的相同骨干模型高出9.35和10.29个百分点，且仅有4B参数的训练优化器在BFCL v4上达到76.63%的成绩，优于所有评估的通用现成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的研究表明，专家撰写的自然语言技能可以提升工具使用智能体的性能，但智能体自行生成的技能反而比不使用任何技能差8-11点。这种差距说明"遵循程序性指导"和"从执行证据中改进指导"是两种不同的能力。现有的推理时循环可以修复技能但无法改进编写下一个技能的模型。因此需要一种方法，将中间技能的执行经验组织成训练状态，用于训练一个专门的优化器。

Method: WER框架包含三个主要阶段：（1）Write阶段：训练Skill Optimizer（在冻结的执行器外部）提出技能；（2）Execute阶段：冻结的智能体反复执行每个技能，程序化验证器对执行结果打分；（3）Refine阶段：利用分数提供相对信用信号，选择混合结果的记录，将匹配的成功与失败轨迹构造成下一阶段的细化训练状态，使优化器能从其早期输出的后果中学习。

Result: 在BFCL v4多轮和tau2-bench上，WER相比无技能基线分别提升7.80和3.85个Pass@1百分点；在相同的细化工作流程下，比未训练优化器的相同骨干模型高出9.35和10.29个百分点。训练后的4B优化器在BFCL v4上达到76.63%的成绩，在平均性能上优于所有作为技能优化器评估的现成通用模型。

Conclusion: 训练专门的技能优化器可以显著提升工具使用智能体的性能。通过对技能执行结果进行程序化验证和混合结果轨迹筛选，优化器能够从自身输出的后果中学习并持续改进技能质量，从而弥补智能体自主编写技能与专家编写技能之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Expert-written natural language skills can improve tool-using agents, yet agent-authored skills perform 8-11 points worse than using no skill. This gap suggests that following procedural guidance and improving it from execution evidence are distinct capabilities. Inference time loops can repair skills but do not improve the model that writes the next one. We study how to organize execution experience from intermediate skills into training states for an optimizer. We introduce WER (Write, Execute, and Refine), a multi-phase framework that trains a Skill Optimizer outside a frozen executor. The optimizer proposes skills, a frozen agent executes each repeatedly, and a programmatic verifier scores the outcomes. The scores provide relative credit and select mixed-outcome records. Matched successful and failed trajectories from these records form the next phase's refinement states, so the optimizer learns from the consequences of its earlier outputs. On BFCL v4 multi-turn and tau2-bench, WER improves average Pass@1 over the no-skill baseline by 7.80 and 3.85 points, respectively. Under an identical refinement workflow, it outperforms the same backbone without optimizer training by 9.35 and 10.29 points. The trained 4B optimizer reaches 76.63 percent on BFCL v4, outperforming all evaluated off-the-shelf general-purpose models used as skill optimizers on average.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-turn Conversational AI from Text to Multimodal Interaction: Data, Models, Evaluation, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17605)
*Syeda Faiza Ahmed,Zien Sheikh Ali,Hunzalah Hassan Bhatti,Firoj Alam,Shammur Absar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了多轮对话式AI的研究进展，涵盖纯文本对话、AudioLLMs和语音原生系统、多模态及全模态系统、工具增强型智能体，并指出多模态感知能力的发展快于跨会话连贯交互能力，当前系统仍面临持久记忆、跨轮次接地、全双工交互、鲁棒评估和跨文化对齐等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI正从孤立的文本提示走向持续的、多模态交互。真实对话中用户会澄清目标、修改请求、打断回应、切换话题、引入新证据，这要求系统跨轮次保持上下文，使多轮对话成为一个独特挑战，需要系统性文献整理和分析。

Method: 该研究对多轮对话式AI进行综述，按数据集与基准、建模范式、训练策略、评估设置和跨领域挑战等维度组织文献，覆盖纯文本对话、AudioLLMs与语音原生系统、多模态/全模态系统以及工具增强型智能体四大方向。

Result: 分析显示，多模态支持能力的进展速度快于跨会话连贯交互能力的提升。尽管系统在多模态感知、说话和行动方面能力更强，但持久记忆、跨轮次接地、全双工交互、鲁棒评估和文化对齐等方面仍存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 作者提出研究议程，指向能够跨轮次、跨模态、跨文化地进行记忆、修订、接地、说话、倾听、行动和自适应的系统，并为该领域未来研究提供了方向性指南。

Abstract: Conversational AI is moving beyond isolated text prompts toward sustained, multimodal interaction. In real conversations, users clarify goals, revise requests, interrupt responses, switch topics, and introduce new evidence while expecting systems to preserve context across turns. This makes multi-turn dialogue a distinct challenge requiring systems to maintain and update memory, ground responses across modalities, tools, and external knowledge, and adapt across languages and cultures. This study reviews multi-turn conversational AI across text-only dialogue, AudioLLMs and speech-native systems, multimodal and omni-modal systems, and tool-augmented agents. We organize the literature around datasets and benchmarks, modeling paradigms, training strategies, evaluation setups, and cross-cutting challenges. Our analysis shows that support for multiple modalities has advanced faster than the ability to sustain coherent interaction across a session. Despite stronger capabilities to perceive, speak, and act across modalities, current systems still struggle with persistent memory, cross-turn grounding, full-duplex interaction, robust evaluation, and cultural alignment. We conclude with a research agenda for systems that can remember, revise, ground, speak, listen, act, and adapt across turns, modalities, and cultures. (https://github.com/faiza-sfa/multiturn-conversational-ai-survey)

</details>


### [119] [Thinking in a Low-Resource Language: What SFT Builds, What RL Fixes, What Accuracy Cannot See](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17744)
*Ayoub Kirouane,Christos Petrocheilos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 作者对三个前沿MoE模型（Ali、OpenAI、NVIDIA，每个约3.6-4B活跃参数）进行低资源语言（希腊语）的微调实验。首先发现准确性基准在该规模下全是噪声（仅改随机种子就变化7.7分），但真正的重要变化发生在准确率看不到的地方：基础模型从不使用希腊语推理（1000条轨迹中0条），即使问题是希腊语；SFT后所有模型在98%的条目上使用与问题相同的语言推理。作者提出六个可测量的行为维度（全部克制与输出长度相关的指标），并用对照实验发现自身工具的六个失败。SFT无法修复自己的缺陷（格式跳过率25%、答案泄漏到推理通道、英文推理指令遵守率不足50%），而可验证奖励的RL预注册实验修复了前两个并改善了第三个，同时希腊语推理习惯在纯准确率梯度下不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言下的推理可读性、可审计性和可修正性难以被准确性基准捕捉，现有评估方法在该规模下被噪声淹没，需要新的行为层面测量维度和控制实验来准确衡量微调的真实影响。

Method: 对三个MoE模型（3.6-4.0B活跃参数）进行监督微调，使其在低资源语言（希腊语）中推理；提出六个行为维度评估推理语言、可读性等，每个维度设置与输出长度相关指标的拒绝门控；设计包含对照组（随机奖励等）的预注册RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）实验来修复SFT遗留的缺陷。

Result: 准确性基准纯属噪声（种子变化7.7分>所有数据与配方效应）；基础模型0/1000条轨迹用希腊语推理，SFT后98%条目用问题语言推理且judged语法性提升、通用能力几乎无损失；RLVR将格式跳过率从24%降到2.5%、泄漏从3.5%降到0.0%（对照平坦随机奖励组验证）、英文指令遵守率提升9.1pp；希腊语推理习惯在准确率梯度下保持。

Conclusion: 低资源语言推理的可读性等行为特征是SFT可显著改善且准确率基准无法捕捉的维度；SFT有自身无法修复的结构性缺陷，须靠预注册的RLVR修复；整套工具（仪器、对照、预注册）可迁移到任何低资源语言。

Abstract: Take three frontier mixture-of-experts models (Alibaba, OpenAI, NVIDIA; 3.6-4.0B active parameters each) and fine-tune them to reason in a low-resource language. On accuracy benchmarks almost nothing happens, and the benchmark itself is noise at this scale: changing only the random seed moves the score by 7.7 points, more than every data and recipe effect we measured. That null is our first result. The real changes live where accuracy cannot see. Base models never think in Greek: 0 of 1,000 reasoning traces, even when the question is Greek, so the model answers correctly while reasoning in a form its user cannot read, audit, or correct. After supervised fine-tuning (SFT), every released checkpoint reasons in the language of the question on ~98% of items, one family at 3x fewer tokens, with judged grammaticality improving on all four models and general ability within a few points of each base: nothing was forgotten, and fluency was gained. We propose six behavioural dimensions that make such changes measurable, each gated to reject any metric that correlates with output length, and we report how our own instruments lied: six failures, each caught by a control. What SFT cannot do is fix its own defects: a quarter of answers skip the requested format, answers leak into the reasoning channel, and an explicit "think in English" is obeyed under half the time. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, pre-registered before training, fixes the first two outright (fallback 24% to 2.5%, leak 3.5% to 0.0%, both against a flat random-reward control) and moves the third (+9.1pp), while the Greek reasoning habit survives an accuracy-only gradient untouched. We release five checkpoints. The instruments, the controls and the pre-registration travel to any low-resource language; Greek is the case that let us measure them.

</details>


### [120] [Preference Is Not Intervention: The Structure and Stability Boundaries of Reader-Specific Evidence Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17781)
*Shi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了RAG系统中读者模型身份的条件依赖效应，发现读者间存在稳定的序数偏好结构（可复现），但序数相似性无法预测干预（帮助/伤害）迁移，即"偏好≠干预"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML系统越来越多地基于下游模型身份（即"哪个读者模型"）来调整决策，但这种做法只有在模型间差异构成可复用的稳定结构时才有效，而非仅限于输入局部交互。作者旨在检验RAG场景中读者模型的特异性效用是否具有可测量、可迁移的稳定结构。

Method: 在RAG框架下，固定查询、证据、任务、评分与干预方式，进行三种测量分离：证据活性（activity）、序数偏好（ordinal preference）和条件符号方向（conditional signed direction）。通过九个读者的两两干预实验、拆分半信度检验、置换零假设、以及四个独立设置（LOO干预、PRISM、RAMDocs、RAGuard）来验证读者结构的稳定性与可迁移性。

Result: 九个读者在33%共同影响的单元上存在符号分歧；读者×查询交互解释29.8%的效用方差（置换零假设仅8.4%）；自选证据使F1提升+0.031。序数读者几何在四个独立设置中稳定（split-half ρ=0.60-0.83）；符号几何受任务限制，开放式QA中弱（0.14, 0.35），二值事实核查中强（0.75）。但稳定序数相似性无法预测跨读者干预迁移（预言距离ρ=-0.27，遗憾可靠性-0.28）。

Conclusion: 读者特异性效用真实存在，但序数偏好不等于干预效果：稳定的排序相似性并不许可帮助/伤害决策的跨读者迁移。

Abstract: ML systems increasingly condition decisions on downstream model identity, but this is useful only if model-specific differences form reusable structure rather than input-local interactions. We test this in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), where evidence utility can be measured under controlled interventions. Holding query, evidence, task, scoring, and intervention fixed, nine readers disagree on effect sign in 33\% of jointly affected cells; reader$\times$query interaction explains 29.8\% of utility variance versus an 8.4\% permutation null; and self-selected evidence improves F1 by $+0.031$ ($t=3.39$). We then ask the sharper question: \emph{which components of this heterogeneity are stable reader properties across queries?} Separating three measurable objects---evidence \emph{activity}, \emph{ordinal preference}, and \emph{conditional signed direction}---we find ordinal reader geometry stable across four independent settings (split-half $ρ=0.60$--$0.83$): leave-one-out interventions, PRISM preferences, RAMDocs, and RAGuard. Signed geometry is task-bounded: weak in open-ended QA (0.14, 0.35), especially for misleading and irrelevant evidence, but strong in binary fact-checking (0.75) with no significant ordinal gap, though still below its sparsity-matched ceiling. Sparsity, decoding noise, and metric artifacts do not explain the main ordinal--signed gap. Finally, stable ordinal similarity fails to predict cross-reader intervention transfer (oracle-distance $ρ=-0.27$; regret reliability $-0.28$). Reader-specific utility exists, but preference is not intervention: stable ranking similarity does not license transfer of help/harm decisions.

</details>


### [121] [TraceSQL: Traceable Answerability Estimation for Reference-Free Text-to-SQL Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17795)
*Neelesh Kumar Shukla,Debasmita Panda,Srutanik Bhaduri,Aditya Banerjee,Viji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TraceSQL是一个轻量级、可追溯的Text-to-SQL验证模型，基于67个显式诊断特征（涵盖问题歧义、需求、问题-模式-SQL一致性、SQL结构及意图对齐），在BIRD开发集上达到66.47% F1和64.48% ROC-AUC，超越GradeSQL-7B ORM基线，同时保留特征级证据支持预测可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-SQL系统在实际部署中面临验证难题：真实场景下推理时没有ground-truth SQL或参考执行结果可用于监督。现有方法（LLM作为裁判或专智能体）的决策难以追溯，而Outcome Reward Models虽然通过学习执行标签来打分，但对每个验证背后的信号可见性有限。因此需要一种既准确又可解释、可追溯的SQL验证方案。

Method: TraceSQL构建于显式诊断特征之上，融合67个特征，分为五个维度：问题歧义、问题需求、问题-模式-SQL一致性、SQL结构、意图对齐。这些特征不仅用于预测，还可用于追溯决策到具体诊断证据，并通过特征归因分析模型依赖的信号。

Result: 在BIRD开发集上，TraceSQL达到66.47% F1和64.48% ROC-AUC，相比GradeSQL-7B ORM基线（61.87% F1和58.26% ROC-AUC）有显著提升。特征归因显示模型同时依赖语义grounding和确定性SQL结构信号。

Conclusion: 实验证明SQL验证可以通过轻量级学习模型实现，同时保留特征级证据用于检查和诊断预测，兼顾了验证性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems are commonly evaluated using ground-truth SQL queries or reference execution results, but such supervision is unavailable at inference time in real-world deployments. This creates a critical verification problem: given only a user question, database context, and generated SQL, can a system estimate whether the generated query is likely to correctly answer the question? Recent approaches use LLMs as judge or specialized agents to inspect generated SQL, but their decisions can be difficult to trace. Outcome Reward Models (ORMs) address this by learning from execution-labeled candidate SQLs and assigning correctness scores to unseen queries, yet they still provide limited visibility into the signals behind each verification. To address this limitation, we propose TraceSQL, a lightweight and traceable verification model built on explicit diagnostic features. TraceSQL combines 67 features capturing question ambiguity, question requirements, question-schema-SQL consistency, SQL structure, and intent alignment. These signals remain available for examining which factors influence each prediction and for tracing decisions back to diagnostic evidence. On BIRD development databases, TraceSQL achieves 66.47% F1 and 64.48% ROC-AUC, compared with 61.87% F1 and 58.26% ROC-AUC for the GradeSQL-7B ORM baseline on the same generated-SQL evaluation. Feature attribution further shows that the model relies on both semantic grounding and deterministic SQL-structure signals. These results show that SQL verification can be performed with a lightweight learned model while retaining feature-level evidence for inspecting and diagnosing its predictions.

</details>


### [122] [Whether LLMs Can Navigate Beliefs and Facts Depends on How You Phrase It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17809)
*Quang Minh Nguyen,Luis Frentzen Salim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理用户信念与事实交织时的能力，发现LLMs在回应基于错误信息的用户信念时表现出系统性弱点，其强弱方向和程度取决于表达信念所使用的动词。研究表明该现象源于任务混淆——模型默认去事实核查底层主张，而忽略用户的信念陈述。通过简单的指令可以逆转这一失败，但解码时抑制注意力只能部分恢复准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类日常交流中经常通过"我认为""我猜"等信念表达来传达观点，这些表达往往与事实和知识交织。随着LLMs越来越多地部署在面向用户的场景中，模型需要同时处理用户信念和事实信念的能力至关重要。此前研究已发现LLMs在确认基于错误信息的用户信念方面存在系统性弱点，但该现象背后的机制和影响因素尚不清楚。

Method: 扩展评估到10个LLM和18种信念表达动词；通过对比事实与虚假信息下的准确性差距来量化弱点方向和大小；使用思维链分析验证任务混淆假设；进行注意力机制分析揭示模型对未确认的虚假信念的关注模式；测试单条指令和推理时注意力抑制等干预方法。

Result: 准确性差距随动词不同从+50%（"I vaguely remember"）到-14%（"I seriously doubt"）不等；任务混淆被确认为根因——模型默认事实核查底层主张，覆盖用户信念；明确事实核查的思维链在虚假信息上准确性更低；单条指令即可在不同动词族中逆转失败；注意力分析显示模型对未能确认的虚假信念注意力更高，但解码时抑制该注意力仅能部分恢复准确性且仅在部分模型中有效。

Conclusion: 本研究澄清了先前结果，表明事实核查这一通常理想的行为会干扰LLMs的信念追踪。模型对用户信念表达的处理能力因动词而异，本质上是事实核查与信念追踪之间的任务混淆。研究呼吁未来在干预方法上继续探索，以更好地平衡这两个能力。

Abstract: Humans naturally form and express beliefs in daily communication, e.g., "I think the answer is 3" or "I suppose that's right." Such beliefs inevitably intertwine with fact and knowledge, making the ability to handle them in tandem desirable for large language models (LLMs), as they are increasingly deployed in user-facing settings. Prior work showed that even capable LLMs exhibit a systemic weakness in acknowledging user beliefs grounded in incorrect information. We extend this evaluation to 10 LLMs across 18 epistemic expressions and find that the size and direction of the weakness depend on the verb used to express the belief, with the accuracy gap between factual and false information ranging from +50% on "I vaguely remember" to -14% on "I seriously doubt". We further show that the phenomenon stems from task confusion: models default to fact-checking the underlying claim, overriding the user's stated belief; chains of thought that explicitly fact-check show lower accuracy on false information than those that do not; and a single instruction can reverse the failure across verb families. Mechanistically, models attend more to false beliefs they fail to confirm, but suppressing this attention at decoding time recovers accuracy only partially and only in some models, calling for future work on intervention methods. Our findings clarify prior results and show how fact-checking, a generally desirable behavior, can interfere with belief tracking in LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/ngqm/belief-fact-phrasing.

</details>


### [123] [Interpretable Humans, Alien LLMs: Expert Analysis of Latent Structures in Assessment Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17810)
*Alona Strugatski,Licol Zeinfeld,Jason Cooper,Shelley Rap,Gil Schwarts,Giora Alexandron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过探索性因子分析（EFA）和专家盲评，发现大语言模型（LLMs）的潜在因子结构与人类学习者的认知构造存在本质差异：人类因子可被专家赋予教学意义，而LLM因子在定量推理中完全无法被解释，在化学中仅解释了一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估依赖人类设计的测试，隐含假设AI与人类使用相似的认知构造。作者质疑这一假设，希望验证LLM性能背后的潜在因子是否具有与人类认知构造相同的实质性和可解释性含义。

Method: 收集人类和六个LLM在定量推理与化学评估中的作答数据，分别进行探索性因子分析（EFA），随后由学科专家（SMEs）对产生的因子图进行盲评，以赋予因子教学意义。

Result: 专家能够成功解释大多数人类衍生的因子；但无法解释定量推理中任何LLM衍生的因子，在化学中仅能解释一半的LLM因子。

Conclusion: 结合数据驱动的EFA与专家盲评框架表明，LLM通常运行在统计上不透明、与人类推理不同的机制上，这对当前LLM评估方式的有效性提出了根本性质疑。

Abstract: The evaluation of large language models (LLMs) relies heavily on human-designed assessments, implicitly assuming that AI and humans employ similar underlying cognitive constructs. Challenging this assumption, we investigate whether the latent factors governing LLM performance carry the same substantive, human-interpretable meaning as the cognitive constructs governing human learners. Using responses from humans and six LLMs across quantitative reasoning and chemistry assessments, we conducted Exploratory Factor Analysis (EFA) separately for both groups. Subject-Matter Experts (SMEs) then blindly evaluated the resulting factor graphs to ascribe pedagogical meaning to the emerged constructs. SMEs successfully interpreted most of the human-derived factors. Conversely, they could not ascribe meaning to any LLM-derived factors in quantitative reasoning and interpreted only half of the LLM factors in chemistry. By combining data-driven EFA with blind expert interpretation, this framework shows that LLMs frequently operate on statistically opaque mechanisms distinct from human reasoning.

</details>


### [124] [From Global Benchmarks to Local Evaluations: Benchmarking LLMs for the German Public Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17827)
*Camilla Dalerci,Thilo Michael,Robin Schaefer,Daniel Weinland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MÖVE框架，从能源消耗、供应商透明度和德国政党立场知识三个维度对德国公共部门适用的LLM进行整体评估，发现各模型在不同维度上存在显著权衡，没有单一模型全面胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共机构在选择适合其特定情境的LLM时面临持续挑战，而现有基准主要反映英语和美国中心化环境，且通常仅评估任务性能，无法满足公共部门对治理维度（如能源消耗、透明度）的评估需求。

Method: 提出MÖVE（德国公共部门整体评估框架），从三个较少被研究的治理维度进行评估：能源消耗估算、供应商信息透明度、以及对德国政党立场的知识水平。

Result: 研究发现各模型间能源消耗估算差异超过60倍且不能仅由模型规模解释；信息披露在不同供应商间存在系统性差异；欧洲模型并未展现出对德国政党立场的更强知识。

Conclusion: 公共机构的模型选择不能仅依赖性能排名，评估还应反映部署情境中的治理要求，需要采用融合多维度治理考量在内的整体性评估框架。

Abstract: Public institutions face a persistent challenge in selecting LLMs suited to their specific context. Existing benchmarks, however, are of limited use as they primarily reflect English-language and US-centric settings, and often only evaluate task performance. In this paper, we present first results of MÖVE, a holistic evaluation framework for the German public sector, examining three rarely considered governance dimensions: energy consumption, provider transparency, and knowledge of German-party positions. Our results reveal significant trade-offs, with no single model excelling across all dimensions: estimated energy consumption varies more than 60-fold and is not explained by model size alone, information disclosure varies systematically across providers, and European models do not exhibit stronger knowledge of German party positions. Model selection for public institutions thus cannot rely on performance rankings alone. Instead, evaluations should also reflect the governance requirements of the deployment context.

</details>


### [125] [Encoded but Not Actionable: Auditing the Decode-Generate-Steer Gap in Frozen LLMs for Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17843)
*Man Liang,Xinzhao Cheng,Faizan Wajid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过参数化CAD约束作为受控测试平台，系统探究了六个冻结解码器LLM在几何推理任务中编码的信息是否可解码、可生成、可被激活干预及可被行为操控。研究发现预训练能显著提升局部几何关系的解码能力，但草图级自由度状态在随机初始化表示中就已高度可解码；更关键的是，可解码的信息并不总是可操作的——在生成、激活恢复和均值差操控测试中均出现解码能力与行为控制相背离的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在结构化推理任务上表现强劲，但其内部究竟编码了什么信息、这些编码是否真正影响模型行为，仍不明确。作者希望通过可控的几何推理测试平台，将"局部成对关系"与"草图级约束状态"分离，以区分模型是未能编码几何结构，还是编码了但无法表达或控制这些信息。

Method: 使用参数化CAD约束作为受控测试床，对六个冻结的仅解码器LLM进行四维审计：(1)线性可解码性——通过线性探针检测隐藏状态；(2)强制选择生成——测试模型是否在输出中表达信息；(3)激活层面影响——通过补丁（patching）扰动特定实体位置观察激活恢复效果；(4)行为可操控性——使用均值差（mean-difference）方向引导测试能否定向操控模型输出。同时引入置乱顺序控制来排除位置线索的干扰，并对比随机初始化与预训练模型的差异。

Result: 预训练显著改善了局部几何关系的解码，且在去除位置线索后优势仍然存在。但草图级自由度状态在随机初始化表示中已高度可解码，预训练仅带来适度提升，说明大部分探针性能无需学习权重即可获得。更重要的是，解码信息并非总能转化为行为：生成经常无法表达这些信息；在两个可干预的骨干模型上，激活恢复效应在补丁实体位置消失而可解码性跨层持续存在；均值差操控也无法可靠控制输出。

Conclusion: 可解码性、生成表达、激活层面影响和行为可操控性这四个维度在测试场景中可以相互背离。该审计框架提供了一种受控方法，用于区分模型未能编码几何结构与其能够编码但无法表达或控制编码信息这两种不同的失效模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on structured reasoning tasks, but what they encode and whether it informs model behavior remain unclear. We investigate this question through geometric reasoning, using parametric CAD constraints as a controlled testbed for separating local pairwise relations from sketch-level constraint status. By probing the hidden states of six frozen decoder-only LLMs, we examine four properties: linear decodability, forced-choice generation, activation-level influence, and behavioral steerability. Pretraining substantially improves the decoding of local geometric relations, and this advantage persists after accounting for positional cues with shuffled-order controls. In contrast, sketch-level DOF status is already highly decodable from randomly initialized representations and improves only modestly with pretraining, indicating that much of its probe performance is available without learned weights. Further analyses show that decodable information is not always actionable. Generation often fails to express this information, and on the two intervention-tested backbones, activation-restoration effects at the patched entity position vanish while decodability persists across depth. Mean-difference steering also does not reliably control outputs. These results show that decodability, generation, activation-level influence, and steerability can diverge in the tested setting. The audit provides a controlled way to distinguish failures to encode geometric structure from failures to express or control encoded information.

</details>


### [126] [BayesPrompt: human readable prompts that make sense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17866)
*Franky Kevin Nando Tezoh,Ali Hussaini Umar,Alessandro Laio,Guido Sanguinetti,Riccardo Rende*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM提示词优化问题重新定义为贝叶斯后验推断，通过该框架采样既高效（低困惑度）又人类可读的提示词，解决了现有优化方法产生不可读伪提示词的问题，并在真实数据集上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词优化方法（如最小化困惑度）虽然能高效生成可诱导目标回答的提示，但往往产生不可理解的伪提示词（pseudoprompts），缺乏人类可解释性。作者认为根本原因是提示词优化任务本身是病态问题（ill-posed），需要新的理论框架来同时兼顾效率与可读性。

Method: 将提示词优化重新形式化为贝叶斯后验推断问题：在给定目标答案的条件下推断提示词的后验分布。基于此框架设计了一个高效算法，从后验分布中采样提示词，使得采样出的提示词既在困惑度上高效，又保持人类可读性。

Result: 在真实数据集上与现有最先进方法进行对比，该算法在多项评估指标上均取得了显著改进，证明贝叶斯后验推断框架能够同时兼顾提示词的有效性与可读性。

Conclusion: 通过贝叶斯视角重新审视提示词优化任务，可以将原本病态的优化问题转化为良态的后验推断问题，从而生成既有高诱导能力又具人类可解释性的提示词。这是提示词优化领域一个新的理论方向和实用算法。

Abstract: Reconstructing prompts that can elicit a desired answer or behaviour in an LLM is an open and important research topic. Optimisation methods which aim at minimising the perplexity of a given answer, however, consistently yield so-called pseudoprompts, unintelligible strings of tokens which can lack human interpretability. We argue that this is a consequence of the ill-posedness of the prompt optimisation task. By reframing the task as a Bayesian posterior inference over prompts, we propose an efficient algorithm to sample prompts which are both efficient (in terms of perplexity) and human readable. We compare our approach with state of the art alternatives showing on a real data set a marked improvement over a range of metrics.

</details>


### [127] [BEAR-Bench: A Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning Benchmark for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17895)
*Liubov Chubarova,Alexandra Kuleshova,Daniil Volkov,Kirill Sultanov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BEAR-Bench，一个基于英文和俄语商业/科学文本密集文档的1000个人工标注问题组成的双语基准，用于评估MLLM在专业文档推理上的能力。实验表明即使最强模型（如Gemini 3.1 Pro和Qwen3.5-397B）在该基准上仍有显著提升空间，并利用模型输出比较了幻觉检测方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM基准存在三方面不足：侧重信息抽取而非深层推理、需要外部领域知识、专业文档仅作为众多场景之一；同时语言覆盖上以英语和中文为主，俄语等语言严重缺乏代表性。需要一个自包含、聚焦文本密集专业文档推理的双语基准，并关注模型失败可被可靠检测的问题。

Method: 构建BEAR-Bench基准，包含1000个基于商业和科学文本文档的人工标注问题，覆盖英文和俄语；评估16个商业及开源MLLM（含Gemini 3.1 Pro和Qwen3.5-397B）；进一步利用模型输出对不同幻觉检测方法进行比较分析。

Result: 即使在最强的MLLM上，BEAR-Bench仍显示出明显的能力提升空间（clear headroom）；该基准能有效暴露模型在专业文档推理上的短板；通过比较发现现有幻觉检测方法在识别BEAR-Bench上模型失败时的可靠性存在差异。

Conclusion: BEAR-Bench填补了双语（英俄）专业文档推理基准的空白，既为MLLM在文本密集商业与科学文档上的推理能力提供了更严格的评估，也为幻觉检测方法的可靠性比较提供了实证基础。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made significant strides in visual comprehension, their ability to reason about text-dense, professional documents remains incompletely evaluated. Existing benchmarks emphasize information extraction, require external domain knowledge, or cover professional documents only as one of many settings. They are also largely English- or Chinese-centric, leaving other languages and Russian, in particular, substantially underrepresented. To address these limitations, we introduce BEAR-Bench (Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning), a self-contained, complex English-and-Russian benchmark comprising 1000 human-annotated questions based on text-rich business and scientific documents. We evaluate 16 proprietary and open-weight MLLMs, including Gemini 3.1 Pro and Qwen3.5-397B, on BEAR-Bench and observe clear headroom even for the strongest systems. Finally, we use the resulting model outputs to compare existing hallucination detection methods, evaluating not only how often models fail on BEAR-Bench but also how reliably those failures can be identified.

</details>


### [128] [CABLE: Extending the Reach of Memory Retrieval via Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17911)
*Zheling Tan,Jin Gao,Dequan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CABLE（Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion），一种即插即用的记忆增强方法，通过构建与宿主检索器互补的稀疏有向图链接，解决长期对话记忆中语义检索难以触及的"证据可达性"问题，在多个系统（A-MEM、SimpleMem、Mem0g）和多模型（Qwen3.5-27B、DeepSeek-chat、GPT-4o-mini）上均提升了LLM评判分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM代理的长时记忆系统中，语义相似度检索对主题性回忆有效，但常常遗漏与后续事件语义距离较远的前期经验、计划或动机等关键证据。现有记忆图虽然提供了跨记忆结构，但其链接主要依赖语义重叠，往往与宿主检索器已能找回的内容重复，无法真正扩展检索范围。

Method: CABLE为每条新记忆生成基于前因（antecedent）的查询，检索历史记忆后剔除位于宿主检索器直接语义邻域内的候选，对剩余候选进行验证后，将接受的互补关联加入稀疏有向图。检索时，沿这些链接扩展宿主系统的检索种子，以暴露隐性的支持性证据。

Result: 在LoCoMo和MA-LongMemEval基准上，CABLE集成到A-MEM、SimpleMem和Mem0g后，在所有评估的系统级设置中均取得更高的平均LLM评判分数。最大的提升出现在证据分散于多个记忆或会话的场景，包括开放域、多会话和偏好导向的问题。

Conclusion: 研究表明，记忆体系结构应优先构建稀疏的、与推理相关的、且与宿主检索器互补而非重复的关联链接，这比单纯依赖语义重叠的密集图链接更有效地扩展了长期对话记忆的证据可达性。

Abstract: As LLM agents operate across structured workflows and sessions, preserving long-term history does not ensure that later contexts can recover relevant evidence through a bounded memory interface. We study this evidence-reachability problem in long-term conversational memory, where retrieval still relies heavily on semantic similarity. This works well for topical recall, but it often misses earlier experiences, plans, or motivations that are semantically distant from the later events they help explain. Existing memory graphs provide cross-memory structure, yet links driven mainly by semantic overlap can duplicate what the host retriever already recovers. We argue that link construction should instead prioritize a sparse set of retriever-complementary associations. We present CABLE (Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion), a plug-in augmentation that constructs links designed to extend the host retriever's direct semantic reach. For each new memory, CABLE generates antecedent-oriented queries, retrieves prior memories, subtracts candidates in the direct semantic neighborhood, and verifies the remainder before adding the accepted complementary associations into a sparse directed graph. At retrieval time, CABLE expands the host system's retrieved seeds along these links to surface implicit supporting evidence. We evaluate CABLE with A-MEM on LoCoMo and MA-LongMemEval, and further integrate it into SimpleMem and Mem0g on LoCoMo, using Qwen3.5-27B, DeepSeek-chat, and GPT-4o-mini. CABLE yields higher mean LLM-judge scores in every evaluated system-level setting, with the largest gains in categories where useful evidence is distributed across memories or sessions, including open-domain, multi-session, and preference-oriented questions. These results support prioritizing sparse, reasoning-relevant associations that complement rather than duplicate the host retriever.

</details>


### [129] [SpeechSense: A Paralinguistic-Focused Dataset for Fine-Grained Speech Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17931)
*Shicheng Ma,Wenqian Cui,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeechSense数据集，用于细粒度语音情感分析。该数据集定义了8类基于韵律线索而非词汇内容即可检测的人际立场分类体系，通过高保真语音合成和严格人工验证构建。实验表明，具备声学输入的模型 consistently优于纯文本基线，验证了声学线索在检测微妙说话者态度中的首要地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感分析研究存在两大局限：一是主流方法依赖级联自动语音识别与文本分析的文字中心管线，不可避免丢失韵律和语调等关键声学特征，无法捕捉声学上模糊话语中的态度含义；二是现有基准标签粒度不匹配，偏向基础情感（如快乐、悲伤）而非社交敏感性所需的细腻人际立场（如自信、不耐烦）。

Method: 提出SpeechSense数据集，包含：(1)定义专门8类人际立场分类体系，这些立场主要可通过韵律线索检测，超越纯词汇内容；(2)基于该分类体系构建精选数据集，采用高保真语音合成与严格人工验证流程；(3)通过多模态LLM、纯文本LLM和语音编码器的综合实验进行验证。

Result: 综合实验表明，具备声学访问能力的模型在检测细腻说话者态度方面始终优于纯文本基线，实证验证了声学线索的首要性，突出了SpeechSense的必要性。

Conclusion: SpeechSense填补了语音情感分析中细粒度人际立场数据的空白，证明了声学韵律线索在捕捉微妙态度中不可替代的重要性，为未来语音情感理解研究提供了新的基准资源。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have revolutionized speech processing, yet effective speech understanding requires discerning not just what is said, but how it is said. Speech Sentiment Analysis plays a critical role in decoding these paralinguistic cues for diverse real-world applications such as recruitment and customer service. However, existing Speech Sentiment Analysis research faces two primary limitations. First, dominant approaches rely on text-centric pipelines that cascade Automatic Speech Recognition with text analysis. This process inevitably discards essential acoustic features like prosody and tone, failing to capture attitudinal meanings in acoustically ambiguous utterances. Second, current benchmarks suffer from a mismatch in label granularity, prioritizing basic emotions (e.g., happy, sad) over the nuanced interpersonal stances (e.g., confident, impatient) necessary for social sensitivity. To address these limitations, we propose a novel dataset, SpeechSense, for fine-grained speech sentiment analysis. Specifically, we define a specialized 8-class taxonomy of interpersonal stances detectable primarily through prosodic cues beyond lexical content alone. We then construct a curated dataset based on this taxonomy, built from high-fidelity speech synthesis and rigorous human validation. Comprehensive experiments across multi-modal LLMs, text-only LLMs, and speech encoders demonstrate that models with acoustic access consistently outperform text-only baselines. These results empirically validate the primacy of acoustic cues in detecting subtle speaker attitudes, highlighting the necessity of SpeechSense. Dataset and supplementary materials are available at https://github.com/Sher13cked/SpeechSense.

</details>


### [130] [Grading Needs a Rubric, Not Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17938)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"any-to-bench"基准测试框架：由前沿模型在文档摄入时一次性提取试题与评分标准（rubric），再由低成本模型执行所有重复的评分工作。实验表明，小语言模型在明确评分标准下与昂贵模型评分可靠性相当：答案本身解释了95.6%的分数方差，而评判者身份仅解释0.2%；提高答题者推理努力可使得分提升至满分的0.143，但提高评分者推理努力最多仅改变0.006分。消融实验显示，评分标准是解耦"评分"与"评判者智能"的关键，而其中官方参考答案承担了几乎全部作用；移除参考答案后评分可靠性崩塌（ICC从0.888降至0.628）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估成本高昂，尤其是用前沿模型重复评分大量开放题答案时开销巨大。作者希望验证"小模型+明确rubric"能否替代昂贵模型实现同等可靠的评分，从而大幅降低评估成本。

Method: 设计"any-to-bench"框架：前沿模型仅在摄入阶段读取源文档一次，提取每题及其评分标准；随后由低成本模型完成所有重复评分。实验采用两个模型家族、三种推理努力级别的六种成本高效配置，每个配置回答24道开放题，并对每份答卷评分三次（共3,456个评分）。通过方差分解（ANOVA）、组内相关系数（ICC）和两组消融实验（去掉标准细则/级别但保留官方答案；同时去掉官方答案）来剖析rubric各组分的作用，并引入六个前沿级评判者作为基准对照。

Result: 答案身份解释95.6%的分数方差，评判者身份仅0.2%；提高答题者推理努力使分数变化最高达0.143满分的，提高评分者推理努力最多仅0.006。六种低成本配置与六个前沿评判者评分结果一致，后者作为评审团并无更高可靠性。消融一（去掉标准细则和级别）几乎无影响；消融二（同时去掉官方答案）导致ICC从0.888降至0.628、分数膨胀、评判者推理努力重新变得重要。未发现长度偏好或同模型家族偏好。

Conclusion: 评分标准（rubric）是将评分与评判者智能解耦的根本机制，而在rubric内部，官方参考答案承担了几乎全部解释力。这为使用低成本模型替代昂贵模型进行大规模、可靠的开放题评分提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Small language models can grade open-ended examination answers as reliably as substantially more expensive models when they grade against an explicit rubric. We test this claim as the design principle behind any-to-bench: a frontier model reads source documents once, at ingestion, to extract each question and its rubric; lower-cost models then perform all repeated grading work. We evaluate six cost-efficient model configurations from two model families at three reasoning-effort levels. Each configuration answers 24 open-ended examination questions, and each also grades every answer sheet three times, yielding 3,456 per-question grades. Scores depend overwhelmingly on the answer being graded: answer identity explains 95.6% of score variance, whereas judge identity explains only 0.2%.
