Raising a writer's reasoning effort moves earned scores by as much as 0.143 of full marks, while raising a judge's reasoning effort moves assigned scores by at most 0.006. Six frontier-tier judges, added as a check, reproduce these scores and are no more reliable as a panel. Two ablations then decompose the rubric on the same questions and answers. Removing its criteria and levels while keeping the official answer changes nothing measurable. Removing the official answer as well collapses reliability (ICC 0.888 to 0.628), inflates scores, and makes judge reasoning effort matter again. The rubric is what decouples grading from judge intelligence, and within the rubric the official answer does nearly all the work. We find no evidence of length preference or same-family preference under rubric-anchored grading.

</details>


### [131] [Do Large Language Models Play Six Degrees of Separation? Measuring Topological Compression in Long-Context Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17950)
*Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文绕过注意力权重，直接分析LLM隐状态流形的动态几何，证明深层大语言模型潜空间原生组织为小世界网络；发现深层推理层将海量概念距离压缩为严格受限于"六度分隔"（≤6跳）的可导航路径，并将其应用于RAG场景下的零样本幻觉检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于注意力的可解释性方法因注意力汇聚（attention sinks）等路由伪影而难以捕捉真实的语义邻近性，导致LLM在长上下文中实现多跳推理的内在机制仍不明确。

Method: 绕过注意力权重，将长上下文表征的连续相似度矩阵稀疏化为无权图，直接追踪不同架构下高度不连续语义锚点之间的连通性；通过图论分析揭示潜空间的拓扑结构，并将该框架应用于RAG零样本幻觉检测。

Result: 发现存在尖锐的拓扑相变：浅层句法层保持完全割裂，而深层推理层将大规模概念距离压缩为高度可导航的路径，严格受限于六度分隔（≤6个语义跳数）。在RAGognize数据集上，事实正确的生成与源上下文保持约3跳的结构完整性，而幻觉生成引发严重的拓扑坍缩。

Conclusion: 该工作为变换器如何执行抽象推理提供了数学形式化描述，并提出了一种纯几何的、用于评估事实可靠性的新签名。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multi-hop reasoning capabilities over long contexts, yet the internal mechanisms enabling these distant cognitive leaps remain poorly understood. Traditional attention-based interpretability often fails to capture true semantic proximity due to routing artifacts like attention sinks. In this paper, we bypass attention weights to directly analyze the dynamic geometry of the hidden state manifold, proving that deep LLM latent spaces natively organize into Small-World networks. By sparsifying the continuous similarity matrices of long-context representations into unweighted graphs, we trace the connectivity between highly disjoint semantic anchors across two distinct architectures. Our findings reveal a sharp topological phase transition: while early syntactic layers remain entirely fractured, deep reasoning layers abruptly compress massive conceptual distances into highly navigable pathways strictly bounded by the "Six Degrees of Separation" limit (=< 6 semantic hops). Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical efficacy of this framework by applying it to zero-shot hallucination detection within Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) using the RAGognize dataset. We show that factually grounded generations maintain structural integrity with their source context (approximately 3 hops), whereas hallucinations induce severe topological collapse. Ultimately, this work mathematically formalizes how transformers execute abstract reasoning and provides a novel, strictly geometric signature for evaluating factual reliability.

</details>


### [132] [When Writing Style Drifts: Benchmarking Authorship Verification under Distribution Shifts in Genre, Time and the AI-Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17979)
*Lotta Kiefer,Brisca Balthes,Christoph Leiter,Yamen Ajjour,Elena Schmidt,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AVShift是首个德语作者身份验证基准，包含超过15万文本对，覆盖三种体裁和21年时间跨度，用于在统一框架下系统评估跨体裁、时间及AI时代分布漂移对AV模型鲁棒性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作者身份验证（AV）基准通常孤立地研究体裁、时间等分布漂移因素，且主要集中在英语上，无法全面反映模型在现实条件下的鲁棒性表现。

Method: 构建德语AVShift基准（15万+文本对、三种体裁、21年跨度），在统一框架下实现跨体裁、时间及AI时代的受控评估；对基于特征、基于嵌入和基于LLM的多种方法进行基准测试，并开展风格特征分析。

Result: 微调LLM在跨体裁上泛化最佳且受益于风格多样的训练数据；时间漂移是最强影响因素之一，时间间隔增大导致性能显著下降；AVShift内未发现可测的AI时代分布漂移；部分风格特征跨体裁稳定，但其相对重要性随体裁转换而变化。

Conclusion: AVShift为德语AV鲁棒性研究提供了统一、受控的评估框架，揭示了时间漂移的关键影响及LLM的跨体裁优势，并为未来AV研究提供了数据与代码资源。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) assumes that an author's writing style remains sufficiently stable to distinguish it from that of other writers. In practice, however, this assumption is challenged by distribution shifts caused by changes in genre, time, and AI-assisted writing. Existing AV benchmarks typically study these factors in isolation and focus predominantly on English, limiting our understanding of model robustness under realistic conditions. We introduce AVShift, the first German benchmark for systematically evaluating AV under multiple distribution shifts. AVShift comprises over 150,000 text pairs spanning three genres and 21 years, enabling controlled evaluation of cross-genre, temporal, and AI-era shifts within a unified framework. We benchmark representative feature-based, embedding-based, and LLM-based approaches. Our experiments show that fine-tuned LLMs generalize best across genres and benefit substantially from stylistically diverse training data. We further demonstrate that temporal drift is one of the strongest factors affecting AV, with performance degrading significantly as the time gap between documents increases. In contrast, we find no evidence of a measurable AI-era distribution shift within AVShift. Finally, our feature analysis reveals stylistic features that remain stable across genres, while their relative importance varies depending on the specific genre transition. We release AVShift and our code for future research.

</details>


### [133] [Judge, Retrieve, or Abstain: Uncertainty-Guarded LLM Judging with Provable Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17994)
*Sher Badshah,Ali Emami,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个风险可控的LLM评判框架，通过有限样本Clopper-Pearson区间校准不确定性阈值，在参数模式和检索增强模式之间路由，确保被接受评判结果的错误发现率低于用户指定水平α的同时保持高覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者在主观开放任务中已广泛使用，但在客观任务中无参考答案的评判面临可靠性挑战：参数模式可能产生幻觉或证据不足，而工具增强模式虽能提供额外证据但增加计算成本且缺乏风险控制。现有方法无法对接受评判结果的风险提供正式控制或保证指定水平的可靠性。

Method: 提出一个风险控制框架：在保留集上使用有限样本Clopper-Pearson区间校准不确定性阈值，使被接受评判结果的错误发现率以高概率保持在用户指定水平α以下。当参数模式置信度不足时，实例被路由到检索增强模式，评判者收集网页证据并重新评估，使用第二个校准阈值。有限样本保证无需额外假设即可传递到这种双阈值路由机制。

Result: 在开放域QA基准和不同规模的评判器上，该框架保持了目标错误率，同时实现了比单模式基线显著更高的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 双模式路由的风险控制框架能在无需参考答案的客观评估任务中提供形式化的错误发现率保证（有限样本），同时通过参数模式与检索增强模式的结合实现更好的覆盖-风险权衡，为可靠高效的大规模模型评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Using LLMs as judges has become standard practice for evaluating model outputs at scale. This is particularly common for subjective, open-ended tasks such as assessing helpfulness or alignment, where no single reference answer exists. However, objective tasks introduce a distinct reliability challenge for reference-free LLM judging. In the absence of a reference answer, the judge evaluates factual correctness either through its parametric knowledge or through tool augmentation. Although the former enables efficient evaluation, the judge may hallucinate or lack sufficient evidence for its verdict. Conversely, tool augmentation can provide additional evidence but introduces extra computational cost and requires an appropriate mechanism to determine when and how that evidence should be used reliably. More importantly, neither approach alone provides formal control over the risk of accepted verdicts or guarantees their reliability at a specified level. We propose a risk-controlled framework that calibrates uncertainty thresholds on a held-out set so that the false discovery rate among accepted verdicts remains below a user-specified level~$α$ with high probability, using finite-sample Clopper--Pearson intervals. When the parametric mode is not sufficiently confident, the instance is routed to a retrieval-augmented mode, where the judge gathers web evidence and re-evaluates the instance under a second calibrated threshold. The finite-sample guarantee carries over to this two-threshold routing without additional assumptions. Across open-domain QA benchmarks and judges of varying scales, the framework maintains the target error rate while achieving substantially higher coverage than single-mode baselines.

</details>


### [134] [The IOL-AI Challenge: An Open Challenge towards Advancing Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18011)
*Eduardo Sánchez,Rita Berrada,Dan-Mircea Mirea,Sara Rajaee,Alexander Piperski,Ana Meta Dolinar,Boris Iomdin,Andrey Nikulin,Mariya Shmatova,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IOL-AI挑战赛，这是一个基于2026年国际语言学奥林匹克竞赛（IOL）个人赛未公开题目的开放科学竞赛。731份提交来自46支队伍，在严格算力限制（单张T4、30分钟）下进行。竞赛首次由IOL官方评委会按人类选手的评分标准进行人工评审。结果表明Claude Opus 4.8获得金牌等效评分，但资源受限系统的得分仅处于人类选手底部5%。关键发现包括：能力不取决于模型规模（14B模型可超越两倍大小模型）、收益来自解码和输出处理而非模型容量、自动评分与人工评分排序一致但会压缩分数差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理研究主要集中在数学和代码等已提供规则的领域，而语言谜题要求模型先发现规则再推理，更具挑战性。作者希望通过IOL竞赛形式的基准测试，在更严格的人类评审标准下评估LLM的真正语言推理能力，并探究模型规模与推理能力的关系。

Method: 构建IOL-AI挑战赛，使用IOL 2026个人赛未公开题目，结合自动评估和官方IOL评委会按人类评分标准进行人工评审。比赛设有严格算力预算（单T4 GPU、30分钟），同时额外评估15个无约束的前沿和开源模型。通过对比自动评分与人工评分、分析模型规模与性能关系、控制解码和输出处理变量等方式进行系统分析。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8在人工评审中达到金牌等效分数，而资源受限系统仅达到人类选手底部5%水平。14B参数提交的性能超越两倍大的模型；性能提升主要来自解码和输出处理策略而非模型容量。自动评分与人工评分的排序结果一致，但自动评分压缩了差距：将弱系统高估约13分，同时低估强系统。前沿模型虽可能对部分题目语言有先验知识，但这并未显著帮助其解决语言推理任务。

Conclusion: 语言推理（linguistic reasoning）可作为通用推理能力的有力基准代理。模型能力不由规模决定，推理瓶颈更多在于解码和输出处理策略。自动评估在排名上可靠但在绝对分数上有系统性偏差，需人工评审互补。即便有语言先验知识，前沿模型仍无法轻松解决语言谜题，说明该领域尚未饱和，是有效的基准测试方向。

Abstract: Reasoning in LLMs is overwhelmingly studied in domains that provide a model with rules: mathematics and code. Linguistic puzzles invert this: the solver must first discover the system before reasoning within it. We present the IOL-AI Challenge, an open-science competition run on the unseen problems of the International Linguistics Olympiad (IOL) 2026 Individual Contest, evaluated both automatically and, for the first time, by members of the official IOL Jury under the same rubrics applied to human contestants. The challenge drew 731 submissions from 46 teams under a strict compute budget (one T4, 30 mins). We additionally benchmark 15 unconstrained frontier and open models, with Claude Opus 4.8 earning a jury score equivalent to a gold medal, while both resource-constrained systems we submitted for jury grading scored in the range of the bottom 5% of contestants. Capability was not determined by scale: 14B submissions outperform models twice their size, and gains come from decoding and output-handling rather than model capacity. We also found that automatic metrics rank systems exactly as the jury does, but compress the scale, upscoring weak systems by ~13 points and understating strong ones. Our analysis shows that while frontier models might have prior knowledge about some of the problem languages, it does not significantly help them solve the linguistic reasoning tasks, leaving linguistic reasoning as a strong benchmarking proxy for generalizable reasoning skills.

</details>


### [135] [Chain-of-Experience for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18027)
*Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Yizhong Wang,Cihang Xie,Shen Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Chain-of-Experience (CoE)框架，研究大规模语言模型在测试时通过迭代交互积累经验并进行持续改进的能力，实验表明该方法在数学、编程和知识领域显著优于零样本推理基线，整体提升5.6%且API成本降低19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估忽略了模型在推理时通过交互进行持续学习的能力，而人类恰恰是从经验中不断学习的。现有评估范式仅关注单次零样本推理性能，无法反映模型在测试阶段通过迭代交互提升的潜力。

Method: 提出Chain-of-Experience (CoE)框架，模型在测试时通过迭代交互积累经验轨迹，形成持续改进循环。支持多种反馈机制：模型自反馈、环境信号（如正确性或公开编码测试通过率），并在数学、编码和知识域上使用8个LLM（含GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro、Claude-4.5 Sonnet）进行评估。

Result: 迭代经验利用一致优于无反馈基线，仅自反馈即可获得显著提升，整体改进5.6%，API成本降低19%；融合互补反馈通道（模型信号+正确性信号）可进一步增益；CoE在每token准确率上优于现有测试时策略；LLM基础能力与改进容量正相关；模型在弱或虚假反馈下保持鲁棒，不同反馈贡献不同改进方面，且大部分收益出现在迭代早期。

Conclusion: CoE框架验证了测试时迭代经验学习是提升LLM推理表现的有效途径，且高效经济；未来可进一步探索反馈通道的最优组合与自适应迭代策略。

Abstract: Humans continuously learn from experience, whereas conventional large language model (LLM) evaluations ignore the models' ability to improve through inference-time interaction. In this paper, we study how LLMs learn from iterative experience at test time, a setting we refer to as Chain-of-Experience (CoE), where models accumulate experiential traces through iterative interactions with self or environmental feedback to form a continual improvement loop beyond zero-shot inference. We instantiate CoE with diverse feedback mechanisms, including model self-feedback and environmental signals such as correctness or public coding test pass rates, and evaluate across math, coding, and knowledge domains using 8 LLMs, including GPT-5, Gemini-2.5 Pro, Claude-4.5 Sonnet. Our study shows that leveraging iterative experience consistently outperforms feedback-free baselines, achieving substantial gains with self feedback alone, alongside a 5.6% overall improvement and 19% lower API cost across tasks and models. We further show that combining complementary feedback channels (e.g., model and correctness signals) yields additional gains, and that CoE delivers higher accuracy per token than existing test-time strategies. We observe a positive correlation between LLM base ability and improvement capacity, and show that models remain robust under weak or spurious feedback, with different feedback contributing to distinct improvement aspects and most gains emerging early in the iterations.

</details>


### [136] [Language Has Two Parameters: Narrative-Induced Semantic Plasticity and Phase-Sensitive Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18041)
*Hollis Robbins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语言存在第二个关系参数"相位"（phase），用于解释词义如何组合，并主张现有基于计数的词嵌入和注意力权重只能估计"幅度"（振幅），无法捕捉相位。相位决定共激活意义如何组合，可逆转意义贡献，且与个体和二元关系（dyad）的历史索引相关。论文为这一弱版本主张辩护，并提出六个可检验的预测，最终呼吁构建具有智能体索引、带相位语义状态的语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型依赖词共现统计和注意力权重，本质上只是对语料库中所有作者进行平均的计数，无法捕捉个体或二元层面的语义关系。作者认为需要一个额外的参数——"相位"——来解释反讽、引语、暗示等现象中意义共存而非被抑制的机制。此外，可解释性研究以单语义性（monosemanticity）为目标，实际上与相位现象背道而驰。

Method: 论文采用理论论证与形式分析相结合的方法。首先论证幅度（振幅）参数不足以描述语言组合，提出相位作为第二参数；其次分析群体平均如何抹除历史索引的相位信息；再次分析标准transformer在冻结推理中缺乏相位的显式表征；最后提出六个可检验的预测，涵盖被抑制意义是否保持活跃、遭遇顺序是否改变短语功能、标记信号是否改变共享短语理解、模型给定历史后被改变还是仅被告知等。

Result: 论文的弱版本主张是：解释需要第二个关系参数，该参数是有符号（signed）的、持久的，并索引到个体和二元关系。强版本主张（量子演算比经典有符号模型更能约束这些现象）依赖于遭遇顺序约束，尚未推导出来，因此暂未得到支持。标准transformer缺乏相位的显式表征，且可解释性研究中以单语义性为目标的做法与相位现象相矛盾。

Conclusion: 论文呼吁的语言模型架构应是具有智能体索引、携带相位语义状态的模型。量子概率仅作为该参数的一种记号，不表示大脑中的量子过程。总体结论是：现有基于群体平均的方法在理论上无法捕捉个体与二元层面的语义组合关系，需要引入相位这一第二参数来弥补。

Abstract: Language has two parameters. Count how often words occur together and you estimate amplitude, the strength of association. Word embeddings and attention weights refine that count, which sums every writer in the corpus together. This paper claims a second parameter, phase, which signed weights learned from a corpus do not supply. Phase exists only between meanings: it determines how coactivated meanings combine, and it can reverse what a meaning contributes while that meaning stays fully present. A speaker can set phase in the signal through linguistic form; encounters install phase relations and history distributes them. Population averaging deletes history-indexed phase: agent-deindexed corpora identify the population marginal state and determine no individual or dyadic state, at any scale. The standard transformer has no explicit representation for phase in frozen inference, and the interpretability program measuring progress by monosemanticity is optimizing against it: the coexistence it treats as a defect is the condition of allusion, irony, and quotation. Six predictions test whether a suppressed meaning stays active, whether encounter order changes what a phrase does, whether marking the signal changes how a shared phrase is taken, and whether a model given a history is changed by it or only informed about it. The claim defended is the weak version: interpretation requires a second relational parameter, signed, persistent, and indexed to individuals and dyads. Quantum probability is one notation for the parameter; nothing in the formalism claims quantum processes in the brain. The strong version, that the quantum calculus constrains these phenomena as signed classical models do not, rests on an encounter-order constraint not yet derived. The architecture the theory calls for is a language model with agent-indexed, phase-bearing semantic states.

</details>


### [137] [TokEval: A Tokenizer Evaluation Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18062)
*Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokEval，一个超越传统指标（如fertility和压缩率）的分词器评估框架，引入结构敏感的度量指标（如UTF-8字符边界完整性、数字位值边界对齐），并通过受控预训练实验验证这些内在指标与下游任务表现的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词器的设计选择直接影响模型能力，但当前分词器的选择缺乏系统评估，部分原因在于对哪些分词器属性影响哪些下游性能方面了解有限。

Method: 提出TokEval框架，包含超越标准指标（fertility、压缩率）的评估指标，涵盖信息论指标和结构敏感指标；通过仅变化分词器训练数据混合、预分词策略和训练算法的受控预训练实验，在bits-per-byte和多个基准（语言理解、数学推理、代码生成）上评估模型性能，并计算内在指标与下游性能的相关性。

Result: 不同内在属性对不同模型能力有不同影响：信息论指标可预测语言建模能力（Spearman rho最高0.80），而结构敏感指标（如数字和换行处理）与任务准确率相关。

Conclusion: TokEval有望实现更原则性的分词器评估，在二者一致的情况下用内在测量取代预训练扫描。

Abstract: Language model tokenizers are typically selected with minimal evaluation, despite the fact that their design choices directly impact model capabilities. This can be partly attributed to a limited understanding of which tokenizer properties affect which aspects of downstream performance. We introduce TokEval, a framework of tokenizer evaluation metrics that goes beyond standard measures like fertility and compression rate to capture linguistically and structurally meaningful properties, e.g., UTF-8 character boundary integrity and digit place-value boundary alignment for mathematics. To validate whether these metrics are predictive of downstream model performance, we conduct controlled language model pretraining experiments, varying solely the tokenizers' training data mixture, pretokenization strategy, and training algorithm. We evaluate the resulting models on bits-per-byte (a tokenizer-agnostic version of perplexity) and several benchmarks, spanning linguistic understanding, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our experiments suggest that different intrinsic properties have different impacts on model abilities: information-theoretic metrics predict language modeling abilities (Spearman rho up to 0.80), while structure-sensitive metrics, such as those measuring digit and line-break handling, correlate with task accuracy. We hope TokEval enables more principled tokenizer evaluation, replacing pretraining sweeps with intrinsic measurement wherever the two agree.

</details>


### [138] [Multi-Agent AI System for Radiology Report Structuring and Quality Assurance with Independent Radiologist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18072)
*Iryna Hartsock,Cesar Lam,Christopher Otteni,Aliya Qayyum,Robert Gatenby,Cyrillo Araujo,Ghulam Rasool*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了一种本地部署的多智能体AI系统，用于放射学报告的结构化与质量保证。系统基于正则规则和本地大语言模型，将CT报告按句子级标准化为解剖学分段，并检测发现与印象不匹配、性别-解剖冲突及关键发现沟通缺失等问题。在638份报告中，系统成功结构化全部22,270个句子，标记90份（14.1%）报告存在质量问题；经两位放射科医师评估，69%的报告被一致认为重构正确，无临床重要信息缺失或捏造内容，84%的QA性能被评为优秀或良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告中存在格式不统一、发现与印象不一致、关键信息遗漏等质量问题，影响临床沟通和患者安全。传统方式依赖人工质控，效率低且难以覆盖全部报告。本研究旨在开发一种本地部署的多智能体AI系统，将报告结构化与质量保证整合到单一工作流程中，以提升放射学报告的标准化程度和质量控制效率。

Method: 研究采用回顾性设计，纳入2023至2024年间15位委员会认证放射科医师口述的638份胸部、腹部和盆腔CT放射学报告。开发了一套多智能体AI流水线，使用正则规则与本地大语言模型在句子级将报告结构化为标准化解剖学分段，同时检测发现与印象部分之间的不匹配（及部分内不匹配）、性别-解剖学冲突，以及关键发现未记录沟通等问题。两位委员会认证放射科医师独立评估了其中45份报告的子集。

Result: 系统将全部报告的22,270个发现句子成功结构化为预设解剖学格式，同时保留原始报告内容。系统标记了90份（14.1%）报告存在问题，最常见的是分段不匹配（80份，12.5%）。在放射科医师评估中，两位评审一致认为31份（69%）报告被正确重构，2份（4%）被错误重构，剩余12份（27%）存在分歧。两位评审均认为没有遗漏临床重要信息，也没有引入捏造内容。84%的评估报告整体QA性能被评为"优秀"或"良好"，其余被评为"一般"。

Conclusion: 本地部署的多智能体AI系统成功将放射学报告结构化与质量保证整合于单一工作流程中，在放射科医师评估中展现出良好的性能表现。此类系统有望支持放射学实践中报告的标准化和质量保证工作。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a locally deployed multi-agent AI system for radiology report structuring and quality assurance. Materials and Methods: This retrospective study included 638 radiology reports from CT examinations of the chest, abdomen, and pelvis dictated by 15 board-certified radiologists in 2023 and 2024. A multi-agent AI pipeline was developed to perform report structuring and quality assurance (QA). The system structured the report into standardized anatomical sections at the sentence level using regex rules and local large language models. It also detected mismatches between the Findings and Impression sections, or within sections; gender-anatomy conflicts; and undocumented communication of critical findings. Two board-certified radiologists independently evaluated a 45-report subset. Results: The multi-agent system structured the Findings sections of all reports (22,270 sentences) into a predefined anatomical format while retaining the original report content. The system flagged 90 (14.1%) reports, most commonly for section mismatches (80 reports, 12.5%). In the radiologist evaluation, both reviewers agreed that 31 (69%) were correctly restructured, 2 reports (4%) were incorrectly restructured, and disagreed on the remaining 12 reports (27%). Both reviewers agreed that no clinically important information was omitted and no fabricated content was introduced. Overall QA performance was rated as "excellent" or "good" in 84% of the evaluated reports, with the remaining reports rated as "fair". Conclusion: A locally deployed multi-agent AI system combined radiology report structuring and quality assurance within a single workflow. The system demonstrated favorable performance in radiologist evaluation. Such systems may support standardization of reporting and quality assurance in radiology practice.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [RADmesh: Remesh-Aware Mesh Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17182)
*Nam Anh Dinh,Itai Lang,Oded Stein,Rana Hanocka*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 作者提出了一种结合重网格化（remeshing）的生成式网格变形方法，通过顶点级变形优化与周期性各向同性重网格化相结合，使网格在发生大幅度几何变形时仍能保持三角元质量，并支持从粗到细的分辨率逐步细化。该方法既能应对视觉损失带来的噪声监督，又具备局部可扩展性，可在基座上生成具有丰富细节的新特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的、视觉监督的生成式网格变形方法在发生大幅几何变化时，若不改变三角剖分（triangulation），很容易导致网格元素质量退化。尽管重塑网格（remeshing）能显著改善这一情况，但作为一种离散操作，它与视觉损失这类噪声较大的监督信号相结合极具挑战，因此目前大多数方法并未采用。

Method: 提出一种顶点级（vertex-based）变形优化量，能够支持大幅变形并对视觉损失中的噪声具有鲁棒性；在优化过程中周期性地调用各向同性重网格化器（isotropic remesher），该重网格化器对变形优化状态进行插值并延续传递，从而在从粗到细的分辨率增加过程中实现连续的、几何感知的进展。生成形状的三角剖分与优化后的几何形状相匹配，且具有规整的各向同性单元；该方法还具备局部性，可在基座形状上生长高表达力的新特征而不影响其余部分。

Result: 在多种形状和提示词上进行了验证，涵盖局部与全局变形两类任务，展示了该方法优越的视觉质量与三角形效率。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将重网格化引入生成式变形优化框架，有效解决了大变形态下元素质量退化问题，并在视觉质量与三角形效率方面优于现有方法。

Abstract: We propose a remeshing-enhanced method for generatively deforming shapes with visual losses. It is intuitive that sufficiently drastic deformations of a mesh without changing its triangulation can easily compromise element quality, even if such large geometry changes may be semantically desired. Shape deformation methods could thus benefit from changing the triangulation; however, this is not done by most generative, text-based, visually-supervised mesh deformation methods. Remeshing is a discrete operation, proven to be especially challenging to couple with the notoriously noisy supervision signal provided by visual losses. We propose a vertex-based deformation optimization quantity capable of large deformations and robustness to such noise; we periodically remesh using an isotropic remesher that interpolates and carries forward the deformation optimization state. This enables continuous, geometry-informed progress in coarse-to-fine addition of resolution. The resulting shapes' triangulations fit their optimized geometry and have neat isotropic elements. Further, our method is localizable, able to grow new features on a base shape with expressive detail, leaving the rest unchanged. We showcase the effectiveness of our method on a variety of shapes and prompts, both local and global deformations, and demonstrate its superior visual quality and triangle efficiency. Our project page is at https://threedle.github.io/radmesh.

</details>


### [140] [3D Gaussian Accelerated Ray Tracing: Fast training through particle-based backward propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17298)
*Laurent Vit,Oliver Batchelor,Richard Green*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 3DGART提出了一种面向光线追踪高斯渲染的实用训练框架，通过将反向传播从以像素为中心重组为以基元为中心，利用保守的透视校正屏幕空间边界构建紧凑中间缓冲区和瓦片-基元映射，将梯度计算从高竞争的散点操作转变为结构化的收集式过程，在Mip-NeRF 360上实现约3-3.5倍的原始训练加速，并与光栅化管线运行时间相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅在实时新视角合成中高效，但基于光栅化的屏幕空间近似限制了视图相关排序和反射、折射、阴影等二次光线效果。高斯光线追踪通过显式光线-基元求交解决了这些问题，但训练成本高昂，主要瓶颈并非仅光线遍历本身，而是以像素为中心的反向传播中大量线程并发累积梯度到同一基元参数上，导致严重的原子竞争和线程串行化。

Method: 3DGART将反向传播重新组织为围绕基元而非像素进行。利用保守的透视校正屏幕空间边界，构建紧凑的中间缓冲区和瓦片-基元映射，使得每个线程能够在瓦片内累积一个基元对其覆盖像素的贡献，从而将梯度计算转变为结构化收集式（gather-like）过程，取代原先竞争密集的散点式（scatter）操作。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360数据集上，3DGART相对于逐像素基线实现约3-3.5倍的原始训练加速，相对于3DGRT实现约4倍加速，同时提升了渲染质量。这使得全光线追踪高斯训练变得实用，运行时间可与光栅化管线相媲美。

Conclusion: 3DGART证明了通过基元中心化的反向传播重组可以有效解决光线追踪高斯训练中的原子竞争瓶颈，使得光线追踪高斯渲染在保持反射、折射、阴影等二次光线效应优势的同时，训练效率达到与光栅化管线相当的水平，为高质量实时渲染提供了实用训练框架。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has made Gaussian primitives a highly efficient representation for real-time novel view synthesis, but its rasterisation-based formulation relies on screen-space approximations that limit accurate view-dependent ordering and the integration of secondary ray effects such as reflections, refractions, and shadows. Gaussian ray tracing addresses these limitations by evaluating explicit ray-primitive intersections, yet it remains costly to train. We observe that the main bottleneck is not ray traversal alone, but the pixel-centric backward propagation, where many threads concurrently accumulate gradients into the same primitive parameters, causing severe atomic contention and thread serialisation.
  We present 3DGART, a practical training framework for ray-traced Gaussian rendering. Our key idea is to reorganise backward propagation around primitives rather than pixels. Using conservative perspective-correct screen-space bounds, we build a compact intermediate buffer and a tile-primitive mapping that allows each thread to accumulate the contribution of one primitive over its covered pixels within a tile. This transforms gradient computation from a contention-heavy scatter operation into a structured gather-like process. On Mip-NeRF 360, 3DGART achieves an $\approx 3-3.5\times$ raw training speedup over per-pixel baseline and $\approx4 \times$ over 3DGRT on Mip-NeRF 360 while improving quality. More importantly, 3DGART makes fully ray-traced Gaussian training practical, reaching runtimes competitive with rasterisation-based pipelines while preserving benefits of ray tracing.

</details>


### [141] [Six Ways to Draw Vangers with WebGPU: Real-Time Rendering of Editable Multi-Layer Height Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17390)
*Dzmitry Malyshau*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文比较了六种地形渲染方法（高度场射线步进、体素加速射线步进、切片代理几何、逐采样条栅格化、计算散射、拟合三角形网格）在支持多层手绘地形（含洞穴/悬垂）、可交互破坏和实时渲染约束下的表现，发现贪心三角不规则网络（TIN）在帧时间上最优，但内存占用高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地形LOD研究几乎只针对数字高程模型（单值、平滑、采样自真实地形），而游戏地形常为多层、手绘、非平滑的数据。此前缺乏在统一引擎和数据路径下，对这些非传统地形的渲染方法进行公平比较的研究。作者希望通过严格的公平对比，回答在多层可破坏地形场景中哪种渲染方法最适用的问题。

Method: 在单一引擎（wgpu/WebGPU、规范WGSL）和单一数据路径下，实现六种渲染方法：高度场射线步进、体素加速射线步进、切片代理几何、逐样本条栅格化、计算散射和拟合三角网格。以Vangers（1998）手绘多层地形为测试数据，以CPU光线投射为基准评分。所有方法必须保留地面样本处的两个实心区间、以交互帧率渲染、并支持局部地形破坏而无需重新加载关卡。

Result: 在俯视视角下六种方法视觉结果几乎无差别，但在视平线视角下差异明显：点散射覆盖不足、切片出现条带、过度简化网格可能漏掉墙面。在选定质量设置下，贪心TIN在所有测试设备上平均帧时间最低，但其拟合代价由第二层决定而非地表起伏，且可编辑网格占用319 MiB GPU几何数据和535 MiB CPU三角化数据。

Conclusion: 对于多层可破坏地形，TIN方法在帧率上表现最优，但内存成本较高。研究还排除了将洞穴视为装饰或对不可变地图进行静态预处理的方案。作者开源了引擎、测试框架和一条命令的测量协议，便于复现和进一步比较。

Abstract: Terrain level-of-detail is measured almost exclusively on digital elevation models: single-valued, smooth at the sampling scale, sampled from real topography. Game terrain is often none of these. We compare six rendering methods - height-field ray marching, voxel-accelerated ray marching, sliced proxy geometry, per-sample bar rasterization, compute scattering, and a fitted triangle mesh - implemented in a single engine over a single data path, on the hand-authored multi-layer terrain of Vangers (1998), scored against a CPU ray cast of the same source data. Every method must preserve the two solid intervals available at a ground sample, render at interactive rates, and reflect local terrain destruction without reloading the level. These constraints rule out treating caves as decoration or amortizing a static preprocessing step over an immutable map.
  From the original game's top-down camera the six methods look interchangeable. At eye-level horizons they do not: point scattering loses coverage, slicing bands, and an over-simplified mesh can miss a wall. At the selected quality settings a greedy triangulated irregular network (TIN) has the lowest mean frame time on every device we measured, but the fit cost is set by the second layer rather than by floor relief, and making that mesh editable retains 319 MiB of GPU geometry and 535 MiB of CPU triangulation. All six implementations use the same native wgpu / WebGPU API and canonical WGSL. We release the engine, the harness, and a one-command measurement protocol.

</details>


### [142] [Variational r-Adaptive Cloth Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17833)
*Jiahao Wen,Zhen Chen,Jernej Barbič,Danny M. Kaufman*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了第一个适用于现代布料管线的r-自适应方法，用于模拟带摩擦接触的布料动力学与静力学。针对分段线性壳中变分r-自适应的两个耦合失效模式（IP优化陷于局部最小值、元素坍缩伪造低势能），引入退化激活质量正则化，并设计了动态非线性求解器，在同等顶点数与时间预算下取得比固定网格更高的视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄布料需要高有效空间分辨率以再现褶皱、折叠、屈曲和锐利接触特征，但将现有变分r-自适应性应用于分段线性壳时存在两个耦合的失效模式：离散化增量势能(IP)优化可能陷入不良局部极小值导致次优物理构型；优化还可能通过坍缩元素人为降低IP，使目标依赖的有限元近似失效。

Method: 提出退化激活质量正则化：对形态良好的元素保持非激活以保留各向异性适应和局部加密，但接近退化时变强，抑制虚假低能盆地、改善脱离次优物理极小值的能力，并防止元素聚集（布料滑过锐利接触特征时元素逐步坍缩的布料专属失效）。同时引入动态非线性求解器，通过加速导数求值和动态IPC容差更新利用时间步内相干性，加速r-自适应迭代信赖域(ITR)求解。

Result: 在挑战性的摩擦接触场景实验中，与先前最优ITR相比取得3-6倍加速；在同等顶点数和时间预算约束下，比固定网格方法获得更高的视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 退化激活质量正则化有效解决了r-自适应布料模拟中局部极小值陷阱和元素坍缩问题，配合利用时间相干性的动态非线性求解器，实现了高效且高保真的自适应布料接触模拟。

Abstract: We present the first r-adaptive method for simulating cloth dynamics and statics with frictional contact in modern cloth pipelines. Thin cloth requires high effective spatial resolution to reproduce wrinkles, folds, buckling, and sharp contact features. However, applying existing variational r-adaptivity to piecewise-linear shells reveals two coupled failure modes. Discretized incremental-potential (IP) optimization can become trapped in poor local minima, yielding suboptimal physical configurations. It can also lower IP artificially by collapsing elements, invalidating the finite-element approximation on which the objective relies. We address both problems with degeneracy-activated quality regularization. The regularizer remains inactive for well-shaped elements, preserving anisotropic adaptation and local densification, but becomes strong near degeneracy. It suppresses spurious low-energy basins, improves escape from suboptimal physical minima, and prevents element bunching, a cloth-specific failure in which elements progressively collapse as cloth slides across sharp contact features. For practical performance, we introduce a dynamic nonlinear solver that exploits within-timestep coherence through accelerated derivative evaluation and dynamic IPC tolerance updates for r-adaptive iterative trust-region (ITR) solves. This yields a 3-6x speedup over prior optimal ITR. Experiments on challenging frictional-contact scenarios show that, under equal vertex-count and time-budget constraints, our method achieves higher visual fidelity than fixed meshes.

</details>


### [143] [Love Handles: Decimation for Deformation Handles with Compact Support and Low Memory Footprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17930)
*David IW Levin,Paul Kry,Kartic Subr,Ryan Schmidt,Etienne Vouga,Teseo Schneider*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于抽取（decimation）的算法，用于计算稀疏、紧支撑的变形控制柄（handles），从而对固体弹性动力学进行降阶模拟，实现实时仿真，最高支持包含796,623个四面体的复杂网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体变形物理模拟在计算机动画、工程和机器人领域至关重要，但传统有限元模拟计算开销大，且随着离散化程度提高扩展性差。降阶方法虽能减少自由度，但现有基于控制柄的方法缺乏自动、高效且适用于复杂几何（如高亏格、多孔结构）的生成方案。

Method: （1）提出首个基于抽取（decimation）的算法，通过迭代代数简化优化控制柄变形以匹配任意输入变形（如线性振动模态）；（2）无需修改几何，适用于任意体积网格（包括高亏格和多孔特征）；（3）设计高效算法计算和更新紧支撑及其权重；（4）利用紧支撑特性开发高效的降阶三次积分（reduced-cubature）计算方案。

Result: 优化后的控制柄具有内存高效性，能实现复杂几何的实时弹性动力学仿真。在多种四面体网格上展示了实时性能，最高支持796,623个四面体。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了降阶模拟中控制柄自动生成的问题，通过紧支撑和降阶三次积分实现了大规模复杂网格的实时弹性动力学仿真，兼具计算效率和内存高效性。

Abstract: Estimating the deformation of solids via physical simulation is an important problem spanning fields such as computer animation, engineering and robotics. Such simulations are computationally expensive and scale poorly when the representation of an object is refined by increasing the level of discretization. Reduced Order Methods (ROM) offer computational savings by decreasing the number of degrees of freedom, for example by using \emph{handles} that control groups of vertices. We present the first decimation-based algorithm for computing a sparse, compactly supported set of deformation handles. The crux of our method utilizes iterative algebraic simplification to optimize handle deformation to match any input deformation, such as linear vibration modes. This applies to any volumetric input mesh, including those with high genus or porous features, since we do not alter the geometry. We also devise an efficient algorithm to compute and update compact supports and their associated weights. We leverage compact support to develop an efficient, reduced-cubature computation scheme. Once optimized, our handles offer a memory-efficient solution while enabling real-time elastodynamics simulation of complex geometry. We show real-time performance on a variety of tetrahedral meshes with up to 796,623 tetrahedra.

</details>


### [144] [MetaSapiens v2: Advancing Real-Time Foveated Neural Rendering via Foveation-Aware Pruning and Stereo Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17969)
*Weikai Lin,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: MetaSapiens v2是一个面向VR/AR设备的实时点基神经渲染系统，通过效率感知剪枝、注视点渲染、选择性双目扭曲和专用加速器设计四种技术，在保持人类视觉质量的同时实现比现有PBNR模型高一个数量级的渲染加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点基神经渲染（PBNR）作为一种有前景的渲染技术正在渗透AR/VR和数字孪生等各个领域，但在VR/AR设备上实现实时PBNR渲染面临严峻挑战——设备计算资源有限，而实时高保真渲染需求持续增长。

Method: MetaSapiens v2融合了四种技术：1）效率感知剪枝技术以优化渲染速度；2）利用人类周边视觉敏锐度低的特性，提出面向PBNR的注视点渲染（FR）方法及高效图元；3）利用双眼之间的冗余提出选择性扭曲方法以降低双目渲染的计算开销；4）设计面向双目FR的专用加速器，解决FR-based PBNR中的负载不均衡问题并支持双目渲染的扭曲操作。

Result: 评估表明MetaSapiens v2相比现有PBNR模型实现了数量级的加速提升，同时保持了人类视觉质量。

Conclusion: MetaSapiens v2通过在算法层面（剪枝、FR、选择性扭曲）与硬件层面（专用加速器）的协同设计，成功实现了VR/AR设备上的实时PBNR渲染，证明了软硬件协同优化是弥合神经渲染与边缘设备算力差距的有效路径。

Abstract: Point-Based Neural Rendering (PBNR) is emerging as a promising class of rendering techniques, which are permeating all aspects of society, driven by a growing demand for real-time, photorealistic rendering in AR/VR and digital twins. However, achieving real-time PBNR on VR/AR devices is challenging. This paper proposes MetaSapiens v2, a PBNR system that delivers real-time neural rendering on VR/AR devices while maintaining human visual quality. MetaSapiens v2 combines four techniques. First, we present an efficiency-aware pruning technique to optimize rendering speed. Second, we introduce a Foveated Rendering (FR) method with an efficient primitive for PBNR, leveraging humans' low visual acuity in peripheral regions to relax rendering quality and improve rendering speed. Third, we leverage the redundancy between the two eyes and propose a selective warping method to further reduce the computation overhead in AR/VR binocular rendering. Finally, we propose an accelerator design for binocular FR, addressing the load imbalance issue in (FR-based) PBNR and supporting warping for efficient binocular rendering. Our evaluation shows that MetaSapiens v2 achieves an order of magnitude speedup over existing PBNR models while maintaining the visual quality.

</details>


### [145] [aDSL: Agentic 3D Creation via Joint Agent-Program Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17975)
*Rui-Huan Wang,Si-Tong Wei,Jia-Qi He,Heng-Yi Wei,Baoquan Chen,Peng-Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向智能体的领域特定语言（aDSL）与角色专业化多智能体系统的联合设计方法，通过平面-执行-批评循环将自然语言或图像意图转化为可编辑的3D程序化表示，在文本到形状和图像到形状任务上优于现有基于LLM的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的3D程序化生成工作流较为脆弱，难以将高层级用户意图转化为一致的底层几何结构。作者将此归因于现有程序化接口与LLM推理优势之间的不匹配——LLM擅长语义结构和空间关系推理，而非脆弱的数值选择。

Method: 本文采用联合设计策略：（1）设计了一种面向智能体的领域特定语言（aDSL），通过组合性和空间推理桥接语义逻辑与几何约束，使智能体能够通过关系运算符而非绝对坐标来操作几何体；（2）构建了一个免训练的多智能体系统，遵循Plan-Execute-Critic循环，分解请求、合成代码，并利用执行反馈迭代修复错误和约束违规。

Result: 实验表明，该联合设计方法在文本到形状和图像到形状任务上优于先前基于LLM的基线方法，同时保留了显式结构、可编辑性和可解释性。该方法还支持下游应用，如关节物体创建和结构化场景组合。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的3D程序化生成的关键在于语言设计与智能体架构的协同优化，而非单纯依赖更强大的模型。通过语义优先、关系驱动的DSL设计与角色分工的多智能体协作，可以显著提升程序化3D内容生成的鲁棒性、可控性和对用户意图的忠实度。

Abstract: Programmatic representations provide a compelling paradigm for 3D content creation, enabling fine-grained edits, interpretability, and explicit structural control. Yet, agentic workflows that rely on large language models (LLMs) to author 3D programs remain brittle, often failing to translate high-level intent into consistent low-level geometry. We attribute this fragility to a mismatch between existing programmatic interfaces and the reasoning strengths of LLMs, which favor semantic structure and spatial relations over fragile numeric choices. In this paper, we jointly design an Agent-centric Domain-Specific Language (aDSL) and a role-specialized multi-agent system to close this gap. aDSL bridges semantic logic and geometric constraints by emphasizing composability and spatial reasoning; it enables agents to manipulate geometry through relational operators instead of brittle absolute coordinates. Building on aDSL, our training-free multi-agent system follows a Plan-Execute-Critic loop to decompose requests, synthesize code, and iteratively repair errors and constraint violations using execution feedback. Experiments show that this co-design improves robustness, controllability, and faithfulness to user intent. Our method outperforms prior LLM-based baselines on text-to-shape and image-to-shape tasks while preserving explicit structure, editability, and interpretability. It also enables downstream applications such as articulated object creation and structured scene composition. Our code is available at https://github.com/sig-pku/aDSL.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Detecting and Discriminating Operator Misspecification in Hybrid PDE-Parameter Learning: a Reference-Free Instrument, with Discrimination Bounded In Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16925)
*Eric Fock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种无需oracle、仅从单次拟合即可区分"算子假设错误"与"参数不可辨识"两种失效模式的统计仪器。在自伴抛物型反问题上，信息矩阵统计量在正确设定下中位数为0.19（误拒率0.033低于预注册阈值0.10），在两种误设定下分别升至224和85并全样本触发；在正确设定但不可辨识的设计下保持沉默（n=200时为0.050），同时秩统计量在预注册边界处坍缩至零。该分离能力是核心贡献，而现有精度检查对此盲区：误设定估计器域内RMSE为2.7×10⁻²低于观测噪声，但系数误差高达29.7%-31.2%。不同架构（单参数曲线、裸参数、MLP）收敛到同一伪真值，而PINN因其复合目标收敛到另一个值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反问题估计器的精度检查（如in-domain RMSE）存在根本盲区：一个设定错误（错误假设算子）的估计器可以展现出低于观测噪声的良好RMSE，但其系数误差可达30%左右。标准验证方法无法区分两种根本不同的失效模式——模型设定错误（operatore misspecification）与参数不可辨识（unidentifiability），而这两者需要截然不同的应对策略。因此需要一种能从单次拟合中分离这两种失败的统计工具。

Method: 构建基于信息矩阵的统计量与秩统计量组成的双重检验框架。信息矩阵统计量以plug-in尺度、按种子参数计算，用于检测算子设定错误；秩统计量在预注册边界c₅*=2.15×10⁻³处检验参数不可辨识性。仪器在自伴抛物型逆问题上，通过三种设计（正确设定、两种误设定、正确但不可辨识）验证其分离能力，并对比多种估计架构（单参数曲线、裸参数、49/241参数MLP、PINN）的收敛行为。

Result: 信息矩阵统计量在正确设定下中位数0.19、误拒率0.033（低于预注册的0.10上限）；在两种误设定下分别升至224和85，所有重复全部触发报警。在正确但不可辨识的设计下，信息矩阵统计量保持沉默（0.050，Clopper-Pearson区间[0.024,0.090]），而秩统计量在边界处坍缩至零。不同架构收敛同一伪真值（匹配精度0.07%），但PINN因复合目标收敛到不相交的另一个值。误设定估计器系数误差29.7%-31.2%，但RMSE低于噪声水平。

Conclusion: 从单次拟合中即可分离算子误设定与参数不可辨识两种失效，这一分离能力是核心贡献，而非单纯的检测。仪器在样本内给出界，样本外给出方向。但仪器存在已知盲区：在预注册的负例中，神经估计器在恢复任务上输给Tikhonov正则化反演；并且存在一个假设条件下仪器保证成立但训练网络违反该条件的情形。

Abstract: We build an instrument that reads, from a single fit and with no oracle, whether the operator a hybrid PDE-parameter estimator postulates is wrong-and separates that from a merely unidentifiable parameter. On one self-adjoint parabolic inverse problem, an information-matrix statistic with plug-in scale and per-seed parameter has median 0.19 under correct specification, rejection rate $0.033$ against a pre-registered ceiling of $0.10$, and rises to $224$ and $85$ under two misspecifications, firing in every replicate. On a correctly specified but non-identifiable design it stays mute-$0.050$ at $n=200$, Clopper-Pearson $[0.024, 0.090]$-while a rank statistic collapses to zero at a pre-registered boundary $c_5^*=2.15\times10^{-3}.$ Two readings of one fit therefore separate the two failures across the three designs a deployable test reaches. That separation is the contribution; detection alone is a crowded flank. In sample it is a bound, out of sample a direction. It is needed because the usual accuracy check is blind: the misspecified estimator's in-domain RMSE is $2.7\times 10^{-2}$, below the observation noise for $σ\geq 0.05,$ while the coefficient is wrong by $29.7\%$ at zero noise, $31.2\%$ at the loudest. Nor is the failure architectural: a one-parameter curve fit, a bare parameter and multilayer perceptrons of $49$ and $241$ parameters converge to the same pseudo-true, matched in closed form to $0.07\%,$ whereas a physics-informed network, with its composite objective, converges to a disjoint one. We report where the instrument is blind, a pre-registered negative where a neural estimator loses to Tikhonov-regularized inversion at recovery, and the hypothesis under which its guarantee holds but a trained network violates it.

</details>


### [147] [Proactive Road Safety Intervention in Australia: Predicting Risky Driving Hotspots from Connected Vehicle Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16913)
*Adriana-Simona Mihăiţă,Clarence Cheung,Artur Grigorev,Tuo Mao,David Lillo-Trynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用澳大利亚大悉尼地区的联网车辆遥测数据，通过g力阈值（急刹车>0.6g、急转弯>0.47g、急加速>0.5g）量化危险驾驶行为，在地方政府区域（LGA）层面检测和预测"近碰撞"风险驾驶事件，并构建时空热力图识别高风险区域。八种预测模型被跨三个家族（集成学习、深度学习、经典时间序列方法）进行基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道路安全监测是反应性的，依赖于伤亡事故发生后的碰撞记录分析。在事故实际发生之前主动识别高风险位置和危险驾驶行为是一个关键但未被充分探索的挑战，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用澳大利亚大悉尼地区的联网车辆遥测数据，通过g力阈值（急刹车>0.6g、急转弯>0.47g、急加速>0.5g）量化危险驾驶行为，构建时空热力图识别高风险区域，并基准测试八种预测模型（Random Forests、XGBoost、LightGBM、LSTM、N-BEATS、ARIMA、Exponential Smoothing、Prophet）。

Result: ARIMA取得了最低的平均绝对误差（MAE：162.21），表现与LSTM（MAE：163.92）相当，优于所有集成学习方法；N-BEATS达到MAE 180.75。

Conclusion: 在训练数据量有限的情况下，简洁的时间序列模型与深度学习方法具有竞争力。研究凸显了基于物联网的联网车辆数据在支持主动道路安全干预方面的潜力，悉尼内城区和西部的LGA（CBD、Parramatta、Bankstown）被确定为持续高风险区域，需要针对性的政策干预。

Abstract: Road safety monitoring has historically been reactive, relying on crash-record analysis after fatalities and injuries have already occurred. Proactive identification of high-risk locations and dangerous driving behaviour before incidents occur is a critical but underexplored challenge. This paper addresses this gap using connected vehicle telemetry data from Greater Sydney, Australia, to detect and forecast near-miss risky driving events at the Local Government Area (LGA) level. Risky driving is quantified through g-force thresholds (hard braking >0.6g, harsh cornering >0.47g, harsh acceleration >0.5g), and spatio-temporal heatmaps are constructed to identify high-risk zones. Eight predictive models are benchmarked across three families: ensemble learning (Random Forests, XGBoost, LightGBM), deep learning (LSTM, N-BEATS), and classical time-series methods (ARIMA, Exponential Smoothing, Prophet). ARIMA achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE: 162.21), performing comparably to LSTM (MAE: 163.92) and outperforming all ensemble methods, with N-BEATS reaching an MAE of 180.75. These results demonstrate that parsimonious time-series models are competitive with deep learning approaches when training data volume is limited. The study highlights the potential of IoT-based connected vehicle data to support proactive road safety interventions, with Sydney's inner and western LGAs (CBD, Parramatta, Bankstown) identified as persistent high-risk zones warranting targeted policy action.

</details>


### [148] [Reinforcement Learning as (Discrete) Potential Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17181)
*Christopher Connolly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了概率论与位势理论之间的深层联系，并从位势理论的视角审视了固定策略假设下的强化学习核心表征与算法，指出该视角可能为提升样本效率和施加形式化约束提供新路径，并将线性位势理论框架自然扩展至非线性情形以放宽固定策略假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习的理论基础根植于马尔可夫链所依赖的概率论，而概率论与位势理论之间存在深刻联系。作者希望通过引入位势理论的视角，为强化学习的核心表征和算法提供新的理解方式，从而在样本效率和形式化约束方面取得改进。

Method: 本文采用综述（review）的方法，首先梳理概率论与位势理论之间的理论联系，然后在固定策略假设下，用位势理论的框架重新审视强化学习的核心表示与算法。此外，作者还展示了如何将线性的位势理论框架自然延伸至非线性情形，从而放松固定策略的假设。

Result: 论文表明位势理论视角能够为强化学习提供新的分析框架，可能带来样本效率的提升和形式化约束的引入；同时，将线性位势理论扩展至非线性情形后，可以处理策略非固定的更一般情境。

Conclusion: 位势理论为强化学习提供了一种有前景的替代性理论视角，其潜在优势包括提升样本效率和提供形式化约束，且该框架可以被推广至非线性情形以覆盖更广泛的RL设置。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) theory fundamentally depends on probability theory through the Markov chain. There is a deep connection between probability theory and potential theory. This paper reviews that connection and explores the potential-theoretic viewpoint for core reinforcement learning representations and algorithms under a fixed-policy assumption. This viewpoint may offer a path for improved sample efficiency and formal constraints that can be applied to RL. When the fixed-policy assumption is relaxed, the linear potential theory framework can be naturally extended to the nonlinear case.

</details>


### [149] [The concentration game: Bayesian updating, regret, and information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18061)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个二人零和重复博弈框架，其中学习者和自然（nature）之间的博弈其价值恒等式同时生成贝叶斯更新和指数加权遗憾的精确核算，并提供了比较器类的变分形式，统一了一大类集中现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在建立一个统一的博弈论框架，将贝叶斯更新、指数加权遗憾、大偏差界以及多种强化学习方法（bandits、后验采样、聚合、boosting）置于同一个理论体系之下，取代传统的二次变差（quadratic-variation）代理方法。

Method: 方法的核心是定义一个二人零和重复博弈：终端收益为比较器在固定相对熵（相对于先验）下能获得的最大收益，单步约束是自然在学习者混合行动下的信息预算。学习者的行动不受其他限制，Gibbs/贝叶斯权重作为其唯一的Bellman均衡策略（即使每轮损失独立于自然移动方向的混合行动）出现，log-配分函数充当价值函数。遗憾被精确分解为三部分：每轮信息损失（反映观测结果的变异）、加性重调温漂移（精确核算轮间测量尺度变化）、以及比较器相对于先验携带的信息。

Result: 该分解精确且普遍成立，方差和有界范围代理只是此分解的松弛版本。两位玩家的策略可逐项从分解中读出，重复博弈产生信息论意义上的自博弈账本。该比较器类几何同时解释了经典大偏差界，bandits、后验采样、聚合和boosting等方法都是这一统一遗憾分解的特例。

Conclusion: 该框架给出了一个以信息论为核心、以博弈论为语言的统一遗憾分析理论，将Gibbs/贝叶斯更新、指数加权遗憾和广泛的方法论统一在一个精确的分解之下，且两位玩家的最优策略均可显式读取。

Abstract: We give a two-player zero-sum repeated game between a learner and nature whose value identity generates Bayesian updating and an exact accounting of exponential-weights regret at once, and supplies the comparator-class variational form that a wide class of concentration phenomena share. The terminal payoff is the most a comparator can gain at fixed relative entropy from the prior, and the one-step constraint is an information budget on nature's move under the learner's mixed action. With the learner's move otherwise unrestricted, Gibbs/Bayes weights emerge as its unique Bellman equalizer -- the mixed action that makes the per-round loss independent of which direction nature moves -- with log-partition functions playing the role of value functions. The regret decomposes exactly into three parts: a per-round information loss reflecting the variation in observed outcomes, an additive retempering drift that accounts exactly for any change of measurement scale between rounds, and the information the comparator carries relative to the prior. The variance and bounded-range proxies that drive standard regret bounds are looser relaxations of this decomposition, which holds generally and governs them all. Both players' strategies are read off from the decomposition term by term, and repeated play yields an information-theoretic ledger of self-play in place of the usual quadratic-variation surrogate. The same comparator-class geometry accounts for the classical large-deviation bounds, and methods across bandits, posterior sampling, aggregation, and boosting are specializations of the one regret decomposition.

</details>


### [150] [Data-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization for Target Model Data Selection in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16926)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Data-DPO，一种以目标模型为导向的SFT数据选择方法，通过一步探测观察目标模型在样本上的局部训练反馈，将激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量奖励模型学习目标模型感知的数据偏好，并结合外部质量分数和边际多样性构建更稳定有效的训练子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法通常将数据价值视为相对静态的属性，忽视了数据与目标模型能力分布之间的兼容性。因此需要一种考虑目标模型自身能力分布的数据选择方法。

Method: Data-DPO通过三步实现：(1) 使用一步探测观察目标模型在不同样本上的局部训练反馈；(2) 将样本间的激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量奖励模型学习目标模型感知的数据偏好；(3) 在最终选择阶段结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性，构建稳定有效的训练子集。

Result: 在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT上的实验表明，Data-DPO在多种数据预算下一致优于现有数据选择基线，且稳定超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: 数据选择应面向目标模型的能力分布进行动态评估，Data-DPO通过将模型反馈转化为数据偏好的方式，为SFT数据选择提供了一种更有效且稳定的方案。

Abstract: Data selection in supervised fine-tuning aims to select a small set of effective samples from large-scale candidate data, reducing training cost while preserving model performance. However, existing methods usually treat data value as a relatively static property, and pay limited attention to the compatibility between data and the capability distribution of the target model. To address this issue, we propose Data-DPO, a target model-oriented SFT data selection method. Data-DPO observes the local training feedback of the target model on different samples through one-step probing, transforms activation differences among samples into pairwise data preferences, and trains a lightweight reward model to learn target-model-aware data preferences. In the final selection stage, Data-DPO further combines target model preference, external quality scores, and marginal diversity to construct a more stable and effective training subset. Experimental results on Vision-Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that Data-DPO consistently outperforms existing data selection baselines under multiple data budgets and stably surpasses full data training performance.

</details>


### [151] [Hierarchical Data Selection via Manifold Coverage and Sparse Feature Coverage in LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16927)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MASS提出了一种从粗到细的分层覆盖数据选择方法，通过密集自编码器学习低维主流形坐标进行粗粒度语义分组，再使用TopK稀疏自编码器在每个组内进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖。在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT上，MASS在多个预算下均优于强基线，且仅用少量子集数据即可匹配甚至超越全量数据训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着监督微调数据规模不断扩大，从大型候选池中选取高价值子集对降低训练成本和提升模型性能至关重要。现有方法直接在原始嵌入空间中度量多样性，但几何指标会将主导语义方向、细粒度监督差异和局部噪声纠缠在一起，无法有效区分高质量数据。

Method: 将数据选择形式化为从粗到细的分层覆盖问题。首先使用密集自编码器学习低维主流形坐标，实现粗粒度语义分组；然后使用TopK稀疏自编码器在每个分组内执行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，从而在保持语义多样性的同时捕捉细粒度监督差异。

Result: 在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT基准上，MASS在多个数据预算设置下均一致优于强大的数据选择基线，并且在若干设置中仅使用小部分子集数据就能匹配或超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: MASS通过分层粗到细覆盖策略有效解决了原始嵌入空间中几何度量混杂的问题，证明了层级稀疏特征覆盖在数据选择中的有效性，为大规模监督微调数据筛选提供了更优方案。

Abstract: As supervised fine-tuning data continues to scale, selecting high-value subsets from large candidate pools is crucial for reducing training cost and improving model performance. Existing methods often measure diversity directly in the original embedding space, where geometric metrics entangle dominant semantic directions, fine-grained supervision differences, and local noise. We address this limitation by formulating data selection as a coarse-to-fine hierarchical coverage problem and propose MASS. MASS learns low-dimensional principal manifold coordinates with a dense autoencoder for coarse semantic grouping, and then performs quality-aware sparse feature coverage within each group using a TopK sparse autoencoder. Experiments on Vision Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that MASS consistently outperforms strong data selection baselines across multiple budgets, and in several settings matches or surpasses full data training with only a small subset of data.

</details>


### [152] [Benchmarking Classical and Transformer-Based Models for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16928)
*Aleesha Zainab,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍Strategic 16K——一个从WikiLeaks PlusD构建的16,000条外交电报的泄露受控语料库，系统性地识别并消除了三类残留分类标记（标签泄露），并在该干净基准上对六种模型架构（经典机器学习和Transformer）进行了评估。BERT表现最佳（准确率89.14%，F1=89.33%），TF-IDF+逻辑回归在经典模型中表现最强且计算成本显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织文档的自动敏感性分类是一个关键但未被充分解决的问题，错分后果包括监管违规和安全漏洞。AI方法的可靠性取决于训练数据的完整性，而该领域普遍存在标签泄露问题：文档主体中残留的分类标记允许模型利用表面捷径而非学习真实的内容敏感性信号，导致性能估计虚高且不可靠。现有研究缺乏泄露控制的基准。

Method: 从WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD)构建Strategic 16K语料库，包含16,000条外交电报。采用扩展的泄露移除协议，识别并消除文档主体中嵌入的三类残留分类标记。在干净基准上系统评估六种模型架构，涵盖经典机器学习（如TF-IDF+逻辑回归）和Transformer方法（如BERT、ELECTRA）。

Result: 在干净基准上，BERT取得最佳性能（Accuracy=89.14%，F1=89.33%），ELECTRA紧随其后（Accuracy=88.57%，F1=88.90%）。经典模型中TF-IDF+逻辑回归在显著较低的计算成本下达到最强性能。

Conclusion: 这是首个在明确的泄露控制条件下从WikiLeaks PlusD构建的完全可复现的敏感性分类基准，为组织文档自动敏感性分类提供了可靠的评估标准。

Abstract: Automatic sensitivity classification of organizational documents is a critical yet underserved problem, where the consequences of misclassification range from regulatory violations to security breaches. While AI-based approaches offer a scalable alternative to manual review, their reliability depends fundamentally on the integrity of training data. A pervasive but underreported problem in this domain is label leakage: residual classification markers embedded within document bodies that allow models to exploit surface shortcuts rather than learning genuine content-based sensitivity signals, producing performance estimates that are inflated and unreliable. This paper addresses this problem by introducing Strategic 16K, a carefully constructed, leakage-controlled corpus of 16,000 diplomatic cables sourced from the WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD), and presents a systematic benchmark evaluating six model architectures spanning classical machine learning and transformer-based approaches. We document an extended leakage removal protocol that identifies and eliminates three categories of residual classification markers embedded within document bodies. On the clean benchmark, BERT achieves the strongest performance (Accuracy = 89.14%, F1 = 89.33%), followed by ELECTRA (Accuracy = 88.57%, F1 = 88.90%). Among classical models, TF-IDF with Logistic Regression achieves the strongest performance at significantly lower computational cost. These results constitute the first fully reproducible sensitivity classification benchmark constructed under explicit leakage-controlled conditions from WikiLeaks PlusD.

</details>


### [153] [Mr.Dec: Daily-Scale Longitudinal Multimodal Modeling for 30-Day Readmission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16929)
*Minjun Kim,Jong Hak Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mr.Dec（多模态再入院风险预测解码器），将住院过程建模为每日多模态事件的时序序列，利用Transformer解码器融合每日EHR更新与间歇性胸部X光发现，并通过疾病特异性监督对比学习增强鲁棒性，在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上达到最新最优性能，同时能识别住院期间的"关键日"以提供临床可解释的风险分层依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法往往将复杂的纵向住院病史压缩为固定表示，丢失了反映患者生理状态演变的关键日级临床信号。30天再入院风险随住院期间证据积累而动态变化，需要捕捉这种动态轨迹以更准确地评估患者状态并优化医疗资源配置。

Method: Mr.Dec将每次住院建模为每日多模态事件的自然时间序列，通过Transformer解码器在时间对齐的流中整合每日EHR更新与间歇性CXR发现，模拟真实临床工作流程；同时利用疾病特异性监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在潜在空间中诱导诊断感知的结构。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上的评估表明，Mr.Dec通过保持临床序列的完整性实现了最先进的性能，并能识别住院期间的"关键日"，为实时风险分层提供可操作且具有临床依据的解释。

Conclusion: Mr.Dec证明了保留住院期间每日多模态临床信号的时序完整性相比压缩为固定表示能显著提升再入院风险预测性能，且具备良好的临床可解释性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Predicting 30-day hospital readmission is essential for assessing patient stability and optimizing healthcare resources. As clinical risk evolves with the accumulation of evidence during hospitalization, capturing these dynamic trajectories is essential. However, many existing approaches compress the complex longitudinal history into fixed representations, often losing the granular, day-level clinical signals that reflect a patient's evolving physiological state. To address this, we propose Mr.Dec (Multimodal Readmission-risk prediction Decoder), which models each admission as a natural chronological sequence of daily multimodal events. By leveraging a Transformer Decoder, Mr.Dec integrates daily Electronic Health Record(EHR) updates and intermittent Chest X-ray(CXR) findings in a time-aligned stream, reflecting the actual clinical workflow. To ensure robustness, we utilize Disease-Specific Supervised Contrastive Learning as an auxiliary regularization to induce a diagnosis-aware structure in the latent space. Evaluations on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets show that Mr.Dec achieves state-of-the-art performance by preserving the integrity of the clinical sequence. Furthermore, our model identifies "Critical Days" within an admission, providing actionable and clinically grounded interpretations for real-time risk stratification. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/MR.DEC

</details>


### [154] [EMAN: Optimization-Driven Capacity Growth through Path Emergence in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16930)
*Chenlei Fang,Jingchen Li,Hongzong LI,Qingyao Li,Yixuan Zhang,Huarui Wu,Haobin Shi,Chunjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN)，一种优化驱动的多任务学习框架。它从单一路径计算开始，仅当出现持续的优化证据时才动态生长出新的独立路径，通过潜在相对相位暴露反对称的生长方向，并在训练期间监控多个决策信号将局部优化证据转化为结构决策。在rank控制实验、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTL方法在容量变化上受限于预定义结构或依赖任务边界和冲突信号触发，缺乏从单路径动态生长新路径的能力。作者提出核心问题：网络能否从精确的单路径计算开始，仅在出现持续的优化证据时才生长出一条新的独立路径？

Method: EMAN包含两个关键机制：(1) 通过潜在相对相位暴露反对称的生长方向，而无需实例化第二条路径；(2) 训练期间监控多个决策信号，将局部优化证据（local optimization evidence）转化为结构决策（structural decision）。只有在认证（certification）通过后，才实体化两条等容量（equal-capacity）的独立路径，并自适应地分配共享和任务特定的表示容量。

Result: 在控制秩（controlled rank）设定、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2三个基准上进行了广泛实验，取得了改进的性能，同时保持了有竞争力的计算成本。

Conclusion: EMAN验证了优化驱动的动态结构生长在多任务学习中的可行性：网络能够从单路径启动，依据训练中的优化证据自动生长独立路径并合理分配共享与任务特定容量，在提升性能的同时不显著增加计算开销。

Abstract: Existing multi-task learning methods rely on hard sharing, multiple paths or experts, adaptive sharing, and dynamic expansion. However, their capacity changes are usually constrained by predefined structures or triggered by task boundaries and conflict signals. This raises a fundamental question: can a network start from exact single-path computation and grow a new independent path only when persistent optimization evidence appears? We propose the Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN), an optimization-driven framework for exposing an antisymmetric growth direction through latent relative phases without instantiating a second path, and for monitoring multiple decision signals during training to transform local optimization evidence into a structural decision. EMAN materializes two equal-capacity independent paths only after certification. EMAN adaptively allocates shared and task-specific representation capacity to accommodate varying task requirements. Extensive experiments on controlled rank settings, PASCAL-Context, and NYUv2 validate its effectiveness, achieving improved performance at a competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [155] [Physics-Informed and Hybrid Machine Learning in Additive Manufacturing: Application to Fused Filament Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17246)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Sankaran Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在熔融沉积成型（FFF）零件的粘结质量和孔隙率预测中，将物理知识融入数据驱动深度学习模型的多种物理信息与混合机器学习策略。通过三种策略（损失函数中嵌入物理约束、物理模型输出作为额外输入、物理模型预训练后用实验数据微调）八种组合，表明多策略结合能在有限实验数据下产生精确的机器学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFF零件的粘结质量和孔隙率预测对零件性能至关重要，但纯数据驱动的深度学习模型往往缺乏物理一致性，且实验数据获取成本高、样本有限。因此需要探索如何将物理知识（如物理定律和多物理场仿真结果）引入数据驱动模型，以提高预测准确性和物理合理性。

Method: 文章探索了三种将物理约束和FFF多物理场仿真结果融入深度神经网络（DNN）的策略：(1) 在DNN损失函数中加入物理约束；(2) 将物理模型输出作为DNN的额外输入特征；(3) 先用物理模型的输入输出对DNN进行预训练，再用实验数据更新微调模型。这三种策略被组合成八种不同的变体进行系统研究。

Result: 结果表明，多策略组合能够产生准确的机器学习模型，即使在实验数据有限的条件下也是如此。通过物理约束强制了粘结质量与拉伸强度之间物理一致的关系，使孔隙率预测具有物理意义。

Conclusion: 将多种物理信息策略（损失函数约束、物理模型输入注入、预训练微调）相结合，可以有效提升深度学习模型在FFF零件质量预测中的准确性和物理一致性，尤其适用于实验数据有限的实际工程场景。物理知识与数据驱动的混合学习范式是提高增材制造质量预测可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: This article investigates several physics-informed and hybrid machine learning strategies that incorporate physics knowledge in experimental data-driven deep-learning models for predicting the bond quality and porosity of fused filament fabrication (FFF) parts. Three types of strategies are explored to incorporate physics constraints and multi-physics FFF simulation results into a deep neural network (DNN), thus ensuring consistency with physical laws: (1) incorporate physics constraints within the loss function of the DNN, (2) use physics model outputs as additional inputs to the DNN model, and (3) pre-train a DNN model with physics model input-output and then update it with experimental data. These strategies help to enforce a physically consistent relationship between bond quality and tensile strength, thus making porosity predictions physically meaningful. Eight different combinations of the above strategies are investigated. The results show how the combination of multiple strategies produces accurate machine learning models even with limited experimental data.

</details>


### [156] [SW-ProxyCE: Zero-Query Adversarial Transfer from Public EEG Encoders to Private Downstream Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16931)
*Linhua Cong,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了EEG基础模型在"公共编码器-私有下游模型"部署场景下的对抗迁移攻击安全风险，提出无查询的任务感知攻击框架SW-ProxyCE，通过缩水白化类原型恢复任务决策几何，在无需训练替代分类器的情况下生成可迁移的对抗样本，并在多个EEG任务和编码器上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型的公开编码器促进了下游任务开发，但公开的表示可能使私有下游模型遭受对抗攻击。已有研究未探索这一安全风险，即攻击者无法访问或查询受害者模型的参数、输出和梯度时，仍可能通过公开的编码器生成有效对抗样本。

Method: 提出Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE)框架。攻击者拥有编码器的白盒访问权和少量任务匹配的标注参考集；该方法通过缩水白化类原型恢复任务级决策几何，直接生成可迁移的对抗样本，无需训练额外的替代分类器，是一种无查询（query-free）的任务感知攻击方法。

Result: 在三个EEG任务上使用三个通用基础编码器和一个范式特定预训练编码器进行评估，覆盖线性探测和全微调下游模型，跨受试者和受试者内场景。结果显示从公共编码器和有限标注参考生成的对抗样本可有效迁移到不可访问的下游模型；SW-ProxyCE一致优于任务无关的表示偏移攻击，表明EEG基础模型的强迁移性并不必然带来对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型虽具强迁移性，但其公开编码器的部署引入了新的对抗安全漏洞。SW-ProxyCE揭示了任务感知的对抗迁移攻击在EEG基础模型场景中的威胁性，提示社区需关注EEG基础模型部署中的安全设计。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for EEG decoding by learning reusable representations from large-scale heterogeneous neural recordings. However, the open release of EEG foundation encoders, while facilitating downstream developments, also introduces a previously unexplored security risk: publicly available representations may make private downstream models vulnerable. This paper investigates adversarial transfer attacks in EEG foundation model deployment in a public-encoder and private-downstream setting, where attackers have white-box access to a released encoder and a small task-matched labeled reference set, but no access or query to victim parameters, outputs, or gradients. We propose Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE), a query-free task-aware attack framework that recovers task-level decision geometry from a small labeled reference set through shrinkage-whitened class prototypes, enabling transferable adversarial generation without training an additional surrogate classifier. We evaluated SW-ProxyCE across three EEG tasks using three general-purpose foundation encoders and a paradigm-specific pre-trained encoder, covering both linear-probing and full-fine-tuning downstream models in cross-subject and within-subject scenarios. Results demonstrated that adversarial examples generated from the public encoder and limited labeled references can effectively transfer to inaccessible downstream models. SW-ProxyCE consistently outperformed task-agnostic representation-shift attacks, revealing that the strong transferability of EEG foundation models does not necessarily lead to adversarial robustness. Our code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [157] [DOW-KE: Anchor-Free Multi-Layer Knowledge Editing via Direct End-to-End Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16932)
*Ran Chen,Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DOW-KE，一种无锚点的知识编辑方法，核心原则是"优化即部署"，通过端到端反向传播联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，同时将保护投影嵌入更新参数化中以确保编辑不污染受保护知识，在六组模型-数据集设置中的五组取得最佳综合Score和邻域Specificity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层定位-然后-编辑（locate-then-edit）方法存在闭包差距：它们在选定层优化目标残差流激活（锚点），但部署的是多层权重更新，而多层权重更新通过真实前向传播的联合效应从未被直接优化。每个更新仅来自局部求解，传播引起的衰减和失真无法被校正，导致锚点目标与实际编辑效果不一致。

Method: DOW-KE采用无锚点设计：(1) 将最终编辑目标通过完整模型反向传播，联合优化所有编辑层的更新，使跨层传播与耦合进入每个梯度步骤；(2) 将保护投影嵌入更新参数化中（置于计算图内部），使每个梯度作用于实际部署的更新上，而非采用事后约束（事后约束会重新打开闭包差距）。

Result: 在两个数据集和三个模型的大规模顺序编辑实验中，DOW-KE在六个模型-数据集设置中的五个取得最高综合Score和邻域Specificity。

Conclusion: 通过在优化目标中直接纳入真实前向传播的联合效应，并以计算图内嵌的保护机制约束搜索空间，DOW-KE实现了更忠实于部署效果的知识编辑，验证了"优化即部署"这一原则的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-layer locate-then-edit methods for knowledge editing first optimize target residual-stream activations (anchors) at selected layers, then realize them layer by layer as weight updates. This pipeline optimizes an intermediate representation but deploys multi-layer weight updates whose joint effect through the true forward pass is never itself optimized: regardless of how anchors are set or propagated, each update comes from a local solve, so propagation-induced attenuation and distortion go uncorrected, leaving a closure gap between anchor targets and realized edits. We propose DOW-KE, an anchor-free method built on a single principle: what is optimized must be exactly what is deployed. DOW-KE backpropagates the final editing objective through the complete model, jointly optimizing the updates of all edited layers so cross-layer propagation and coupling enter every gradient step. The same principle dictates where preservation resides: embedding the preservation projection in the update parameterization, inside the computation graph, makes every gradient act on the deployed update; post-hoc constraints would reopen the gap, and the constrained search keeps edits clear of protected knowledge. In large-scale sequential editing on two datasets and three models, DOW-KE achieves the highest overall Score and neighborhood Specificity in five of six model-dataset settings among the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [158] [TabNSM: Neural Sparse Mixer for Tabular Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18026)
*Ali Eslamian,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TabNSM，一个可扩展的深度表格回归框架，通过自适应稀疏交互模块（ASIM）、多阶段回归头、GridLoss序数感知软分箱目标和RISE困难感知采样策略，在九个真实世界回归基准上实现了强预测性能和实际可扩展性，尤其在高维和异构数据集上表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维表格回归任务具有挑战性：树模型鲁棒但缺乏端到端表示学习能力，而深度模型虽然支持灵活的特征学习，但常常面临高昂的交互建模成本和噪声/冗余特征的敏感性。

Method: TabNSM由三部分组成：1) 核心的ASIM（自适应稀疏交互模块）将前景特征发现、稀疏局部交互编码和特征-令牌混合整合在一起，在固定稀疏配置下提供近线性复杂度；2) 多阶段回归头用于渐进式预测精化；3) GridLoss作为序数感知的软分箱目标函数，将目标结构融入表示学习；4) RISE（基于误差的重加权实例采样）利用损失分位数分箱实现困难感知采样。

Result: 在九个真实世界回归基准上，TabNSM展现了强预测性能和实际可扩展性，特别是在高维和异构数据集上取得了持续性增益。

Conclusion: 选择性交互建模、结构化回归监督和困难感知采样为深度表格回归提供了一种有效且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Large-scale, high-dimensional tabular regression remains challenging: tree-based models are robust but lack end-to-end representation learning, while deep models enable flexible feature learning but often incur costly interaction modeling and sensitivity to noisy or redundant features. We propose TabNSM, a scalable regression framework that extends our earlier sparse-attention and mixer architectures. At its core, the Adaptive Sparse Interaction Module (ASIM) integrates foreground feature discovery, sparse local interaction encoding, and Feature-Token Mixing, providing near-linear complexity under fixed sparse configurations. For regression, TabNSM introduces three complementary components: a Multi-Stage Regression Head for progressive prediction refinement; GridLoss, an ordinal-aware soft-binning objective that incorporates target structure into representation learning; and RISE (Reweighted Instance Sampling by Error), a difficulty-aware sampling strategy based on loss-quantile bins. Across nine real-world regression benchmarks, TabNSM delivers strong predictive performance and practical scalability, with particularly consistent gains on high-dimensional and heterogeneous datasets. These results demonstrate that selective interaction modeling, structured regression supervision, and difficulty-aware sampling provide an effective and scalable approach to deep tabular regression.

</details>


### [159] [Study-Strategy Clusters from EdNet Logs Track Engagement, Not Mastery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16963)
*Qingchuan Lyu,Yingxin Li,Albert Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在EdNet-KT3数据上检验了"对ITS日志进行无监督聚类得到的簇可作为学习类型并预测学习效果"这一假设。聚类学习策略特征得到8种稳定的学习风格，早期聚类能预测后期参与度（坚持度η²≈0.106）但无法预测后期无辅助正确率（p_adj≈0.093）；知识追踪模型（SAKT）仅比基准略好（AUC提升+0.051），且行为风格与知识掌握信号几乎无关（ARI=0.007）。结论：行为聚类描述的是学习风格与参与度，而非知识增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析领域普遍假设可以从智能导学系统（ITS）日志中无监督聚类出"学习者类型"，并认为这些类型应能预测学习结果。作者旨在检验这一隐含假设是否成立，探究行为聚类究竟预测的是参与度还是实际的知识获取。

Method: 使用EdNet-KT3数据集，选取5000名活跃学习者；对学习策略特征（资源使用、复习、视频、题目练习）进行层次聚类，先以轮廓系数选取k=5父切分，再对残余群体（约64.9%）重新聚类得到8种命名策略并通过bootstrap验证稳定性。将每个学习者时间线按应答计数分为前后两半，用前半聚类、后半评估结果。对比早期簇对后期参与度（坚持练习与完成会话）和无帮助正确率（无求助首次尝试正确性）的预测力。此外，使用SAKT知识追踪模型在7个TOEIC考试部分预测下次正确性，并与仅知各部分难度的基准对比；用ARI评估行为风格与掌握信号的重叠度。

Result: 聚类产生4个对比极性（阅读型、视频型、复习型、先解题型）加上约64.9%的均值残余，残余再聚类得到4种更精细风格，形成bootstrap稳定的8策略层次结构。早期簇显著预测后期参与度（坚持度η²≈0.106，完成度η²≈0.021），但不预测后期无辅助正确率（p_adj≈0.093）。仅基于学习量的聚类与策略标签几乎不对齐（ARI=0.064）。SAKT模型仅比难度基准有微弱提升（AUC抬升+0.051，CI[+0.045,+0.058]），行为风格与掌握信号几乎独立（ARI=0.007）。

Conclusion: 行为聚类能够有效刻画学习者的学习风格与参与模式，但其预测力局限于参与度层面，无法波及实际的知识获得（无辅助正确率）。行为-掌握信号的极低ARI揭示行为风格与知识状态是两个几乎独立的维度。这挑战了"聚类日志可得学习类型并能预测学习"的传统假设，提示在研究与实践中使用行为聚类作为学习效果代理时需谨慎。

Abstract: Learning analytics often treats unsupervised clusters of intelligent tutoring system (ITS) logs as learner types that should predict learning. We test that assumption on EdNet-KT3. Clustering study-strategy features (resource use, revision, video, problem practice) for 5{,}000 active learners yields a silhouette-selected parent cut ($k=5$) with 4 contrast poles (reading-focused, video-heavy, revision-heavy, and problem-first) plus a large near-mean residual ($\sim$64.9\%). Reclustering that residual adds four finer styles, giving a bootstrap-stable hierarchy of 8 named strategies. We split each learner's timeline by respond count so clusters use only the early half and outcomes only the late half. Early clusters predict later engagement (continuing to practice and finishing late sessions, especially persistence, $η^{2}\approx 0.106$; completion $η^{2}\approx 0.021$) but not later unassisted accuracy (correctness on late first-attempts without help; $p_{\mathrm{adj}}\approx 0.093$). Volume rises with some styles, yet volume-only clustering barely matches strategy labels (ARI$=0.064$). A knowledge-tracing model (SAKT) on the seven TOEIC exam sections predicts next correctness only modestly better than a baseline that knows only how hard each section usually is (AUC lift $+0.051$; CI $[+0.045,+0.058]$), and that mastery signal is nearly independent of behavior styles (ARI$=0.007$). Behavioral clustering here describes study styles and engagement, not knowledge gains.

</details>


### [160] [RoBell-RVFL: A Robust Generalized Bell Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16965)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoBell-RVFL，一种基于广义钟形（gbell）隶属函数的鲁棒随机向量函数链接网络，通过双策略样本级加权机制在保留少数类信息的同时自适应抑制多数类中的噪声、边界和离群样本，在不牺牲RVFL闭环学习效率的前提下显著提升类不平衡与标签噪声场景下的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集中的多数类主导问题会导致随机神经网络产生有偏的决策边界，而现有方法（如SMOTE和类加权损失函数）主要关注类比例而忽视类内分布，对标签噪声和离群点敏感，缺乏对样本质量的感知能力。

Method: RoBell-RVFL采用双策略样本级加权机制：对少数类样本使用单位权重严格保留其信息，对多数类样本通过核诱导特征空间中的概率加权广义钟形（gbell）隶属函数自适应调节其影响，从而抑制噪声、边界和离群样本，同时将局部类概率与类分布信息显式融入学习过程，并保持RVFL的闭环（closed-form）求解效率。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上以及高达40%标签噪声的鲁棒性测试中，RoBell-RVFL一致且显著优于近期最先进的RVFL变体，验证了自适应质量感知样本加权在噪声和不平衡环境下对鲁棒RVFL学习的关键作用。

Conclusion: 自适应、质量感知的样本加权是鲁棒RVFL学习的关键，传统全局加权方案在噪声和不平衡环境中表现不佳，RoBell-RVFL通过局部自适应加权机制提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The dominance of majority classes in real-world datasets poses a fundamental challenge to randomized neural networks, often biasing decision boundaries and overlooking critical minority samples. Existing remedies, such as synthetic minority over-sampling (SMOTE) and class-weighted loss functions, primarily address class proportions while neglecting intra-class distribution, making them vulnerable to label noise and outliers. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RoBell-RVFL}, a robust and lightweight \emph{quality-aware} generalized bell random vector functional link network that redefines how randomized models handle class imbalance and noisy data. RoBell-RVFL employs a dual-strategy, sample-level weighting mechanism that strictly preserves minority class information using unit weights, while adaptively regulating the influence of majority class samples through a probability-weighted generalized bell (gbell) membership function in a kernel-induced feature space. This design effectively suppresses noisy, boundary, and outlier samples within the majority class, enabling the network to learn from informative samples rather than merely abundant ones. By explicitly incorporating local class probability and class distribution information into the learning process, RoBell-RVFL achieves adaptive control over sample contributions without sacrificing the closed-form learning efficiency of RVFL networks. Extensive evaluations on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, along with robustness tests under up to 40\% label noise, demonstrate that RoBell-RVFL consistently and significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art RVFL variants. The results indicate that adaptive, quality-aware sample weighting is essential for robust RVFL learning, rendering conventional global weighting schemes ineffective in noisy and imbalanced environments.

</details>


### [161] [MultiSigBERT: Beyond Survival Analysis through Multimodal and Sequential Modeling in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16972)
*Paul Minchella,Stéphane Chrétien,Guillaume Metzler,Loïc Verlingue,Rémi Vaucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MultiSigBERT，一个基于路径签名表示的统一多模态时序生存建模框架，融合EHR中的自由文本报告与结构化数据，在肿瘤学真实队列（2500+患者）上达到C-index 0.743。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）包含互补的异构数据（叙事性临床报告、数值测量、结构化变量），但大多数生存模型局限于单一模态，未能利用患者轨迹的时序特性，限制了临床决策支持的能力。

Method: MultiSigBERT框架包含四步：(1)利用BERT将自由文本医疗报告转换为句子嵌入（通过提取并平均上下文词嵌入）；(2)通过模态特定的PCA压缩表示并与结构化协变量拼接形成联合时序轨迹；(3)使用Rough Paths理论中的Signature变换高效编码高阶时序跨模态交互（无需监督）；(4)将Signature特征作为高维特征输入LASSO正则化的Cox模型估计个体化风险评分。

Result: 在Léon Bérard中心的真实肿瘤学队列（超120,000份医疗报告、2,500+患者）上，模型在独立测试集上实现一致性指数（concordance index）0.743（标准差0.029），验证了联合建模多模态时序动态与患者级几何结构对生存预测的益处。

Conclusion: 联合利用多模态时序信息（文本+结构化）以及路径签名对患者轨迹几何结构的捕捉，能够显著提升生存预测性能，为临床决策提供更精确的个体化风险分层。

Abstract: Machine learning has become an essential component of modern healthcare, where the integration of heterogeneous data sources offers unprecedented opportunities to improve clinical decision-making. Electronic Health Records (EHR) contain complementary information -- including narrative clinical reports, numerical measurements, and structured variables -- yet most survival models remain limited to a single modality or fail to exploit the temporal nature of patient trajectories. We propose MultiSigBERT, a unified framework for multimodal sequential survival modeling in oncology based on path signature representations. Here, narrative medical reports (free-text) are converted into sentence embeddings by extracting and averaging contextual word embeddings. These representations are then compressed via modality-specific PCA and concatenated with structured covariates to form joint temporal trajectories which are then encoded using the Signature transform, a tool from Rough Paths theory that efficiently captures higher-order temporal interactions across modalities without supervision needed. The computed Signature features are finally incorporated as high dimensional features into a LASSO-regularized Cox model to estimate individualized risk scores. The performance of our novel MultiSigBERT pipeline is illustrated on the analysis of a real-world oncology cohort from the Léon Bérard Center, comprising over 120,000 medical reports and structured records from more than 2,500 patients. The model achieves a concordance index of 0.743 (sd 0.029) on an independent test set, demonstrating the benefit of jointly modeling multimodal temporal dynamics together with patient-level geometric structure for survival prediction.

</details>


### [162] [Position: Fairness Failure in Generative Models is an Evaluation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16974)
*Mariia Vladimirova,Jean-Yves Franceschi,Thibaut Issenhuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fairness Cards——一种最小化报告工件，旨在解决生成式模型中公平性评估缺乏可比性、可复现性和可操作性的问题，推动从临时性偏见检查转向标准化评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式模型在过去十年取得突破性进展，但其公平性问题（如加剧社会不平等、伤害边缘化群体）仍未得到充分关注且难以采取行动。核心问题在于公平性评估结果在论文间很少可比，也难以指导部署决策。因此需要诊断当前实践中的经验性和概念性失败模式，并推动评估范式转变。

Method: 本文属于立场论文（position paper），通过诊断当前生成式模型公平性评估中反复出现的经验性与概念性失败模式，提出Fairness Cards作为最小化报告工件，显式记录评估选择（提示族、反事实协议、指标和拒答处理），以实现可复现性、可比性和问责性。

Result: 提出了Fairness Cards这一标准化报告工件框架，囊括评估的关键决策维度；并给出向评估标准范式转变的额外建议，附有项目页面供进一步参考。

Conclusion: 文章主张公平性失败本质上是评估问题，需要通过标准化的、生成式模型特有的评估机制（如Fairness Cards）来取代临时性的偏见检查，从而实现公平性发现的跨论文可比性与对部署决策的可操作性。

Abstract: Despite groundbreaking advancements in generative models during the last decade, concerns about their lack of fairness, reinforcing societal inequalities and harming marginalized groups, remain under-addressed and difficult to act upon. This position paper argues that fairness failures in generative models, albeit driven by multiple factors, are ultimately stemming from an evaluation problem: fairness findings are rarely comparable across papers or actionable for deployment decisions. This paper diagnoses recurring empirical and conceptual failure modes in current practice and motivates a shift from ad-hoc bias checks to standardized, generative-specific evaluation. We propose Fairness Cards as a minimal reporting artifact that makes evaluation choices explicit (prompt families, counterfactual protocols, metrics, and refusal handling) enabling reproducibility, comparability, and accountability. We conclude with additional recommendations towards a paradigm shift in evaluation standards. Our project page can be found at https://mariiavladimirova.github.io/fairness-cards .

</details>


### [163] [Agents unlock new capabilities through Switching LoRA Adapters as a Tool (SLAaaT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17034)
*Kenneth Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种让代理在长轨迹执行过程中通过工具在多个专门化的LoRA适配器之间自主切换的方法，从而在不牺牲其他领域能力的前提下解锁新能力，相比单一适配器代理可降低最多18倍的能力损失，并显著优于子代理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练可以解锁新能力并提升专门任务的性能，但往往伴随着灾难性遗忘，损害其他领域的能力。在需要组合多种能力的长时间代理轨迹中，这种权衡成为瓶颈。作者拒绝接受这种权衡，目标是让代理既能获得专门化能力，又不丢失通用能力。

Method: 给代理提供一个工具，使其能够在执行轨迹中途（mid-trace）切换不同的专门化LoRA适配器。通过组合两个逻辑简单但需要专门化的合成编码任务来测试该方法的有效性。

Result: 该方法使模型能够解决之前无法解决的问题；模型能够自主切换适配器，并在一个任务上找到了超越人工启发式基线的策略；相比仅使用单一专门化适配器的代理，能力损失最多降低18倍；在任务能力和token使用方面均大幅优于派生子代理（spawning subagents）的方法。

Conclusion: 通过工具化的LoRA适配器切换，可以在不承受灾难性遗忘代价的情况下获得专门化能力，为长代理轨迹中组合多种能力提供了一种有效且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training can unlock new capabilities and improve performance on specialized tasks, but sometimes at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in other domains. This poses a problem in long agent trajectories that compose different capabilities. We reject this tradeoff by giving an agent a tool to switch between specialized LoRA adapters mid-trace. To test its effectiveness, we compose two synthetic coding tasks that are logically simple but require specialization. We find that this allows the model to solve problems it previously could not, that the model is able to switch autonomously (and find a new strategy that beats our human heuristic baseline on one task), and that this incurs an up to an 18x reduction in capability tax compared to an agent using only one specialized adapter. Our approach also substantially outperforms spawning subagents in both task capabilities and token usage.

</details>
