Conclusion: 统一关联扫描框架能够有效覆盖多种RL信用分配算法，并通过消除不必要的HBM中间往返实现可观加速，证明了基于Triton的专业化内核可在该领域显著优于通用编译方案。库已开源提供。

Abstract: We present rl-triton, an open-source library of high-performance GPU kernels for reinforcement learning credit assignment, implemented in Triton. The core contribution is a unified associative scan framework that recasts seven distinct RL estimation algorithms - Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE), V-Trace, Retrace($λ$), TD($λ$) returns, discounted returns, eligibility traces, and episodic prefix sums - as instances of a single first-order linear recurrence solved in $O(\log T)$ parallel steps. All algorithms share the same associative scan operator, with algorithm-specific fused Triton kernels constructing their recurrence coefficients on-chip. We verify the associative operator algebraically and define the treatment of terminated and truncated episodes explicitly. Benchmarks show a 1.6-5.70$\times$ full-call speedup over a vectorized torch.compile baseline in the massively parallel simulation regime (thousands of environments, short rollouts). The reported range covers all seven algorithms on both GPUs, both with and without per-step truncation handling. For most algorithms, speedups increase at longer sequence lengths, as the baseline requires more scan stages as $\log T$ grows, each adding an intermediate HBM round-trip. The library is available at https://github.com/simonsays1980/rl-triton.

</details>


### [200] [Elimination Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17646)
*Mian Huang,Xueqin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出消除几何（EG）框架，一种带类型、本征损失、审计导向的理论体系，用于研究局部最优对象何时能被共享部署规则实现，并系统区分架构阻碍与模型近似、泛化、实现误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除与压缩可能抹除预测、推理、控制或表征所需的关键区分，现有框架难以回答哪些区分被丢失、缺陷对声明任务是否可见，以及如何通过改变信息、架构、动作空间或部署域来修复。

Method: 综合几何、优化、信息论、统计学与机器学习工具，构建可积性、表征容许性、资源约束、观测重叠和公共部署等接口；分离局部可解性、全局可实现性与有限样本可证性；从原始目标推导本征缺陷。

Result: 对规则、奇异、组合与资源受限机制给出带明确前件和声明边界的正式结果；应用于稀疏模型选择、无分布预测、观测治疗策略、路由专家与检索系统、学习分数场；通过可复现的合成与真实数据研究展示证书如何指导架构修复。

Conclusion: 只有在部署契约、本征端点、竞争解释、信息和计算预算及验证规则全部固定后，才能将持久性能下限归因于架构；该框架将结构诊断与有限数据授权、机制匹配干预和独立验证联系起来。

Abstract: This monograph develops elimination geometry (EG), a typed, native-loss, audit-oriented framework for studying when locally optimal objects can be realized by a shared deployment rule. Elimination and compression may erase distinctions required by prediction, inference, control, or representation. EG asks which distinctions are lost, whether the induced defect is visible to the declared task, and whether changing information, architecture, action space, or deployment domain can repair it. EG separates local solvability, global realizability, and finite-sample certifiability. It derives native defects from the original objective and distinguishes architecture obstruction from model approximation, generalization, and implementation error. The monograph synthesizes tools from geometry, optimization, information theory, statistics, and machine learning into interfaces for integrability, representation admissibility, resource constraints, observational overlap, and common deployment. Formal results address regular, coordination, singular, compositional, and resource-limited mechanisms with explicit antecedents and claim boundaries. Applications include sparse model selection, distribution-free prediction, observational treatment policies, routed expert and retrieval systems, and learned score fields. Obstruction-Aware Learning and Inference links structural diagnosis to finite-data authorization, mechanism-matched intervention, and independent validation. Reproducible synthetic and real-data studies illustrate how certificates can guide architecture repair while recording failed gates and unresolved cases. The framework requires the deployment contract, native endpoint, competing explanations, information and compute budgets, and validation rule to be fixed before a persistent performance floor is attributed to architecture.

</details>


### [201] [Picard Proximal Monte Carlo for Parallel Bayesian Imaging with Score-Based Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17666)
*Deliang Wei,Evan Bell,Wenhan Guo,Yifan Chen,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PiX-MC，一种基于近端Langevin动力学和Picard迭代的时间并行后验采样框架，用于贝叶斯成像逆问题，在保持重建质量的同时大幅缩短墙钟时间，在稀疏视图CT上实现最高50倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯成像逆问题需要对高维后验分布进行采样，而现有的基于分数和扩散模型的贝叶斯先验采样过程本质上是顺序的且计算开销大，难以扩展到大规模成像应用。

Method: PiX-MC结合近端Langevin动力学（利用成像似然的高效近端算子）和Picard迭代（在离散节点间暴露并行性，支持多GPU实现），并发展出多块和退火变体以进一步提升可扩展性和采样性能。

Result: 在多样化的成像逆问题实验中，PiX-MC大幅减少墙钟时间同时保持重建质量。在512×512×80的稀疏视图CT问题上，退火多块PiX-MC使用8个GPU实现较标准Langevin采样器最高50倍加速。

Conclusion: PiX-MC在透明假设下建立了收敛保证，涵盖非对数凹后验、不完美学习的分数模型、多块实现和退火调度，证明了时间并行采样在贝叶斯成像逆问题中的有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian imaging inverse problems often require sampling from high-dimensional posterior distributions. While recent score-based and diffusion models provide expressive Bayesian priors, their sampling procedures remain inherently sequential and computationally expensive for large-scale imaging applications. We propose PiX-MC, a time-parallel posterior sampling framework based on proximal Langevin dynamics and Picard iteration. The proximal-likelihood formulation exploits the fact that many imaging likelihoods admit efficient, problem-specific proximal operators, while Picard refinement exposes parallelism across discretization nodes and naturally supports multi-GPU implementation. To further improve practical scalability and sampling performance, we develop multi-block and annealed variants of the proposed framework. We establish convergence guarantees under transparent assumptions, accommodating non-log-concave posteriors, imperfect learned score models, multi-block implementations, and annealing schedules. Experiments on a diverse collection of imaging inverse problems demonstrate that PiX-MC substantially reduces wall-clock time while preserving reconstruction quality. On a $512\times512\times80$ sparse-view computed tomography (CT) problem, annealed multi-block PiX-MC achieves up to a $50\times$ runtime speedup over the standard Langevin sampler using eight GPUs.

</details>


### [202] [Conformal Prediction for Molecular Properties under Label Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17678)
*Hyeonsu Lee,Juyeon Kim,Erkhembayar Jadamba,Seungjin Choi,Hyunjin Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对标签漂移（label shift）量身定制的保形预测框架，通过使用边缘标签概率比加权保形分数，在不重新训练模型的情况下生成统计严格的预测区间，从而在药物研发中实现稳健的不确定性量化，提升AI预测的可靠性和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现与开发成本高昂且失败率高，分子性质预测（溶解度、效价、毒性等）是其中的关键瓶颈。尽管AI加速了这一过程，但分布偏移（实验条件偏离训练数据）严重削弱了AI的可靠性；同时传统点预测仅提供单值估计，难以支撑高风险实验设计中的决策。

Method: 提出一个针对标签漂移定制的保形预测框架。核心思想是利用边缘标签概率比（marginal label probability ratios）对保形分数进行加权，从而在不进行模型重训练的情况下，生成具有统计严谨性的预测区间。

Result: 该方法在属性分布漂移的情况下仍能提供鲁棒的不确定性量化，直接应对AI在真实药物开发中应用所面临的最普遍障碍之一，增强了AI驱动预测的可信度。

Conclusion: 该框架超越了单纯准确性指标，提供了可操作的置信度度量，使预测建模与监管机构对透明度和不确定性报告的要求对齐，最终支持数十亿美元级开发管线中更可靠的决策。

Abstract: Drug discovery and development underpins healthcare but remains costly and failure-prone. A critical bottleneck lies in predicting molecular properties such as solubility, potency, and toxicity, which directly determine whether a candidate can advance from preclinical to clinical trials. Artificial Intelligence (AI) has accelerated this process, yet its reliability is often undermined by distribution shift, as experimental conditions frequently diverge from training data. In addition, conventional point predictions provide only single-value estimates, offering limited guidance for high-stakes experimental design. We address these challenges with a conformal prediction framework tailored to label shift. By weighting conformal scores using marginal label probability ratios, our method produces statistically rigorous prediction intervals without retraining. This enables robust uncertainty quantification even when property distributions drift, directly tackling one of the most pervasive obstacles to applying AI in real-world drug development. By moving beyond accuracy alone to provide actionable confidence measures, our approach enhances the trustworthiness of AI-driven predictions. This further aligns predictive modeling with regulatory demands for transparency and uncertainty reporting and ultimately supports more reliable decision-making in billion-dollar development pipelines.

</details>


### [203] [Cross-View Correspondence Is a Measurement Intervention: Two-Sided Validation for Agent Evaluation and Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17713)
*Zhen Zhang,Ahmad Hafez,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个被忽视的测量问题：在智能体评估和基于轨迹的学习中，跨视图输出的对应关系并非中性预处理，而是一种测量干预。作者提出了一套包含双端验证、全最优识别和不确定性传播的效度理论框架，并通过代码/SQL流水线及工具使用审计实验证明，忽略或不当设置对应关系会导致敏感性/不变性的人为制造、机制标签与符号化学习信用不可识别，最终建议跨视图对应必须被声明、验证并传播到不确定性中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估和基于迹线的学习方法通常将跨视图（跨变换）输出之间的对应关系视为中性预处理，认为其不影响下游结论。作者指出这种假设是错误的：对应关系的选择本质上是一种测量干预，其省略或不当配置会系统地扭曲评估结果，包括人为制造敏感性或不变性，甚至导致学习信用和机制标签无法识别。这让作者得以提出建立严格的效度理论的必要性。

Method: 作者提出了一个三组分效度框架：(1) 双端验证——同时验证干扰项（nuisance）移除和响应保留两个方面；(2) 全最优识别——在精确最优对应集合上进行下游结论的识别分析；(3) 效度建立后的不确定性传播。理论上，作者刻画了响应保留的干扰移除的线性可行性边界，计算了精确最优点集上的信号锐利范围，并给出一个无分布假设的证书，仅当所有精确最优在该信用坐标的非零符号上一致时才保留该坐标。实验上，作者在公开代码/SQL流水线和工具使用审计（含任务与种子不相交的复制）上验证框架。

Result: 在公开代码与SQL流水线上，两个确定性最优回溯在55.9%的1,586条非零轨迹对上对时间定位给出不一致结果；两个冻结的800次rollout工具使用审计（包括任务和种子不相交的复制）显示精确最优会在预期轮次信用上发生反转，而干净的公开快速启动子集未发现此问题。预注册的传输门在自然响应上失败；冻结的修正与持有对照实验表明，仅在良性示例上校准的映射会清除所有保留的有害响应，而双端验证能选出保留响应的替代方案。

Conclusion: 跨视图对应不能被当作无害的预处理步骤。在智能体评估或信用分配支持任何要点结论之前，必须对对应关系进行声明、验证，并将其不确定性传播到最终结论中。当前实践中的"中性对应"假设在真实数据上会产生可量化的、严重的推论扭曲。

Abstract: Agent evaluations and trace-based learning often compare outputs across transformed views through a post-response correspondence treated as neutral preprocessing. We show that this correspondence is a measurement intervention: omitting it can manufacture sensitivity, an over-aggressive map can manufacture invariance, and multiple optimal correspondences can leave mechanism labels and signed learning credit unidentified. We develop a validity theory and audit with three components: two-sided validation of nuisance removal and response preservation, all-optima identification of downstream conclusions, and uncertainty propagation after validity is established. We characterize the linear feasibility boundary for response-preserving nuisance removal, compute sharp ranges over exact-optimum correspondence sets, and give a distribution-free certificate that retains a credit coordinate only when all exact optima agree on its nonzero sign. Across public code and SQL pipelines, two deterministic optimal tracebacks disagree on temporal localization for 55.9% of 1,586 nonzero trajectory pairs; two frozen 800-rollout tool-use audits, including a task-and-seed-disjoint replication, expose exact-optimum reversals of intended turn-level credit, although a clean public quick-start subset shows none. A pre-registered transport gate failed on natural responses; frozen corrected and held-out controls then show that a map calibrated only on benign examples erases every retained harmful response, while two-sided validation selects response-preserving alternatives. Cross-view correspondence must therefore be declared, validated, and propagated into uncertainty before agent evaluation or credit assignment supports a point conclusion.

</details>


### [204] [MAGPIE-Net: Predicting short-duration heavy-rainfall events in station neighborhoods from multitemporal FY-4A AGRI observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17753)
*Xiang Lin,Yunying Li,Chengzhi Ye,Zitong Chen,Jing Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAGPIE-Net提出了一种面向站邻域事件、端到端可微的卫星到站点映射深度学习框架，将FY-4A AGRI多时相红外与水汽观测直接转化为0-3小时短时强降水站点级预警，在2023年暖季中国中东部独立测试中显著优于基于网格降水后处理的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习短时强降水预警方法大多先预测网格化降水，再通过后处理将其转化为站点邻域预警，导致"站点邻域事件目标"无法直接监督"卫星到站点"的学习路径，限制了预警性能。

Method: MAGPIE-Net包含三个关键组件：(1)地理自适应且可微的网格到站点映射模块，处理不规则站点位置；(2)对流初生特征提取与多尺度编码路径；(3)辅助网格降水诊断分支。通过站点邻域事件损失函数直接约束卫星表征及其到站点的映射，实现0-3小时事件预测。

Result: 在2023年暖季中国中东部独立测试中，40km/20mm h-1定义下0-1、1-2、2-3小时的CSI分别为0.371、0.304、0.238。跨事件检测率达65.1%，平均提前时间64.6分钟，优于最优网格基线(23.6%、18.3分钟)。在关键早期预警阶段（前1小时降水<1mm）检测率达51.9%，平均提前时间38.5分钟。

Conclusion: 事件导向的卫星到站点建模比网格降水建模更能有效将多时相静止卫星云与湿度观测转化为本地强降水预警，在检测率、提前时间和早期预警能力方面均具有显著优势。

Abstract: Short-duration heavy-rainfall warning determines whether 1 h rainfall will exceed a threshold within a target-station neighborhood over the next few hours. Multitemporal infrared and water-vapor observations from the Fengyun-4A Advanced Geostationary Radiation Imager (FY-4A AGRI) capture cloud-top cooling, moisture evolution, and cloud expansion before substantial surface rainfall develops. However, most deep-learning nowcasting methods convert these signals into local warnings by post-processing gridded precipitation predictions, preventing station-neighborhood event targets from directly supervising the satellite-to-station learning pathway. We propose MAGPIE-Net, which embeds a geographically adaptive, differentiable grid-to-station mapping in a pathway combining convection-initiation features, multiscale encoding, and auxiliary gridded precipitation diagnosis. Station-neighborhood event losses thereby constrain the satellite representation and its mapping to irregular station locations for 0-3 h event prediction. In independent 2023 warm-season tests over central and eastern China, critical success index (CSI) values under the primary 40 km/20 mm h-1 definition were 0.371, 0.304, and 0.238 at 0-1, 1-2, and 2-3 h. Across episodes, MAGPIE-Net achieved a detection rate of 65.1% and a mean lead time of 64.6 min, compared with 23.6% and 18.3 min for the best gridded-output baseline, and remained superior for smaller neighborhoods and the 50 mm h-1 threshold. During the critical early-warning stage, when antecedent 1 h rainfall within 40 km remained below 1 mm, MAGPIE-Net detected 51.9% of episodes with a mean lead time of 38.5 min. These results show that event-oriented satellite-to-station modeling converts multitemporal geostationary cloud and moisture observations into local heavy-rainfall warnings more effectively than gridded-precipitation modeling.

</details>


### [205] [Training-Free Human-in-the-Loop Anomaly Detection via Memory Bank Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17775)
*Ayusha Abbas,Saram Abbas,Kabita Adhikari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练、人在回路的异常检测框架，通过直接编辑PatchCore的记忆库（memory bank）来完成人工纠错，无需重训练、梯度或原始训练数据，仅凭十张"黄金样本"加操作者纠错即可显著提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测器在最需要部署的场景（如新投产的生产线上，训练数据稀缺且现场无机器学习工程师）反而最难部署。传统方法依赖重训练或梯度，但现场条件不允许；因此需要一个领域专家能在无重训练条件下直接修正检测器的框架。

Method: 提出训练免费的人在回路框架：领域专家通过直接编辑PatchCore的记忆库来纠正误报/漏报。误报纠正时，使用自校准新颖性门控（novelty gate）将待检图像的正常patch插入记忆库，该门控仅允许那些超过池中位数近邻距离的patch进入。采用保留评估协议（每类20个划分、Holm校正的Wilcoxon检验）避免纠错图像进入记忆库后因记忆化导致朴素评估虚高至AUROC 1.0。此外对比了被动与主动查询、匹配标签预算对照组的43%成本效益分析，以及缺陷记忆扩展实验。

Result: 在仅用十张黄金样本建立的记忆库基础上，操作者纠错平均填补了与完全训练库之间66%的中位差距（均值80%，三个类别甚至超过完全训练的基线）。15个MVTec AD类别中12个显著改善，无一受损。在已训练的记忆库上，收益空间较小且集中在正常外观覆盖不足的类别（toothbrush +0.10，metal nut +0.09，zipper +0.05，screw +0.05），仅grid类别有显著损害。被动与主动查询在统计上无显著差异；匹配标签预算对照组显示收益来自部署期标签生产，成本仅为穷举审查的43%；缺陷记忆扩展方案验证失败。

Conclusion: 免训练的记忆库编辑式人在回路纠错在小样本场景下极为有效：十个样本加人工纠错可超越数百个样本的训练库。但该方案依赖模拟反馈，真实专家试验（尤其小记忆库上误标成本最高的场景）仍是未来工作。

Abstract: Anomaly detectors are hardest to deploy exactly where training data is scarcest: a newly commissioned production line has a handful of verified "golden" samples and no machine-learning engineer on the factory floor. We present a training-free human-in-the-loop framework in which a domain expert corrects a PatchCore detector by direct memory bank editing: no retraining, no gradients, no original training data. A false-positive correction inserts the reviewed image's normal patches through a self-calibrating novelty gate admitting only those beyond the median pool-normal nearest-neighbour distance. From a bank built on only ten golden samples, operator corrections close a median 66% of the gap to an uncorrected fully trained bank (mean 80%, raised by three categories that overshoot parity), significantly improving 12 of 15 MVTec AD categories and harming none: ten samples plus corrections outperform hundreds of samples without them. On already-trained banks the headroom is smaller and concentrated where the bank undersamples normal appearance (gated: toothbrush +0.10, metal nut +0.09, zipper +0.05, screw +0.05), and no category except grid is significantly harmed. Evaluation uses a held-out protocol (20 splits per category, Holm-corrected Wilcoxon), because corrected images entering the bank inflate naive evaluation toward AUROC 1.0 by memorisation. Passive and active querying are statistically indistinguishable; a matched-label-budget control attributes gains to deployment-time label production at 43% of exhaustive-review cost; a defect-memory extension fails decisively. Feedback is simulated from ground truth; live expert trials, where mislabelling is costliest on small banks, remain future work.

</details>


### [206] [Debate Training Reduces Reward Hacking in RLAIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17776)
*Zachary Kenton,Lili Janzer,Rory Greig,Tian Huey Teh,Kirill Tyshchuk,Jonah Brown-Cohen,Harri Edwards,Senthooran Rajamanoharan,Noah Y. Siegel,Natasha Jaques,Rohin Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明使用辩论（debate）这一双人对抗博弈框架进行RL微调，相比RLAIF基线能显著减少奖励黑客行为。在数学任务上，使用较弱的冻结裁判模型时，辩论方法在整个训练过程中维持了裁判性能，恢复45%的性能差距，且优于RLAIF基线。实验进一步表明：更弱的裁判会导致更快的黑客攻击，但可通过增加辩论轮次补偿；辩论激励可覆盖提示错位；LLM裁判的RL训练/验证奖励差距小于可验证奖励；使用真实标签学习批判是可能的但较慢。同时，批评字数限制（最高150词）能有效平衡博弈防止裁判黑客，但牺牲了批评表达的清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励黑客（reward hacking）是RLAIF中的核心障碍：随着训练进行，策略会学会利用AI裁判的系统性错误来获得高奖励，导致任务性能下降，而这个问题在裁判比策略更弱时尤甚——这正是监督日益强大的AI系统最相关的场景。本文旨在探索辩论框架能否缓解这一问题。

Method: 采用生成器与批评者之间的双人对抗博弈，由较弱的LLM裁判仲裁。使用Gemini 2.5 Flash级策略，搭配冻结的更弱Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite裁判，在数学任务上对比单玩家RLAIF基线与辩论方法。额外进行四组消融实验：弱化裁判、增加辩论轮次、提示错位、以及使用可验证奖励的对比分析。

Result: 辩论方法在训练全程维持裁判性能，峰值验证准确率恢复45%的性能差距；基线RLAIF快速黑客攻击裁判。进一步发现：更弱裁判加速黑客但可被额外辩论轮次补偿；辩论激励覆盖提示错位；LLM裁判的RL训练/验证奖励差距小于可验证奖励；用真实标签学习批判可行但缓慢。批评字数限制（≤150词）有效平衡博弈避免裁判黑客，但限制批评表达清晰度。

Conclusion: 辩论是对抗奖励黑客的一个正向更新，尤其在裁判弱于策略这一关键场景。但多智能体训练的平衡至关重要：没有玩家约束，对抗训练风险会退化为批评者对裁判的黑客攻击。字数限制提供了可行的平衡手段，但引入表达清晰度与博弈平衡之间的权衡。

Abstract: We demonstrate that RL finetuning an LLM using debate, a two-player adversarial game between a generator and a critic adjudicated by a weaker LLM judge, reduces reward hacking compared to a reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF) baseline. Reward hacking is a central obstacle in RLAIF: as training progresses, the policy learns to exploit systematic errors in its AI judge, degrading task performance, a problem that worsens precisely when the judge is weaker than the policy, the setting most relevant to overseeing increasingly capable AI systems. We study mathematics tasks, where final-answer correctness is verifiable, allowing us to measure reward hacking dynamics. We train a Gemini~2.5 Flash-class policy with a frozen, weaker Gemini~2.5 Flash Lite judge, comparing a single-player RLAIF baseline against debate. While the baseline quickly hacks the judge, debate maintains judge performance throughout training, leading to a higher peak validation accuracy (45\% performance gap recovered) that persists through many RL steps. Additional experiments show that: 1) further weakening the judge leads to faster hacking, but this can be compensated by adding an additional debate round; 2) debate incentives override prompted misalignment; 3) RL using an LLM judge has a smaller train/validation reward gap than RL from verifiable rewards; 4) learning to critique to convince the judge using ground truth labels is possible but slow. Taken together, our results are a positive update on the feasibility of debate, while highlighting that balancing multi-agent training is critical: without player constraints, adversarial training risks defaulting to critic judge-hacking. We show that critique word limits (effective up to 150 words) successfully balance the game and avoid judge hacking, though this introduces a trade-off by restricting critic expressive clarity.

</details>


### [207] [Fourth-Moment Geometry of Rademacher Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17802)
*Peigan Gao,Jian Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了归一化Rademacher和的高阶矩如何依赖于四阶质量，通过尖锐的固定q矩包络与凸性阈值以下的分离论证，得到了全范围p≥4的高斯稳定性不等式，并确定了p≥5时尖锐的有限维L_p/L_4 Khintchine常数，平坦系数向量为极值元。同时证明了p=3时Jakimiuk猜想中的二次稳定性估计，并给出系数敏感的尾部界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Jakimiuk以及Barański、Murawski、Nayar和Oleszkiewicz提出的猜想，建立Rademacher和高阶矩与四阶质量之间的精确依赖关系，并确定L_p/L_4 Khintchine常数的精确值，此前这些常数和稳定性不等式仅在部分范围内已知。

Method: 结合两种互补论证：(1) 固定q的尖锐矩包络（sharp fixed-q moment envelope），用于主要范围；(2) 在凸性阈值以下的分离论证（separate argument below the convexity threshold），覆盖p≥4的完整范围。利用四阶质量作为关键框架参数刻画高阶矩行为，并采用Laplace变换形式导出系数敏感的尾部界。

Result: 证明了p≥4全范围的高斯稳定性不等式（线性in-q界）；确定了p≥5时尖锐的有限维L_p/L_4 Khintchine常数，极值元为平坦系数向量；证明了p=3时Jakimiuk猜想的二次稳定性估计。上述结果解决了所提出的猜想，且界保留了稀疏性和有效维度信息，可用于Rademacher随机投影和随机符号误差等应用。

Conclusion: 本文通过四阶质量框架完整刻画了Rademacher和高阶矩的行为，解决了多个公开猜想，为Rademacher和的矩估计、Khintchine常数和稳定性不等式提供了精确结果。值得注意的是，证明过程借助了ChatGPT 5.6 Sol的大量协助。

Abstract: Let $\varepsilon_1,\ldots,\varepsilon_n$ be independent Rademacher signs and let $a=(a_1,\ldots,a_n)\in\R^n$ satisfy the normalization below. For the normalized Rademacher sum, we determine how its higher moments depend on the fourth-order mass. Combining a sharp fixed-q moment envelope with a separate argument below the convexity threshold gives the Gaussian stability inequality for the full range $p\geq4$ of this linear-in-q bound. The same fourth-order framework determines the sharp finite dimensional $L_p/L_4$ Khintchine constant for $p\geq5$, with the flat coefficient vector as the extremizer. These results settle the conjectures of Jakimiuk and of Barański, Murawski, Nayar, and Oleszkiewicz stated below. We also prove Jakimiuk's conjectured quadratic stability estimate at $p=3$. The resulting bounds retain information about sparsity and effective dimension, with applications to Rademacher random projections and randomly signed errors; those applications are not developed further here. Their Laplace-transform form also gives coefficient-sensitive tail bounds. The proofs are discovered with substantial assistance from ChatGPT 5.6 Sol.

</details>


### [208] [An Empirical Study of Reward Specification and Benchmark Reliability in GRPO-based LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17804)
*Rubén Balbastre,Juan Manuel Orduña,Mariano Pérez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM未学习（unlearning）在生成式问答中的一个规范空白：当目标相关提示允许更广泛的回答而不泄露目标特定知识时，模型应在此层面回答而非泄露、逃避或拒绝。通过在受控的LoRA-GRPO RWKU设置中对比四种奖励设计，发现优化成功并不等同于行为上的未学习，多种评估指标可能得出不同结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM未学习评估通常只关注两个目标：抑制目标特定知识和保留非目标效用。但在生成式QA中，存在第三种行为未被规范化——对于目标邻近的提示，模型应当给出更广泛的回答而不泄露目标特定内容。这一定义不清的问题可能导致未学习效果不完整或不理想。

Method: 在受控的LoRA-GRPO RWKU设置中进行实验，设计了四种奖励方案：(1) 词法抑制（lexical suppression）、(2) 反拒绝塑形（anti-refusal shaping）、(3) 基于rubric的广泛回答、以及(4) 显式拒绝对比（explicit refusal contrast），并在有无SFT预热两种条件下进行比较。使用多种评估手段包括RWKU遗忘分数、留出集补全审计、终端训练回滚审计和训练动态分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，优化成功不等于行为上的未学习：RWKU遗忘分数、留出集补全审计、终端训练回滚审计和训练动态可能指向不同的结论。这些分歧可追溯至奖励黑客端点（reward-hacking endpoints）、GRPO中的策略支持限制（policy-support limits）、未能捕捉端点变化的基准探测（benchmark probes）、以及能够选择广泛主题回答且低语义泄露的奖励设计。

Conclusion: 未学习优化目标需要更仔细地设计以覆盖"广泛回答"这类第三种行为；单一评估指标不足以判断未学习是否真正成功，必须结合多种审计方法并关注奖励设计与优化机制之间的交互影响。

Abstract: Practical LLM unlearning is usually evaluated through two objectives: suppress target-specific knowledge and preserve non-target utility. In generative QA, this leaves a third behavior underspecified: when a target-adjacent prompt admits a broader answer without target-specific leakage, the model should answer at that level rather than leak, evade, or refuse. We study this specification problem in a controlled LoRA-GRPO RWKU setting, comparing four reward designs that span lexical suppression, anti-refusal shaping, rubric-based broad answering, and an explicit refusal contrast, with and without SFT warm-up. The experiments show that optimization success is not equivalent to behavioral unlearning: RWKU forget scores, held-out completion audits, terminal training-rollout audits, and training dynamics can point to different conclusions. We trace these disagreements to reward-hacking endpoints, policy-support limits in GRPO, benchmark probes that miss endpoint changes, and rewards that can select broad-topic answering with low semantic leakage during optimization.

</details>


### [209] [Efficient RLVR Scheduling via Graph-Structured Online Difficulty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17941)
*Zhizhao Liu,Zhiliang Tian,Xi Wang,Zhihua Wen,Yihang Xiong,Zhiquan Lai,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个即插即用的基于图的在线难度估计器（Graph-based Online Difficulty Estimator），通过共享邻近样本间的rollout反馈、利用Potts先验和贝叶斯状态级模型，持续更新样本难度估计，克服冷启动和反馈过时问题，无需额外探测开销，可集成到现有RLVR调度器中实现自适应探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR框架对不同难度的样本分配相同的探索预算非常低效：简单样本获得冗余rollout，困难但可学习的样本探索不足。现有自适应调度器依赖难度估计，但专用探测开销大、基于历史的方法存在冷启动和反馈过时问题，且忽略样本间关系。

Method: 1) 基于语义与推理相似性构建难度感知的样本图；2) 引入潜在难度状态并通过Potts先验促进相邻样本共享相同状态；3) 采用状态级Beta-Binomial模型聚合各状态的rollout结果；4) 使用在线均值场变分算法随新反馈持续更新潜在状态归属与状态级难度估计。该框架可插入样本选择和rollout分配调度器。

Result: 在多种基础模型、RL调度器和基准测试上的实验表明，该框架相较于现有方法取得了更好的性能。

Conclusion: 该图式在线难度估计器通过样本间反馈共享和持续更新，有效解决了冷启动和过时反馈问题，实现了无需额外探测的成本高效、难度自适应的RLVR探索，且具有良好的即插即用兼容性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models but relies on costly rollout exploration. Assigning the same exploration budget to samples with different difficulty levels is inefficient: easy samples may receive redundant rollouts, whereas difficult but learnable samples may receive too little exploration. Existing adaptive schedulers address this mismatch through curriculum-based sample selection or non-uniform rollout allocation based on estimated sample difficulty. However, obtaining reliable online difficulty estimates remains challenging: dedicated probing adds substantial generation overhead, whereas history-based estimators face a cold start with no initial observations and stale feedback, and typically ignore relations among samples. To address these limitations, we propose a plug-and-play graph-based online difficulty estimator that shares rollout feedback across related samples and continuously updates their difficulty estimates, mitigating cold start and staleness without dedicated probing. Specifically, we first construct a difficulty-aware sample graph based on semantic and reasoning similarities. Based on this graph, we introduce latent difficulty states and use a Potts prior to encourage neighboring samples to share the same state. We then employ a state-level Beta-Binomial model to aggregate the rollout outcomes associated with each state. Finally, we use an online mean-field variational algorithm to continuously update the latent-state assignments and state-level difficulty as new feedback arrives. Our framework can be integrated into sample-selection and rollout-allocation schedulers, enabling difficulty-adaptive exploration without dedicated probing. Experiments across multiple base models, RL schedulers, and benchmarks demonstrate that our framework achieves better performance.

</details>


### [210] [MotoSafety: Edge-AI with Learned Temporal Importance for Two-Wheeler Collision Risk Assessment Under Time Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17823)
*Sumit S. Shevtekar,Chandresh K. Maurya,Gourab Sil,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MotoSafety，一个基于"学习时序重要性"原理的边缘AI架构，用于在低中收入国家评估动力两轮车骑行者（PTW）在时间压力（Time Pressure）条件下的碰撞风险。研究基于包含129,000条多变量时间序列的大规模数据集（153次模拟骑行、51名参与者、64维特征），该架构在分类任务上达到94.97%准确率和99.33% ROC AUC，在预测任务上达到0.039 MSE，仅需1.15M参数和0.135ms延迟即可在低成本CPU硬件上边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低中收入国家中PTW骑行者面临严重的安全挑战，但关于时间压力等认知压力因素如何影响碰撞风险的研究十分有限，缺乏大规模的标注数据集和适用于边缘部署的轻量级风险评估模型。

Method: 构建了包含129,000+标注多变量时间序列的大规模数据集，涵盖153次模拟骑行、51名参与者在无/低/高三种时间压力条件下的64维特征（车辆动力学、控制输入、近距离、行为违规）。提出MotoSafety，一个基于Learned Temporal Importance原则的轻量级edge-AI架构，支持分类与预测任务，并利用真实时间压力作为归纳偏置来提升性能。

Result: 分类准确率94.97%、ROC AUC 99.33%，优于包括TimesNet和LLM4TS在内的10个基线；预测MSE 0.039、MAE 0.094（比Time-LLM和iTransformer误差低4.4倍）；仅1.15M参数和0.135ms延迟，适配低成本CPU边缘部署。使用真实TP作为归纳偏置使准确率从94.09%提至94.97%，预测TP达到94.82%；仅用21个IMU+GPS特征仍达93.91%准确率。在人类活动识别（97.66%）和临床（99.65%）领域展现良好迁移性。

Conclusion: MotoSafety作为轻量级框架有效推进了PTW碰撞风险评估，支持智能交通系统的安全系统方法（Safe System Approach），验证了边缘AI在有限认知压力条件下的可行性与跨领域迁移能力。

Abstract: Powered two-wheeler riders face critical safety challenges in low- and middle-income countries, yet limited studies exist on how cognitive stressors such as Time Pressure influence collision risk. To address this gap, we introduce a large-scale dataset of over 129,000 labeled multivariate time-series sequences from 153 simulator rides by 51 participants under No, Low, and High TP, capturing 64 features across vehicle dynamics, control inputs, proximity, and behavioral violations. Building on this dataset, we propose MotoSafety, a novel edge-AI architecture grounded in the Learned Temporal Importance principle. MotoSafety achieves 94.97% accuracy and 99.33% ROC AUC, outperforming ten baselines, including TimesNet and LLM4TS, and achieves 0.039 MSE and 0.094 MAE for forecasting (4.4x lower error than Time-LLM and iTransformer). With only 1.15M parameters and 0.135 ms latency, it is suitable for edge deployment on low-cost CPU hardware. Using ground truth TP as an inductive bias improves accuracy from 94.09% to 94.97%, while predicted TP achieves 94.82%. Using only 21 IMU+GPS features, it achieves 93.91% accuracy, indicating practical deployment. Beyond PTW safety, the architecture shows better transferability to human activity (97.66%) and clinical (99.65%) domains. This lightweight framework advances PTW collision risk assessment, supporting the Safe System Approach for Intelligent Transportation Systems.

</details>


### [211] [Leveraging Association Context Retrieval in Knowledge Edit- ing to Build White-Box Attacks on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17836)
*Roman Maksimov,Vladimir Aletov,Vladimir Solodkin,Dmitry Bylinkin,Daniil Medyakov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于知识编辑"定位-修改"范式的白盒攻击方法，通过关联知识检索将约束移除扩展到整个主题类别，提升大语言模型不安全行为诱导的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被赋予越来越多的自主性，研究如何诱导其不安全行为至关重要。现有攻击方法受限于预定义数据集，无法实现跨主题类别的广泛约束移除。

Method: 借鉴知识编辑领域的locate-then-edit方法，利用编辑后模型对编辑目标赋予异常高预测概率的特性，将关联知识从模型中检索出来并整合进编辑框架，使约束移除从特定提示扩展到整个主题类别。

Result: 在多种架构上的实验表明，该方法相比竞争方法具有更高的攻击有效性，同时不会对模型的一般性能造成严重损害。

Conclusion: 基于知识编辑思想的攻击方法能够更广泛、更有效地诱导LLM产生不安全行为，且对模型整体性能影响有限。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are granted increasing autonomy, it is essential to investigate methods that can induce unsafe behavior. We propose a novel white-box attack inspired by locate-then-edit approaches from the field of Knowledge Editing. Our choice is motivated by the observation that models edited with such schemes tend to assign unusually high prediction probabilities to the edit target, a property that is particularly advantageous when designing attacks. We modify the editing framework by incorporating as- sociative knowledge retrieved from the model, thereby extending constraint removal to an entire thematic category rather than being limited to prompts from a predefined dataset. Experiments with various archi- tectures demonstrate improved attack effectiveness over competing methods without dealing critical damage to general model performance.

</details>


### [212] [MoRAX: Mobility-based Representation Augmentation for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17848)
*Ya Wen,Jixuan Cai,Yulun Zhou,Alec Kirkley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoRAX是一个轻量级框架，通过人类移动数据中的功能结构来增强地理空间基础模型（GFM）的嵌入表示，在不牺牲覆盖范围和一致性的前提下，补充EO数据缺失的人类活动信息，实现跨城市零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型（GFMs）主要依赖地球观测（EO）数据，缺乏对人类活动信息的表征。人类移动数据能揭示区域间的功能与关系结构，但通常局限于单一城市，难以用于可迁移的城市表示学习。

Method: MoRAX采用教师-学生架构：教师模型观察移动数据，将人类移动流条件化的调制机制注入GFM嵌入；学生模型不接收移动数据作为输入，通过知识蒸馏学习接近教师的表现。整体框架轻量级，支持有或无移动数据情况下的零样本部署。

Result: 在跨越两个国家的四个目标城市中，观察移动数据的MoRAX教师模型在八项社会经济与环境预测任务上持续优于GFM和强基线；无需移动数据输入的学生模型在多数任务上接近教师的性能。跨国家迁移结果进一步证明了移动流条件化调制是有效的机制。

Conclusion: 通过移动流条件化调制为地理空间基础模型注入城市的人类维度信息，是一种通用且有效的方法，能够在保持GFM覆盖范围的同时增强其对城市功能结构的表征能力，实现跨城市、跨国家的可迁移部署。

Abstract: Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) are emerging as a powerful paradigm for learning semantically rich and geographically consistent visual and physical representations. However, their reliance on Earth-observation (EO) data leaves information about human activity largely underrepresented. Human mobility data reveals the functional and relational structure between regions that is missing from EO data, but is often limited only to the city where it is observed, making it challenging to use for transferable urban representation learning. We introduce MoRAX, a lightweight framework for augmenting geospatial embeddings with functional structure derived from human mobility. MoRAX preserves the coverage and consistency of a GFM while providing information about the functional connectivity among urban regions, permitting zero-shot deployment in unseen cities with or without available mobility data. Across four target cities spanning two countries, the MoRAX teacher model, which observes mobility, consistently outperforms GFMs and strong urban representation baselines in eight socioeconomic and environmental prediction tasks. Meanwhile, the student model, which never takes mobility data as input, approaches the teacher in performance on most tasks. Transfer results across countries further demonstrate that modulation conditioned on mobility flows provides a general mechanism for grounding geospatial foundations in the human dimension of cities.

</details>


### [213] [Efficient Resource Optimization for Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17849)
*Wei Wei,Xianhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向资源受限网络下分割联邦学习(SFL)的高效优化框架，联合优化模型分割与资源分配以最小化训练成本（时延与能量的加权和）。针对模型分割问题设计了多项式时间的全局最优算法；针对联合优化问题提出了具有(1+ε)近似保证的高效近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFL本质上涉及模型分割和资源分配的离散决策变量，构成棘手的混合整数问题。已有的优化方案要么是启发式的，要么计算效率低下，无法处理大规模用户群体，亟需高效且可扩展的优化框架。

Method: 首先将模型分割问题建模为混合整数问题并设计多项式时间全局最优算法；随后将联合模型分割与资源分配问题建模为二维主问题，提出具有(1+ε)近似保证的高效近似求解方法。

Result: 大量实验表明所提方法能够高效求解，实现最优的能量-时延权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为资源受限网络下的大规模SFL提供了计算高效且具有理论保证的优化方案，弥补了现有启发式方法和低效精确方法之间的空白。

Abstract: Split federated learning (SFL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for model training at the edge. However, SFL inherently involves discrete decision variables for model splitting and resource allocation, resulting in a challenging mixed-integer problem. Consequently, prior optimization schemes for SFL are either \textit{heuristic} or \textit{computationally inefficient}, which cannot handle large-scale user populations. To address this limitation, this work establishes an efficient optimization framework for SFL under resource-constrained networks. Our framework jointly optimizes model splitting and resource allocation to minimize training cost, which is defined as the weighted sum of latency and energy costs. We first study the model splitting problem and develop a polynomial-time algorithm that achieves the global optimum. Then, we extend the approach to the joint model splitting and resource allocation problem. In this case, we formulate it as a two-dimensional master problem and develop an efficient approximation method with a $(1+ε)$-approximation guarantee. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach provides efficient solutions to strike the optimal energy--latency tradeoff.

</details>


### [214] [Dynamic Compression in Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17896)
*Jyothish Pari,Ryan Bahlous-Boldi,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态压缩（dynamic compression）机制，允许循环模型选择性地重新访问过去的token并对固定大小的状态进行额外的循环更新，从而在有限的记忆容量下更有效地利用历史信息。实验表明，在需要从多个上下文函数中识别并复用特定函数的少样本任务场景中，动态压缩显著降低了所需的循环状态大小，且随着存储函数数量的增加扩展性更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环模型（如RNN、线性注意力等）通过单次因果扫描将整个序列压缩为固定大小的状态。由于压缩发生在模型知道未来如何利用信息之前，每个输入都必须在高保真度下被压缩，导致有限的状态需要在各种潜在未来需求之间进行妥协。这种单向压缩的固有限制使得模型难以在长上下文中有效保留所有可能相关的信息。

Method: 提出动态压缩机制：允许循环模型在序列处理过程中选择性地重新扫描过去的token，并通过额外的循环更新来修正已有的固定大小状态。低保真度信息可以保留在原始序列中，待其变得相关时再被重新访问和精炼。在一个受控设置中，模型先通过上下文学习多个函数，然后在同一序列中遇到多个少样本任务，每个任务需要识别并复用其中一个函数。通过对比单次通过模型与选择性重扫描模型，评估所需的状态大小与其扩展性。

Result: 动态压缩大幅减少了准确复用函数所需的循环状态大小，并且当存储的函数数量增加时，其性能扩展性比单次通过模型更有利。实验结果展示了计算-记忆之间的权衡：循环模型可以通过花费更多计算来重新访问历史，从而更有效地利用固定大小的状态。

Conclusion: 动态压缩展示了循环模型中计算与记忆之间的可调权衡关系，即通过增加计算开销（重新扫描历史）来弥补固定大小状态的内存限制，在长上下文和需要选择性信息复用的场景中具有显著优势。这为设计更高效的循环架构提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Recurrent models process long contexts efficiently by compressing their history into a fixed-size state, but modern architectures typically do so in a single causal pass over the sequence. Each input must therefore be compressed before the model knows how it will later be used, forcing a limited state to compromise across possible future demands. We introduce dynamic compression, which allows a recurrent model to selectively revisit past tokens and revise its fixed-size state through additional recurrent updates. The model need not preserve every part of the history at uniformly high fidelity in its recurrent state, because lower-fidelity information can be revisited from the retained raw sequence when it becomes relevant. We study this in a controlled setting where the model first learns multiple functions in-context and, later in the same sequence, encounters a series of few-shot tasks that each require it to identify and reuse one of those functions. A single-pass model must preserve every function at sufficient fidelity for any future task, whereas selective re-scanning allows the model to revisit and refine only the function currently needed. We find that dynamic compression substantially reduces the recurrent state required for accurate reuse and scales more favorably as the number of stored functions grows. These results demonstrate a computation--memory tradeoff in which recurrent models can spend more computation revisiting their history to make more effective use of a fixed-size state.

</details>


### [215] [Hybrid ML for Lightweight Pre-Route Delay Estimation in Open-Source IC Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17914)
*Marvin Castro Castro,Erick Carvajal Barboza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合决策树与线性回归的混合轻量级机器学习方法，用于改进OpenLane工具在布线前阶段的延迟估计精度，将误差降低80%，且模型比传统方法小300倍、快2倍、可解释性更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态时序分析（STA）是数字集成电路设计流程中的关键步骤，但在物理设计信息有限的情况下获得准确的延迟估计具有挑战性，需要一种更准确且轻量的延迟估计方法。

Method: 采用混合机器学习方法，将决策树与线性回归相结合，用于改进开源RTL-to-GDSII工具OpenLane生成的布线前延迟估计。

Result: 与OpenLane的估计相比，误差降低80%；即便不使用OpenLane特定参数，仍能实现71%的改进。模型比更复杂的机器学习模型小300倍以上、速度快2倍，且具备更高的可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法为传统延迟传播技术和复杂机器学习模型提供了一种替代方案，兼具准确性、轻量化和可解释性优势。

Abstract: Static Timing Analysis (STA) is a critical step in the design flow of digital integrated circuits, however, obtaining accurate delay estimations can represent a challenge when limited information regarding physical design is available. In response, this work presents a hybrid and light-weight machine learning (ML) based approach that combines a decision tree with linear regression to improve pre-routing delay estimations generated by the open-source RTL-to-GDSII tool OpenLane. The proposed model achieves an 80\% reduction in error compared to OpenLane's estimates, demonstrates a 71\% improvement even without utilizing OpenLane-specific parameters. Overall, this method offers an alternative to traditional delay propagation techniques and more complex machine learning models that is not only accurate, but is also over 300 times smaller, 2 times faster and offers a higher explainability.

</details>


### [216] [SIGMA: SHAP-Guided Implicit-Trajectory Generation for Metadata-Free LLM-Based AutoFE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17948)
*Xuan Zheng,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SIGMA框架，一种基于SHAP增强的隐式轨迹生成的元数据无关自动特征工程方法，通过SHAP值替代语义信息指导特征生成，并采用EXposed-feature Implicit Trajectory（EXIT）方法在常数上下文长度下实现可扩展的长程优化，显著降低重复率并提升特征利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的AutoFE方法存在两个关键挑战：(1) 语义元数据在许多实际场景中不可用，限制了方法的适用性；(2) 轨迹积累会导致上下文窗口溢出风险，而缺少轨迹则导致生成不稳定、陷入局部最优和高重复率问题。需要一种不依赖语义信息且能维持常数上下文的可扩展优化框架。

Method: SIGMA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 利用SHAP值提供任务感知信号来替代语义信息，指导分组特征生成；(2) 提出EXposed-feature Implicit Trajectory (EXIT)方法，通过在提示中暴露已生成的特征来隐式表示优化轨迹，避免显式轨迹积累导致的上下文溢出问题，同时保持生成过程的稳定性。

Result: 实验结果表明SIGMA在几乎恒定的提示长度下达到与SOTA LLM基线相当的性能；EXIT方法将生成特征的重复率从37.2%显著降至6.8%；SIGMA在仅使用平均5.4个特征的情况下匹配传统SOTA性能，展示了大幅的特征利用效率提升。

Conclusion: SIGMA证明了在无语义元数据的条件下，通过SHAP信号和隐式轨迹机制可以实现可扩展、常数上下文的高效AutoFE优化，解决了现有方法在适用性、可扩展性和稳定性方面的问题，同时大幅减少了生成特征的冗余。

Abstract: Recent research has leveraged Large Language Models (LLMs) to enhance Automated Feature Engineering (AutoFE) through semantic descriptions and trajectory-based prompting. However, there exist two challenges that limit their applicability and scalability in long-horizon optimization: (1) semantic metadata is unavailable in many practical settings, and (2) trajectory accumulation increases the risk of exceeding the context window, while without it, the generation process can become unstable, leading to becoming stuck in the local optima and a high duplicate rate of generated features. To this end, we propose a SHAP-enhanced Implicit-trajectory Generation for Metadata-free AutoFE (SIGMA), a scalable constant-context optimization framework. SIGMA leverages SHAP values to provide task-aware signals for guiding group feature generation instead of semantic information. In addition, we adopt an EXposed-feature Implicit Trajectory (EXIT) approach, where the exposed features in the prompt implicitly represent the trajectory. Empirical results demonstrate that SIGMA achieves performance comparable to the state-of-the-art (SOTA) LLM baselines with a nearly constant prompt length. Notably, EXIT significantly reduces the duplicate ratio of generated features from 37.2% to 6.8%. At the same time, SIGMA matches traditional SOTA performance with only 5.4 features on average, demonstrating substantial efficiency gains in feature utilization.

</details>


### [217] [An Omitted Mode Is a Rare Rule: The Sampling-Verification Danger Law in Continuous Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17956)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"代码世界模型"范式（LLM合成可执行世界模型，经典规划器搜索）在连续控制中的安全性问题。作者推导了该管线风险的精确因子：(1-r)^N，证明被接受的"模态盲"模型可被利用，并给出边界点处Lipschitz局部化预算的理论下界。通过真实LLM（GPT-5.x）合成实验和版本空间证书，证明接受仅认证样本一致性而非可辨识性，辨识性是仪器的可测属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Code World Model范式中，LLM合成世界模型被经典规划器搜索，模型通过复现采样转移即被接受。但研究者关心这种接受在连续控制中究竟证明了什么——即接受标准是否足以保证规划安全性，以及在何种条件下模型会被利用导致高风险。

Method: 定义管线危险为期望风险，精确分解其因子为(1-r)^N（N个独立门控rollout全部错过概率为r的关键事件的概率）；在三个混合仪器上验证被接受的模态盲模型可被利用；证明边界点处Lipschitz常数至多L、相差eta的模型在体积至少kappa((eta-eps)/L)^(d+m)的区域上分歧超过容差eps的局部化预算；使用GPT-5.x进行真实LLM合成实验（111次含模态draws中修复1D截断105次，2D区域0/156恢复规则）；通过版本空间证书和重评分1034个artifacts验证接受语义。

Result: 三个混合仪器上被接受的模态盲模型被利用，规划器被锁定在模态边界，遗憾接近整个可获得回报。GPT-5.x在50/56个仪器流块上每次尝试都精确（95% CI [0.781,0.960]），但2D区域无artifact恢复规则（0/156），八次定向干预未修复。在最大剂量下声明拟合20/20块成功，18/20块中每个样本一致圆都在容差内。重评分1034个artifacts证实接受仅认证样本一致性，门控可证明有效时仅覆盖约2%的被利用规划器查询。

Conclusion: Code World Model范式中的接受标准仅认证样本一致性而非模型正确性或安全性。辨识性（identifiability）是仪器的可测属性而非LLM合成能力的保证。定位（location）比形式（form）更难恢复，2D规则对当前LLM不可恢复。该工作为评估该范式在连续控制中的安全边界提供了理论框架和实证证据。

Abstract: In the Code World Model paradigm an LLM synthesizes an executable world model that a classical planner searches, and the model is accepted when it reproduces sampled transitions. We ask what that acceptance certifies in continuous control. We define the pipeline's danger as an expected risk and isolate its exact factor: the probability that N i.i.d. gate rollouts all miss a critical event of probability r is exactly (1-r)^N; an independent acceptance sample adds its budget to the exponent. On three hybrid instruments the accepted mode-blind model is exploited: the planner is pinned at the mode boundary at a regret of nearly the whole attainable return. We prove a localization budget, valid at boundary points: models with Lipschitz constant at most L differing by eta at a point disagree above tolerance eps on a region of volume at least kappa((eta-eps)/L)^(d+m); the discontinuous reset modes studied pay no such budget. With real LLM synthesis, GPT-5.x repairs an omitted 1D clamp in 105 of 111 mode-containing draws -- every attempt exact on 50 of 56 instrument-stream blocks (95% CI [0.781, 0.960]). On 2D regions no artifact recovers the rule (0/156); eight targeted interventions leave the failure in place, and positive controls locate it: a located rule is not induced, while given form and location the constants follow exactly. A version-space certificate proves identification is class-relative: at the widest dose the declared fit succeeds in 20/20 blocks and every sample-consistent circle is within tolerance in 18/20. We prove a class of entry rules exactly consistent with every sample yet harmless at play, so identifiability is a measurable property of the instrument. Re-scoring all 1034 artifacts on independent samples confirms acceptance certifies sample consistency and no more: where the gate is provably informative it covers about two percent of the exploited planner's queries.

</details>


### [218] [Understanding the Surprising Generalization Properties of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17957)
*Nour Shaheen,Junwei Ma,Alex Labach,Frank Hutter,Valentin Thomas,Anthony L. Caterini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现：仅在单个真实表格上进行自监督预训练的Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs)也能产生惊人的迁移能力。表格的实用性与下游任务无关，主要由特征数量而非实例数量决定。这一"任务中心"视角同样指导语料库设计：细粒度列级预处理优于数据集级过滤/去重。最后提出TFM的上下文泛化本质上是基于检索的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Tabular Foundation Models主要依赖大规模合成语料或海量真实数据集的训练，其数据需求高昂。作者旨在探索能否在极简设置（单个真实表格）下实现有效的预训练迁移，并理解TFM泛化的底层机制，为未来的模型和语料设计提供新框架。

Method: (1) 在单个真实表格上进行自监督预训练，评估其迁移性能；(2) 分析表格实用性与下游任务、特征数量、实例数量之间的相关性；(3) 在语料库规模上验证列级预处理vs数据集级过滤/去重的效果差异；(4) 提出基于检索的任务中心视角来解释TFM的上下文泛化机制。

Result: (1) 单个真实表格的自监督预训练即可产生强大的跨任务迁移能力；(2) 表格要么广泛有用要么普遍糟糕，且与下游任务无关，最强预测因子是特征数量而非实例数量；(3) 细粒度列级预处理能持续改善下游表现，而数据集级过滤或去重无改善；(4) 提出TFM的泛化本质上是检索驱动的，好模型擅长在上下文中识别并聚合相关示例。

Conclusion: 提出"任务中心"的表格预训练视角：任务的数量与质量是TFM预训练的核心。同时提出"基于检索"的泛化观点，为未来TFM的模型架构和语料库设计提供了新的理论框架与实践指导。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) increasingly rely on in-context learning, where a model receives labelled examples at inference time and predicts labels for new inputs without updating its weights. Existing TFMs are typically trained on either massive synthetic corpora or very large collections of real datasets. In contrast, we show that surprisingly strong transfer can emerge from self-supervised pre-training on just a single real table. In this setting, we also find that tables tend to be either broadly useful or broadly poor regardless of downstream prediction task, and that the strongest predictor of usefulness is the number of features rather than the number of instances. This leads to a task-centric interpretation of tabular pre-training: the number and the quality of tasks are essential for the pre-training of TFMs.
  We show that the same task-centric perspective can help corpus design at scale: fine-grained column-level pre-processing consistently improves downstream performance, while no improvements are observed when we filter or deduplicate at the dataset level.
  Finally, we offer a new perspective for how TFMs generalize: we believe that tabular in-context generalization is largely retrieval-based, and good models are those that learn to identify relevant examples in the provided context and aggregate them well. The mechanics of TFMs have been relatively understudied; our task-centric, retrieval-based perspective offers a new framework to guide future model and corpus design.

</details>


### [219] [Too Sure to Be Safe: Model Calibration for Reliable Log Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17965)
*Bin Li,Dongdong Wang,Siyang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Online log anomaly detection is critical for maintaining the reliability of large-scale computing systems. Although recent language model-based log anomaly detectors achieve strong detection performance, their confidence estimates remain poorly calibrated. We show that these detectors frequently assign excessive confidence to incorrect predictions, particularly for anomalous logs under severe class imbalance. Moreover, confidence on erroneous predictions remains persistently high even when conventional calibration metrics indicate good calibration, creating a critical reliability gap for operational monitoring systems. To address this issue, we propose Log Reconstruction and Distance (LoRD), a lightweight post-hoc calibration framework for reliable log anomaly detection. LoRD learns prediction-route-specific reliability models from latent representations of correctly classified validation samples and estimates prediction reliability through route-wise reconstruction distances. Based on the estimated reliability, LoRD selectively recalibrates high-risk predictions to suppress overconfident errors while preserving reliable predictions. Extensive experiments on four large-scale log benchmark datasets and multiple language model-based detectors demonstrate that LoRD consistently improves confidence reliability and substantially reduces overconfident anomaly-related errors without sacrificing anomaly detection performance.

</details>


### [220] [Evaluating and improving crop-yield forecasting methods during extreme drought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17971)
*Shrey Gupta,Yi Ming,George Mohler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了ML（非深度学习）与深度学习模型在极端干旱年份（2012年）美国县级玉米产量预测中的表现，通过样本加权和特征选择来应对训练集与测试集特征分布不一致及数据时空稀疏的问题。结果表明这些修改对ML模型有效，但对深度学习模型VITA改善甚微；VITA在有无修改的情况下均优于ML模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变率对粮食生产有重大影响，极端干旱事件（如2012年美国中西部玉米带干旱）会考验现有作物产量预测模型的极限。该预测问题存在训练与测试集特征分布的不相似性（极端干旱的气象条件超出历史观测范围），以及由缺测数据导致的时空稀疏性，这些挑战需要我们理解不同类别预测模型在这种分布偏移下的表现差异。

Method: 使用16个气象驱动因子作为预测变量，对县级玉米产量进行预测。比较了ML（非深度学习）与深度学习模型。针对训练与测试特征分布不一致以及时空稀疏性问题，引入了样本加权（sample weighting）和特征选择（feature selection）作为模型修改手段。

Result: 样本加权和特征选择修改对ML模型带来了性能提升；但对深度学习模型VITA几乎没有改善效果。无论是否经过修改，VITA均优于ML模型。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了训练与测试集特征分布不相似性对不同类型预测模型的影响差异：传统ML模型对这类修改更敏感并能从中获益，而深度学习模型VITA本身具有更强的鲁棒性但难以通过这些修改进一步提升。研究为极端事件下作物产量预测的模型选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The impact of climate variability on food production has led to the creation of various forecasting models that uses machine learning (ML), numerical weather predictors (NWP) or a hybrid of ML-NWP models to identify structural and physical relationships between meteorological drivers and crop growth, in order to predict crop yield. Droughts, for example the 2012 Midwestern US (Corn Belt) drought, are extreme events that affect crop production and test the limits of these forecasting models. Using 16 meteorological drivers as predictors, we compare ML (non-deep learning) and deep learning forecasting models to predict the county-level corn yield for the extreme drought year, 2012. This forecasting problem is characterized by a dissimilarity between the feature distributions of the training and test data, where the meteorological conditions of the extreme drought year fall outside the range of historically observed values. Additionally, the dataset consists of spatial and temporal irregularities where counties with missing yields introduce spatial sparsity and the use of only a subset of daily values per year introduce temporal sparsity. To overcome this, we use sample weighting and feature selection as modifications to improve our forecasting models. These modifications lead to an improvement for ML models; however, the deep learning model VITA shows little to no improvement. While VITA outperforms the ML models with or without modifications, our current study sheds light on the effect of dissimilarity between train and test feature distributions on forecasting models, compares deep learning versus non-deep learning models, and introduces modifications that are effective for non-deep learning models.

</details>


### [221] [Recirculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17981)
*Michael C. Mozer,Shoaib Ahmed Siddiqui,Danny Sawyer,Sunny Sanyal,Rosanne Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为"再循环"（recirculation）的推理时架构增强方法，通过引入特定形式的循环使现成的基础模型作为动态系统追踪信念状态，在不增加生成时延的前提下显著降低困惑度并提升生成与推理任务的准确性。自适应变体仅需轻量超参数调优即可在Gemma3系列上实现23%的困惑度降低和21%的GSM8k准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈Transformer的架构限制在于状态更新受限于模型深度，这使得模型难以在推理过程中持续追踪和更新内部信念状态。现有的深度循环技术（looping）成本高，而递归Transformer训练代价昂贵。作者旨在探索一种无需额外训练、不增加生成时延的推理时架构增强方案。

Method: 核心方法为"再循环"技术，它在推理时引入一种特定形式的循环结构，使模型能够作为动态系统运行并追踪信念状态。该方法区别于链式思维（保留给复杂推理而非基础状态追踪）、深度循环技术和递归Transformer训练。同时提出自适应变体，仅需轻量超参数调优且冻结原始模型权重，利用模型自身特性指导架构修改。

Result: 在Gemma3系列上，自适应再循环相比现成基线实现：困惑度降低23%，GSM8k准确率提升21%，并在其他下游任务上取得稳定的准确率提升。该方法在生成阶段几乎无额外延迟，但预填充阶段需要串行处理。

Conclusion: 训练免费的方法通过让模型自身指导架构修改来取得成功，提出了一条由训练网络属性驱动的架构演化路径，而非强制的任意设计选择。这一思路为推理时架构增强提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We describe an inference-time architectural enhancement for off-the-shelf foundation models that markedly reduces perplexity and boosts accuracy across generation and reasoning tasks. Our approach incurs essentially no additional latency during generation, though it requires serial processing in the prefill phase. Motivated by the fundamental limitation that state updates in feedforward transformers are bounded by model depth, our technique, recirculation, introduces a specific form of recurrence that allows the model to act as a dynamical system and track belief states. We distinguish this technique from chain-of-thought computation---which is better reserved for complex inferences rather than basic state tracking---as well as from popular depth-recurrence techniques (looping) and the costly training of recurrent transformers. We also propose and evaluate an adaptive variant of recirculation which requires only light tuning of hyperparameters while freezing the original model weights. Relative to the off-the-shelf baseline, adaptive recirculation achieves remarkable gains on the Gemma3 family, including a 23% reduction in perplexity on a suite of datasets, a 21% increase in accuracy on GSM8k, and reliable improvements in accuracy on other downstream tasks. Our training-free approach succeeds by leveraging the model itself to inform architectural modifications, suggesting a route to architectural evolution guided by a trained network's properties rather than forced, arbitrary design choices.

</details>


### [222] [Composing Flow-Matching Energies with Known Physics: Generation, OOD Detection, and Inversion on PDE Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18004)
*Yixuan Sun,Anirban Samaddar,Sandeep Madireddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流匹配模型（flow matching）与能量基模型（EBM）相结合的物理场概率建模方法。通过在流匹配框架中引入势诱导速度，显式获得所有传输时间上的标量能量函数，该能量函数的梯度恰好等于转换后的学习分数，并在群体最优时恢复边际负对数密度。该能量可用于能量校正的数据生成、OOD检测的评分函数，以及通过能量组合实现逆问题的后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理场的概率建模需要同时利用数据驱动先验和已知的物理结构（如控制方程）。能量基模型（EBM）因其能量可加性而天然适合注入物理信息，但EBM一直面临配分函数难以处理的训练和采样困难。作者希望克服这一瓶颈，获得一种既能保留EBM能量组合优势、又无需处理难解配分函数的建模方法。

Method: 方法核心是流匹配模型（flow matching）与势诱导（potential-induced）速度相结合。具体而言：(1) 在独立线性高斯插值上通过匹配回归目标（matching regression objective）获得时间依赖的能量函数，无需变分形式或额外MCMC步骤；(2) 采样时保留流ODE；(3) 利用显式能量函数实现三类应用：能量校正的数据生成、能量作为OOD检测的评分函数、能量组合式后验采样用于逆问题；(4) 在预测-校正（predictor-corrector）采样框架中，利用显式能量支持通用MCMC采样器，融入物理能量（如PDE残差）来提升OOD检测精度。

Result: 实验结果表明：预测-校正框架中结合显式能量的MCMC采样器相比流ODE基线降低了PDE残差和谱距离；利用数据能量与物理能量（PDE残差）作为互补机制提升了OOD检测准确率；能量组合与二次观测似然得到的后验能量可用于逆问题的推理时目标。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了流匹配模型与能量基模型之间的显式桥梁：训练后的流匹配模型天然携带可访问的标量能量函数，无需搭设额外训练框架即可获得EBM的诸多优势（能量修正采样、OOD检测、后验推理）。这一发现为在物理场建模中结合数据驱动与物理约束提供了一种实用且高效的范式。

Abstract: Probabilistic modeling of physical fields benefits from both a data-driven prior and known physical structure such as the governing equations. Energy-based models (EBMs) are a natural fit since energies compose additively, which enables augmenting physics information during inference. However, EBMs have been difficult to train and sample from due to the intractable partition function. We show in this work that flow matching models with a potential-induced velocity yield an explicit scalar energy at all transport times, whose gradient is exactly the converted learned score and which recovers the marginal negative log-density at the population optimum. The time-dependent energy functions are obtained purely from the matching regression objective on an independent linear Gaussian interpolation, without a variational form or additional MCMC steps, and the sampling retains the flow ODE. Access to the energy function from a trained model serves three roles: energy-corrected data generation, energy as a scoring function for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, and energy compositional posterior sampling for inverse problems. In particular, we show the explicit energy permits general MCMC samplers in the predictor-corrector sampling framework, reducing PDE residual and spectral distance compared to the flow ODE baseline. Furthermore, we demonstrate utilizing the data energy and physics-based energy (e.g., PDE residuals) as complementary mechanisms to improve detection accuracy for OOD tasks. In addition, we explore the connection to MCMC-based inference for inverse problems by composing the energy with a quadratic observational likelihood that yields a posterior energy, used as an explicitly chosen family of inference-time targets.

</details>


### [223] [Policy-Invariant Reward Shaping from LLM Feedback: A Framework for Hybrid RL Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18008)
*Christophe D. Hounwanou,John Emeka Eze,Yaé U. Gaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了"LLM规划器+RL控制器"的混合架构，将其建模为Goal-Augmented MDP，并证明当LLM的逐状态进度分数作为有界势函数使用时，即使LLM分数不准确，其塑造项仍能保持最优策略集不变。该保证比通用的LLM-as-reward方法更强，并在小规模MDP上通过四种势配置（包括20倍基奖励幅度的对抗性配置）进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型与强化学习结合的研究越来越多，但LLM生成奖励信号的理论性质往往被隐含处理而未明确阐述。作者希望填补这一理论空白，明确LLM奖励信号的有效性保证。

Method: 将混合LLM规划器与RL控制器架构形式化为Goal-Augmented Markov Decision Process（目标增强马尔可夫决策过程），将LLM的逐状态进度分数视为有界势函数（bounded potential function），从理论上证明该势函数塑造项在LLM分数不准确的情况下仍能保持最优策略集不变。然后在小型MDP上进行了四种势配置的数值实验，包括将分数放大到基础奖励20倍的对抗性配置。

Result: 理论证明表明，当LLM进度分数被用作有界势函数时，即使分数不准确，塑造项也不会改变最优策略集，这一保证强于一般LLM作为奖励方法所提供的保证。数值实验在四种势配置下验证了该结论，包括对抗性的20倍放大配置。

Conclusion: LLM分数若以有界势函数的形式注入RL，其不准确性不会破坏策略最优性，这为LLM+RL混合架构提供了理论保障，且该保证优于通用的LLM-as-reward方法。

Abstract: Combining large language models with reinforcement learning is increasingly explored, yet the theoretical status of LLM-derived reward signals is often left implicit. We formalize the hybrid LLM-planner and RL-controller architecture as a Goal-Augmented Markov Decision Process and show that when the LLM per-state progress score is used as a bounded potential function, the resulting shaping term preserves the optimal policy set even when the LLM scores are inaccurate. This guarantee is stronger than what general LLM-as-reward approaches provide. We verify the result numerically on a small MDP under four potential configurations, including an adversarial one scaled to twenty times the base reward magnitude.

</details>


### [224] [Revisiting WEASEL 2.0: Reproduction, Sensitivity, and an Adaptive Ensemble-Size Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18021)
*Cian Higgins,Gerard Carrigan,Pinar Sungu Isiacik,Georgiana Ifrim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现并分析了WEASEL 2.0时分类器，验证了其关键超参数设置（最大窗口大小和最大集成大小）的合理性问题。在114个UCR数据集上复现结果与原文一致（平均精度0.865，中位数0.928，Wilcoxon检验p=0.655）。通过扰动四项设计选择发现前三种（下游分类器、特征加权、窗口大小规则）均稳健，而第四种（最大集成大小规则）在长序列数据集上过度配置。为此提出自适应规则，根据序列长度和类别数设定最大集成大小，在保持精度（中位数变化0%）的同时，固定长度数据集上峰值记忆减少中位数37 MB（平均395 MB），拟合时间减少中位数0.4秒（平均4秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WEASEL 2.0中两个关键超参数（最大集成大小和最大窗口大小）由启发式阈值规则指定，但原论文未对这些阈值进行经验验证。本文旨在通过系统性复现和敏感性分析来验证这些设计选择的合理性，并针对发现的问题提出改进方案。

Method: 首先在114个UCR数据集上复现WEASEL 2.0，验证与原论文结果的一致性（Wilcoxon符号秩检验）。随后对四项设计选择进行敏感性测试：下游分类器、特征加权缺失、最大窗口大小规则、最大集成大小规则。对不稳健的集成大小规则提出自适应规则，根据序列长度和类别数动态设置最大集成大小。

Result: 复现结果与原论文一致（平均精度0.865 vs 发表值，p=0.655）。敏感性分析显示前三项设计选择稳健，但最大集成大小规则在长序列数据集上过度配置。采用自适应规则后，固定长度数据集上峰值记忆减少中位数37 MB（平均395 MB），拟合时间减少中位数0.4秒（平均4秒），精度中位数变化为0%（平均-0.11%），节省主要集中在长序列数据集。

Conclusion: WEASEL 2.0主体设计（分类器、特征加权、窗口大小规则）是稳健的，但其最大集成大小规则在长序列数据集上存在计算资源浪费。提出的自适应规则在保持精度的前提下显著降低内存和时间的开销，为原算法的超参数设定提供了经验依据和更优替代方案。

Abstract: WEASEL 2.0 is a dictionary-based time series classifier that combines dilated sliding windows with a randomised hyperparameter ensemble and a fixed-size dense feature representation. Two of its hyperparameter choices, the maximum ensemble size and the maximum window size, are specified by simple thresholding rules whose chosen thresholds are not empirically justified in the original paper. In this work we reproduce WEASEL 2.0 on 114 UCR datasets, achieving a mean accuracy of 0.865 and median of 0.928, closely matching the published values (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p = 0.655). We then test the sensitivity of four design choices: the downstream classifier, the absence of feature weighting, the maximum window-size rule, and the maximum ensemble-size rule. The first three are robust to perturbation. The fourth is over-provisioned for long-series datasets, motivating an adaptive rule that sets the maximum ensemble size from series length and number of classes. Evaluated on fixed-length datasets, the adaptive rule reduces peak fit memory by a median of 37 MB (mean 395 MB) and fit time by a median of 0.4 s (mean 4 s), with a median accuracy change of 0% (mean -0.11%). Memory and time savings concentrate on long-series datasets where the original rule allocates the largest ensemble size.

</details>


### [225] [Why GPT-Style Models Do Not Directly Transfer to Symbolic Music: Compression in the Wrong Coordinate System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18025)
*Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出符号音乐分词的本质不是寻找更大的可复用音乐组合，而是构建一个"可预测压缩且关系无损"的坐标系，并据此定义了Fact-Token边界和Token-State边界，通过受控实验验证了有效的坐标系构建能提升预测压缩性，而固定的关系投影会限制上下文建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT风格模型通过离散token表示语言取得了强劲性能，这促使符号音乐分词将和弦、动机、乐句等重复音乐结构视为可复用单元。然而作者指出，分词的优势并非来自可复用组合本身，而是来自压缩——有效的压缩需要一种坐标系，使重复出现的规律形成稳定可预测的条件分布。因此关键问题不是找到更大的音乐组合，而是发现使音乐事实可预测压缩的坐标系。

Method: 作者构建了Effectiveness-Losslessness Framework，将分词定义为构建"预测有效且关系无损"的坐标系。具体包括两个原则：(1) Predictive Effectiveness Principle定义Fact-Token边界——通过解耦和解套构造坐标接口以暴露预测规律；(2) Relational Losslessness Principle定义Token-State边界——分词在上下文相关关系被固定之前停止，将关系的计算留给模型状态。最后通过受控符号音乐实验验证这些边界。

Result: 受控实验表明：有效的坐标系构建能改善预测可压缩性，而固定的关系投影会约束上下文建模；仅序列压缩并不保证预测压缩，而保留上下文自由度能使高阶音乐组织无需显式结构标签即可涌现。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了GPT风格模型无法跨模态直接迁移的原因：架构可以迁移，但分词接口不能。分词必须在发现有效表示的同时，保留使上下文结构得以涌现的关系自由度。

Abstract: GPT-style models achieve strong performance by representing language with finite vocabularies of reusable discrete tokens. This success has motivated symbolic music tokenizations to treat recurring musical structures, such as chords, motifs, and phrases, as reusable units analogous to linguistic tokens. However, tokenization derives its advantage not from reusable combinations alone, but from compression: effective compression requires coordinates in which recurring regularities form stable and predictable conditional distributions. The key problem is therefore not to find larger musical combinations, but to discover the coordinate system in which musical facts become predictively compressible. We formulate the Effectiveness--Losslessness Framework and define tokenization as the construction of a predictively effective and relationally lossless coordinate system. The Predictive Effectiveness Principle defines the Fact--Token Boundary: decoupling and denesting construct coordinate interfaces that expose predictive regularities. The Relational Losslessness Principle defines the Token--State Boundary: tokenization stops before context-dependent relations are fixed, leaving their computation to model states. Controlled symbolic-music experiments validate these boundaries. Effective coordinate construction improves predictive compressibility, while fixed relational projections constrain contextual modeling. Sequence compaction alone does not guarantee predictive compression, while preserving contextual freedom allows higher-order musical organization to emerge without explicit structural labels. These results reveal why GPT-style models do not transfer directly across modalities: architectures transfer, but tokenization interfaces do not. Tokenization must discover effective representations while preserving the relational freedom from which contextual structure can emerge.

</details>


### [226] [Optimize Your Sampling: Tuned Diffusion Sampling with Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18040)
*Travis Zhang,Christian Belardi,Justin Lovelace,Jin Peng Zhou,Saebyeol Shin,Carla P. Gomes,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OYS将扩散模型采样时间步选择视为黑箱优化问题，直接使用贝叶斯优化优化目标指标，无需额外训练即可超越默认调度和Align Your Steps等方法，5步调度可保留50步调度89%-94%的质量同时降低10倍推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型采样通常需要进行多次前向传播，生成计算成本高。现有工作多聚焦于高效求解器和采样器，对采样时间步选择的关注不足。已有的时间步优化方法（如Align Your Steps）优化的是理论推导的代理指标而非实际质量指标，与真实目标存在偏差。

Method: 提出OYS（Optimizing Your Sampling）框架，将时间步选择视为黑箱优化问题，利用贝叶斯优化直接优化目标质量指标（而非代理指标）。该方法无需额外训练，可适用于蒸馏模型，并能同时改善简单和复杂采样器（如Euler、DPM-Solver++）。

Result: OYS在文本到图像生成中超越默认调度和Align Your Steps，在图像修复等任务上优于默认调度，定量和人工评估均验证其有效性。5步OYS调度保留50步调度89%-94%的质量，同时推理成本降低10倍。

Conclusion: 直接以目标指标为优化对象的时间步选择方法（贝叶斯优化）比优化理论代理指标更有效，OYS提供了一种通用、无需训练、成本效益高的扩散模型采样加速方案。

Abstract: Sampling from a diffusion model typically requires many forward passes through a large neural network, making generation computationally expensive. While much work has focused on efficient solvers and samplers, comparatively little attention has been paid to selecting the sampling timesteps themselves. A recent line of work optimizes theoretically derived surrogates for sample quality rather than the quality metric itself. We propose Optimizing Your Sampling (OYS), which instead treats timestep selection as a black-box optimization problem, optimizing the target metric directly with Bayesian optimization. OYS outperforms both the default schedules and those of Align Your Steps on text-to-image generation, and improves over the default schedules on inpainting and other image tasks, in both quantitative and human evaluations. OYS requires no additional training, is applicable even to distilled models, and improves both simple and sophisticated samplers such as Euler and DPM-Solver++. A 5-step OYS schedule retains 89%-94% of the quality of a 50-step schedule while reducing inference cost by 10x.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Fairness--Stability Trade-offs in Many-to-One Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17295)
*Genjie Qin*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了多对一匹配市场中（允许可转移效用）公司侧公平性与联盟稳定性之间的权衡。作者将最大可支撑核因子刻画为一个瓶颈融资问题，提出多项式时间线性规划与敏感性公式，并设计了最大边轮算法及互顶安全选择，证明所有安全执行都具有EF1公平性，并给出了稳定性与福利的下界，同时刻画了EF1-核极小极大前沿的有限公司上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多对一匹配市场（含可转移支付）中，公司侧的公平性与联盟稳定性（核稳定性）之间存在内在张力。如何系统地量化这一权衡、设计兼顾两者的匹配算法，是该领域的关键开放问题。

Method: 将固定匹配下的最大支持核因子转化为瓶颈融资问题α(X)=1/Φ(X)，其中Φ(X)=min_{z≥0} max_i R_i(X,z)，据此构造多项式时间线性规划与局部敏感性公式。进一步提出最大边轮算法与互顶安全选择类算法，并延伸至EFX+公平性与容量受限市场。

Result: 每个安全执行均满足EF1；当t=δ(A)为最小正边质量时，保证α(X)≥max{t, 1/[m-(m-1)t]}且SW(X)/OPT≥t+(1-t)/m。给出了EF1-核极小极大前沿的有限公司上下界：两家公司精确、三家公司（δ≤1/2时）精确；公司数趋于无穷时，无尺度稳定性速率为δ。

Conclusion: 本文通过融资框架统一刻画了公平性与稳定性权衡，提供了多项式时间算法与紧的稳定性速率刻画，为多对一匹配市场中的公平-稳定权衡提供了系统性理论工具。

Abstract: We study the trade-off between firm-side fairness and coalition stability in many-to-one matching markets with transferable payments. For a fixed matching $X$, we characterize the largest supportable core factor by a bottleneck financing problem: $α(X)=1/Φ(X)$, where $Φ(X)=\min_{z\ge0}\max_i R_i(X,z)$. This yields a polynomial-time linear program and local sensitivity formulas for one-worker reallocations. We then develop a maximum-edge round algorithm and a broader class of mutual-top safe choices. Every safe execution is EF1 and, with $t=δ(A)$ denoting the minimum positive-edge quality, guarantees $α(X)\ge\max\{t,1/[m-(m-1)t]\}$ and $SW(X)/OPT\geq t+(1-t)/m$. These bounds give finite-firm lower and upper bounds for the EF1--core minimax frontier, with exact results for two firms and for three firms when $δ\le1/2$; as the number of firms grows, the tight scale-free stability rate is $δ$. We also extend the financing formulation to stronger $EFX^+$ fairness and capacity-constrained markets.

</details>


### [228] [Self-Bounding Regret Matching+ in Potential Games and Product-Simplex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17417)
*Pahan Dewasurendra,Subhashini Jayawardhana*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文为Regret Matching+ (RM+)算法建立了精确的一步守恒律，证明其regret受集中时间变异控制，并基于此推导出四个主要结果：RM+在交替博弈、任意平滑（可能非凹）单纯形目标上以O(ε⁻²)迭代找到ε-KKT点，循环块RM+从任意初始化在同复杂度下收敛，并解决了一个关于有限精确势博弈中均匀有界regret的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RM+是大型博弈求解中核心的参数无关、尺度不变算法，但其唯一的一般个体regret保证仅以√T增长，导致在平滑目标上只能获得O(ε⁻⁴)（零初始化时为O(ε⁻⁸)）的次优收敛率。作者希望建立更紧的分析工具来改进RM+的理论保证，并解决势博弈中regret均匀有界的开放问题。

Method: 作者给出RM+的精确一步守恒律：前向效用增益同时支付平方状态运动和regret状态范数增长；状态范数增长至多为(m-1)倍前向增益（对m个动作），系数是紧的。基于此守恒律，作者推导出regret由集中时间变异控制，从而建立四个主要定理，并通过自界证明控制低状态块的有限目标损失，同时自限定每个块状态和总平方路径长度。证明覆盖零状态、尖锐性、公共震荡平稳性和鲁棒增益主导性。

Result: RM+在任何效用路径上的regret由集中时间变异控制；在有限精确势博弈的交替play下regret均匀有界且平方激活间隙可求和；认证惰性和普通循环play均达到ε⁻²指数；在任意平滑（可能非凹）单纯形目标上RM+以O(ε⁻²)迭代找到ε-KKT点；对任意乘积单纯形上的平滑目标，循环块RM+从任意初始化达到相同的O(ε⁻²)指数，带显式轨迹相关常数。Oracle归一化诊断在图形势博弈和稠密非凸目标上对比了RM+与预测及平滑外梯度变体。

Conclusion: 通过精确守恒律这一新分析工具，RM+的收敛率从O(ε⁻⁴)大幅提升到O(ε⁻²)，并解决了势博弈中regret均匀有界的开放问题。守恒律的参数独立性和紧系数表明RM+的固有结构比此前认知更加强大，其理论保证可与更复杂的预测或平滑外梯度方法竞争。

Abstract: Regret matching+ (RM+) is parameter free, scale invariant, and central to large game solving, but its only general individual-regret guarantee grows as $\sqrt{T}$. A recent ICLR result used this envelope to prove that RM+ reaches an $ε$-stationary point of a smooth objective over a product of simplices in $O(ε^{-4})$ iterations, or $O(ε^{-8})$ from the standard zero initialization. We give an exact one-step conservation law for RM+. It states that forward utility gain pays for both squared state motion and growth of the regret-state norm. Norm growth is at most $\sqrt{m-1}$ times forward gain for $m$ actions, and the coefficient is sharp. This yields four results for unmodified RM+. Its regret on any utility path is controlled by centered temporal variation. Its regret is uniformly bounded under alternating play in every finite exact potential game, resolving an open question and making squared activation gaps summable. Both certified lazy and ordinary cyclic play attain an $ε^{-2}$ exponent. On any smooth, possibly nonconcave simplex objective, RM+ finds an $ε$-KKT point in $O(ε^{-2})$ iterations. Most broadly, for a smooth objective over an arbitrary product of simplices, cyclic block RM+ attains the same $O(ε^{-2})$ exponent from arbitrary initialization, with an explicit trajectory-dependent constant. The proof controls the finite objective loss caused by low-state blocks and then self-bounds every block state and the total squared path length. Complete proofs cover zero states, sharpness, common-profile stationarity, and robust gain dominance. Oracle-normalized diagnostics compare RM+ with predictive and smooth extra-gradient variants on graphical potential games and dense nonconvex objectives.

</details>


### [229] [An improved bound for the randomized metric distortion problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17863)
*Fabian Frank*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Mixed Integrated Veto (MIV)的随机社会选择规则，其度量失真度为5/2，优于此前最好的2.75271上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进随机社会选择规则在度量空间中的失真度上界，突破此前最优的2.75271。

Method: MIV是Maximal Lotteries与Integrated Veto的等权重混合。Integrated Veto基于Kizilkaya和Kempe的Simultaneous Veto过程，但与传统返回存活最久候选人的方式不同，它根据候选人在整个过程中的平均得分来分配概率。

Result: MIV实现了5/2即2.5的度量失真度，将此前的最优上界从2.75271改进至2.5。

Conclusion: 通过将Maximal Lotteries与一种新的基于平均得分分配概率的Integrated Veto规则等权重混合，成功达到了更优的度量失真度上界5/2。

Abstract: We propose a randomized social choice rule called Mixed Integrated Veto (MIV) with metric distortion of $5/2$, improving the previous best upper bound of $2.75271$. MIV is the equal mixture of Maximal Lotteries and Integrated Veto, a new rule built on the Simultaneous Veto process of Kizilkaya and Kempe. Rather than returning the candidate surviving longest, Integrated Veto assigns each candidate probability proportional to its average score over the whole process.

</details>


### [230] [Planning Against Learning in Rank-1 Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18067)
*William Overman*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在秩为1的双矩阵博弈（rank(A+B)=1）中，针对学习动力学（Replicator Dynamics）进行战略规划的计算复杂度。结果表明，即使Nash均衡可以在多项式时间内计算，但对抗学习者的最优规划问题在固定加性常数误差内仍是NP难的，除非P=NP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明，在零和博弈中针对Replicator Dynamics优化是可行的，但在一般和博弈中可能很难。作者希望探索零和博弈之外的第一个结构化的博弈类别——秩1双矩阵博弈，以理解均衡计算的可解性与对抗学习动力学规划之间的边界。

Method: 作者针对满足rank(A+B)=1的双矩阵博弈，研究优化者面对遵循Replicator Dynamics（连续时间版本的Multiplicative Weights Update）的学习者时的最优策略规划问题。他们分析了当学习者从均匀状态出发、优化者仅使用常数策略时的复杂度，并给出了若干可解特殊情形下的结构化特征刻画。

Result: 主要结果表明：除非P=NP，否则即使rank(A+B)=1、学习者从均匀状态出发且优化者限制为常数策略，在固定加性常数内近似优化者的最优连续时间奖励仍是NP难的。该困难性在有界支付矩阵和多项式有界时间范围内依然成立。同时，作者识别并刻画了若干可解的特殊情形。

Conclusion: 秩1博弈已经足以证明：有效的均衡计算与对抗学习者的战略规划之间存在根本性分离。这一结果揭示了均衡可计算性并不蕴含对动力学规划的可处理性，为多智能体学习环境中的策略规划复杂度提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Learning algorithms are often used to make decisions in repeated multi-agent environments. When another player understands how a learner adapts from past experience, that player can plan strategically across rounds to influence the learner's future behavior. Recent work shows that optimizing against Replicator Dynamics, the continuous-time analogue of Multiplicative Weights Update, is tractable in zero-sum games but can be hard in unrestricted general-sum games. We study the first structured class beyond zero sum: bimatrix games satisfying $\text{rank}(A+B)=1$, for which Nash equilibria can be computed in polynomial time.
  Our main result shows that this equilibrium tractability does not extend to planning against learning dynamics. Unless $\mathsf{P}=\mathsf{NP}$, approximating the optimizer's optimal continuous-time reward within a fixed additive constant is NP-hard even when $\text{rank}(A+B)=1$, the learner starts from the uniform state, and the optimizer is restricted to constant strategies. The hardness persists for bounded payoff matrices and polynomially bounded horizons. We complement this result with structural characterizations of several tractable special cases. Thus rank-one games already separate efficient equilibrium computation from strategic planning against a learning opponent.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Reduced-Order Physics-Informed Neural Network with Adaptive Basis Refinement for Structural Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17131)
*Rui Zhang,Konstantinos Vlachas,Eleni Chatzi*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种具有自适应基精细化的降阶物理信息神经网络（RO-PINN）框架，用于在已知和不完整物理条件下进行结构识别，通过将降阶控制方程嵌入PINN损失函数并在训练过程中自适应更新投影基，解决了高维度和模型形式误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs直接应用于结构动力学受限于高系统维度和因物理不完整导致的模型形式误差；现有PINN-ROM耦合方法依赖固定降阶子空间、针对正向模拟或假设完整物理，无法在参数变异性或不完整系统知识下进行逆向识别。

Method: 通过投影将降阶控制方程直接嵌入PINN损失函数，在低维潜在空间中进行学习；提出自适应方案在训练过程中更新投影基，使潜在空间随演变的结构参数或学习的残余恢复力逐步重新对齐，减少基不匹配误差及其对推断残余力的影响。

Result: 在带非线性迟滞支撑的四层钢框架上进行验证，结果表明：参数识别精度与贝叶斯模型更新相当或更优且计算成本更低；能够在物理不完整情况下恢复未建模的非线性恢复力；可在同一框架内联合识别残余恢复力和结构参数。

Conclusion: RO-PINN将降阶建模、自适应基精细化和物理信息学习整合于统一框架中，为结构识别提供了统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a flexible framework for solving forward and inverse problems. However, their direct application to structural dynamics remains limited by high system dimensionality and model-form errors arising from incomplete physics. Reduced-order models (ROMs) can alleviate the dimensionality bottleneck, yet existing PINN-ROM couplings typically rely on fixed reduced subspaces, target forward simulations, or assume complete physics, restricting their use for inverse identification under parametric variability or incomplete system knowledge. To address these limitations, this work proposes a Reduced-Order Physics-Informed Neural Network (RO-PINN) framework with adaptive basis refinement for structural identification under known and incomplete physics. Via projection, reduced governing equations are embedded directly into the PINN loss, facilitating learning in a low-dimensional latent space. An adaptive scheme updates the projection basis during training so that the latent space is progressively realigned with evolving structural parameters or learned residual restoring forces. This realignment reduces basis-mismatch errors and limits their influence on the inferred residual force. The method is validated on a four-story steel frame with nonlinear hysteretic braces under sparse and noisy measurements. Results show parameter identification comparable to or more accurate than Bayesian model updating with lower computational cost in the considered cases, recovery of unmodeled nonlinear restoring forces under incomplete physics, and joint identification of residual restoring forces and structural parameters within the same framework. Overall, RO-PINN provides a unified framework for structural identification by integrating reduced-order modeling, adaptive basis refinement, and physics-informed learning within a single formulation.

</details>


### [232] [Modeling the Hydrodynamics in the Oslofjord using ADCIRC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17140)
*Matthew Scarborough,Kai Håkon Christensen,Albert Cerrone,Nils Melsom Kristensen,Eirik Valseth*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文为奥斯陆峡湾的水动力模拟开发了一种新的非结构化计算网格（基于OceanMesh2D和全球水深/海岸线数据），并构建了ADCIRC模型。通过四个不同时期验证，模型在大幅降低计算成本的同时达到与挪威现役预报模型相当的性能。研究还比较了三种潮汐分潮组合的驱动效果，发现增加分潮略改善结果但受限于小域边界条件，改用全球ADCIRC模型输出的水面高度强迫模型以改善极端天气事件下的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于挪威峡湾水动力预报的模型计算成本高昂，需要开发一个计算效率更高且精度相当的替代方案。同时，需要系统评估不同潮汐强迫组合对峡湾模型的影响，并探索改进边界条件的方法，以提升极端天气事件下的模拟精度。

Method: 使用OceanMesh2D基于全球水深和海岸线数据生成非结构化网格（70,410个节点，海岸线处50米分辨率），构建ADCIRC峡湾模型。选取四个不同特征时段进行验证，与现役最优模型及水位计实测数据对比。测试三种不同的潮汐分潮组合作为强迫条件并分析其成本效益，随后尝试使用全球ADCIRC模型的水面高度输出替代潮汐强迫。

Result: 新模型在显著减少计算时间的前提下取得了与挪威现役预报模型相当的结果。增加更多潮汐分潮略微改善模拟结果，但受限于奥斯陆峡湾较小的计算域，潮汐强迫边界条件的技能有限。使用全球ADCIRC模型输出作为强迫条件有助于进一步减小与水位计数据的偏差，尤其在极端天气事件期间。

Conclusion: 该非结构化网格和ADCIRC模型是挪威现役峡湾预报模型的高效替代方案。潮汐强迫的改善空间有限，采用全球模型输出的水面高度作为边界强迫是提升极端事件模拟精度的有效途径。

Abstract: This study introduces a new unstructured computational mesh for hydrodynamic simulations of the Oslofjord. The mesh was created with global bathymetry and shoreline data, using OceanMesh2D. It contains 70,410 nodes, with a resolution at the coastline of 50 meters. We use the new mesh to create an ADCIRC model of the fjord. The model is run for four time periods with different characteristics, and validated against the current state of the art and elevation gauges in the fjord. Results show that the model achieves similar results to the model currently used for forecasting in Norway, while requiring much less computation time. Three different combinations of tidal constituents are used to force the model, and analyze the cost and benefits of using additional constituents, finding that they slightly improve results. However, the skill of the tidal forcing boundary condition is limited, because of the small domain of the Oslofjord. In order to further reconcile the results' deviation from the gauge data, especially during extreme weather events, the water surface elevation output from a global ADCIRC model was used to force the model instead of tides.

</details>


### [233] [Information fusion and machine learning for sensitivity analysis using physics knowledge and experimental data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17248)
