*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Sankaran Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文研究了结合物理模型与实验观测数据的全局敏感性分析方法，通过物理信息机器学习（深度神经网络和高斯过程）两种策略（损失函数物理约束和预训练+数据更新）提高敏感性估计精度，并在增材制造和湖泊温度建模中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工程系统敏感性分析中，单独使用物理模型或数据驱动模型时，敏感性估计会受模型精度和不确定性影响。如何在物理模型和实验观测同时存在的情况下，有效融合两类信息以最大化敏感性估计精度，是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 采用两种代表性ML技术（DNN和GP），分别构建四种模型：纯数据驱动、物理约束损失函数、预训练+实验更新等组合策略；将模型不确定性纳入Sobol指数计算，比较各模型的敏感性估计边界大小。

Result: DNN-based模型凭借大量模型参数和训练选项自由度，相比GP-based模型得到更紧的敏感性估计边界（更小的不确定性区间）。方法在增材制造和湖泊温度建模两个案例中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 物理信息机器学习策略能有效融合物理模型和实验数据以提升GSA精度，其中DNN方法在敏感性估计的不确定性控制方面优于GP方法。

Abstract: When computational models (either physics-based or data-driven) are used for the sensitivity analysis of engineering systems, the sensitivity estimate is affected by the accuracy and uncertainty of the model. This paper considers global sensitivity analysis (GSA) for situations where both a physics-based model and experimental observations are available, and investigates physics-informed machine learning strategies to effectively combine the two sources of information in order to maximize the accuracy of the sensitivity estimate. Two representative machine learning (ML) techniques are considered, namely, deep neural networks (DNN) and Gaussian process (GP) modeling, and two strategies for incorporating physics knowledge within these techniques are investigated, namely: (i) incorporating loss functions in the ML models to enforce physics constraints, and (ii) pre-training and updating the ML model using simulation and experimental data respectively. Four different models are built for each type (DNN and GP), and the uncertainties in these models are included in the Sobol indices computation. The DNN-based models, with many degrees of freedom in terms of model parameters and training options, are found to result in smaller bounds on the sensitivity estimates when compared to the GP-based models. The proposed methods are illustrated for additive manufacturing and lake temperature modeling examples.

</details>


### [234] [Adaptive surrogate modeling for high-dimensional spatio-temporal output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17250)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Shunsaku Matsumoto,Yoshitomo Miyagi,Daigo Watanabe,Sankaran Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于极高维时空输出问题的自适应代理模型方法。通过降维将高维输出映射到低维潜在空间，在低维空间构建代理模型，并利用结合探索与利用的自适应采样策略，以最少的昂贵物理模型运行次数提高代理模型精度。该方法在燃气轮机叶片的热力分析中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空多物理系统的分析计算成本高昂，且包含大量输入输出。在不确性量化和优化等需要大量函数调用的分析任务中，需要代理模型替代物理模型以提高计算效率。然而，高维时空输出给代理模型构造带来了巨大挑战，现有方法难以高效处理。

Method: 该方法首先采用降维技术将高维时空输出映射到低维潜在空间，然后在低维空间构建代理模型。预测误差（包含重建误差和代理模型误差）通过不同误差指标在原始空间中进行评估。基于代理模型的预测精度，识别新的训练点以自适应改进代理模型。提出了一种新颖的自适应采样技术，将探索（exploration）和利用（exploitation）相结合，以最少的昂贵物理模型运行次数提升代理模型精度。

Result: 通过燃气轮机叶片的热力分析验证了所提方法的有效性，证明了该自适应代理建模方法能够在极少数昂贵模型运行次数下高效提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了高维时空输出下代理模型构建的挑战，结合降维和探索-利用平衡的自适应采样策略，为多物理系统的不确定性量化和优化提供了高计算效率的可行方案。

Abstract: This paper develops an adaptive surrogate modeling method for problems with very high-dimensional spatio-temporal outputs. The analysis of spatio-temporal multi-physics systems is computationally expensive and consists of a large number of inputs and outputs. Surrogate models are often constructed to replace the physics-based model to achieve computational efficiency in analyses such as uncertainty quantification and optimization that require many function calls. In order to address the challenge introduced by the high dimensionality of spatio-temporal output, a dimension reduction method is first employed to map the high-dimensional output to a low-dimensional latent space. This is followed by the construction of the surrogate model in the low-dimensional space. The prediction error in the original space, which includes both the reconstruction error and surrogate model error, is evaluated using different error metrics. Based on the prediction accuracy of the surrogate model, new training points are identified for adaptive improvement of the surrogate model. We present a novel adaptive sampling technique that combines exploration and exploitation to improve the surrogate model accuracy with the fewest possible runs of the expensive physics-based model. Thermo-mechanical analysis of a gas turbine engine blade is used to analyze the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [235] [A multi-level preprocessing and modelling framework for spectral imaging of microplastics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17697)
*Zina-Sabrina Duma,Tenzin Tsering,Sara Heikkinen,Tuomo Soininen,Tuomas Sihvonen,Arto Koistinen,Satu-Pia Reinikainen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个多层级预处理与建模框架，用于微塑料的FT-IR光谱成像分析，整合图像级、瓦片级和光谱级校正，并通过聚类质心光谱匹配实现可扩展的聚合物识别，在PS、PET、PE和PP四种聚合物上取得完美分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱成像虽能对微塑料进行化学特异性和空间分辨分析，但常规应用受限于数据量大、采集伪影、光谱变异性以及相似光谱导致的聚合物误识别等问题。

Method: 提出多层级预处理与建模框架：(1) 图像级通过潜变量选择校正采集条件变化；(2) 瓦片级利用背景校正减少照明伪影；(3) 光谱级结合基线校正、平滑、导数计算、归一化和波长选择，并仅保留粒子光谱；(4) 对粒子光谱进行聚类，对聚类质心而非单个像素执行光谱库匹配，评估十二种匹配策略。

Result: 符号不变的导数余弦相似度方法在PS、PET、PE和PP四种微塑料上实现完美分类精度；聚类工作流相比直接软件匹配产生更空间连贯的粒子图，并大幅减少处理时间。

Conclusion: 多层级校正结合聚类质心光谱匹配能够提升光谱成像微塑料识别的鲁棒性、效率和可解释性，为微塑料的常规化光谱成像分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Spectral imaging provides chemically specific and spatially resolved analysis of microplastics, but its routine application is hindered by large data volumes, acquisition artefacts, spectral variability, and misidentification of polymers due to alike spectra. This study proposes a multi-level preprocessing and modelling framework for FT-IR spectral imaging of microplastics that integrates image-level, tile-level, and spectral-level corrections with scalable identification strategies.
  Image-level variation associated with changing acquisition conditions was done with latent variable selection, while a background-based tile correction reduced illumination-related artefacts. Spectral preprocessing combined baseline correction, smoothing, derivative calculation, normalization, and wavelength selection, and only particle spectra were retained for further analysis to improve computational efficiency. For scalable identification, clustering was applied to particle spectra and spectral library matching was performed on cluster centroids instead of individual pixels. Among twelve evaluated matching strategies, a sign-invariant derivative-based cosine similarity method achieved perfect classification accuracy for polystyrene (PS), polyethylene terephthalate (PET), polyethylene (PE), and polypropylene (PP). The clustering-based workflow also produced more spatially coherent particle maps than direct software-based matching while substantially reducing processing time. The framework was evaluated for supervised classification-based MP indentification. These results show that multi-level correction combined with cluster-centroid spectral matching improves the robustness, efficiency, and interpretability of spectral-imaging-based microplastic identification.

</details>


### [236] [Overlap-free multi-material topology optimization for minimum compliance in two and three dimensions by level-set-based negative-mapping interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17963)
*Dong Wang,Qianglin Ran,Xuanliang Wang,Wei Xiang,Wenming Cheng,Run Du*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的水平集负映射插值方法，用于二维和三维多材料比例拓扑优化，通过交替活动相算法将M相问题分解为二相子问题，并结合进化策略避免复杂灵敏度计算，同时利用负映射插值消除界面材料重叠。数值实验表明该方法能消除灰度单元、产生光滑边界并保证无重叠的材料分布，其柔度性能与经典SIMP方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有拓扑优化方法中存在的灰度单元、材料界面重叠等挑战，尤其是在多材料比例拓扑优化问题中。

Method: 将基于水平集的负映射插值方法扩展到多材料比例拓扑优化（2D和3D）；采用交替活动相算法将多相（M相）问题分解为若干简化的二相子问题；引入进化策略以避免复杂的灵敏度计算；通过负映射插值消除界面处的材料重叠。

Result: 在二维悬臂梁、MBB梁及三维悬臂梁上的数值实验表明：灰度元素被消除，边界光滑，材料分布无重叠。在8个二维测试案例中，4个案例的柔度低于SIMP方法，另外4个分别高出0.3%、0.4%、4.8%和12.7%；还讨论了材料属性、界面处理及迭代次数对结果的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效克服灰度单元和材料重叠问题，在多材料比例拓扑优化中获得与经典SIMP方法相当甚至更优的柔度性能。

Abstract: To address challenges such as gray elements and material overlaps, this paper extends the level set-based negative-mapping interpolation method to the multi-material proportional topology optimization of macro-scale structures in two and three dimensions. The approach utilizes an alternating active-phase algorithm to decompose M-phase problems into simplified two-phase subproblems described by level set functions. By integrating an evolutionary strategy, the method circumvents complex sensitivity calculations. A negative-mapping interpolation then removes the material overlaps at the interfaces. Numerical experiments on 2D cantilever and MBB beams and on a 3D cantilever beam demonstrate that the present method eradicates gray elements, produces smooth boundaries and ensures overlap-free material distributions at a compliance comparable to that of the classical SIMP method, lower than the SIMP value in four of the eight two-dimensional test cases and higher by 0.3%, 0.4%, 4.8% and 12.7% in the other four; the influence of the material properties, of the interface treatment and of the number of iterations on the results is also discussed.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GxP-Agent多智能体系统，将临床实验编程（CDISC标准下的数据集生成）的监管流程编码为有向无环图（DAG），将单一数据集生成任务分解为15个领域特定节点。在CDISC-Bench基准上，Claude Sonnet 4.6达到100%的结构匹配（49/49变量，254条记录），远超最好的RAG基线（59.2%），而所有单智能体和扁平多智能体方法均为0%。系统还泛化到ADAE任务（9节点分支DAG，55变量，1191条记录），首次尝试即达100%结构匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实验编程——将研究方案转化为符合CDISC标准的分析就绪数据集——是监管提交中的瓶颈环节。尽管LLM在代码生成方面表现出色，但在这一任务上彻底失败：11次单次尝试中使用5个前沿模型，均未能生成有效的受试者级分析数据集。作者认为仅依赖LLM推理能力不足，需要将领域流程知识显式编码到系统架构中。

Method: 提出GxP-Agent多智能体系统，核心创新在于将监管流程顺序编码为有向无环图（DAG），将原有单一的数据集生成任务分解为15个领域特定节点。每个节点由worker智能体执行，配备pharmaverse技能上下文、验证门控和条件重试机制。同时构建了CDISC-Bench基准，基于FDA试点提交CDISCPilot01数据集（254名受试者，49个ground-truth ADSL变量），采用执行级评估。

Result: GxP-Agent配合Claude Sonnet 4.6在CDISC-Bench上三次独立运行均实现100%结构匹配（49/49变量，254条正确记录），最佳RAG基线仅59.2%，所有单智能体和扁平多智能体方法均为0%。DAG拓扑还增强了弱模型能力：GPT-4.1在DAG架构下达到59.2%平均结构匹配，而其他架构下为0%。系统推广到ADAE任务（9节点分支DAG，55变量，1191条记录），首次尝试即100%结构匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将领域流程知识编码为图拓扑结构——而非依赖LLM自身的推理——是可靠的、符合GxP规范的临床实验编程的关键使能因素。这一发现对更广泛的LLM工程化部署具有启示：显式的流程建模比纯粹依赖模型推理能力更可靠，尤其适用于强监管、易出错的领域。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [238] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Aegis，一个专为Agentic AI系统设计的运行时治理系统，将模型输出视为操作提案，通过可信决策层在工具执行前进行调解，实现"模型提议、运行时决策"的安全边界。在6300行沙箱语料评估中，Aegis实现了零越权工具应用和零危险副作用完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统能够请求修改文件、发送消息、启动作业或改变工作流状态的工具操作，这使安全问题从有害文本生成转变为有害操作副作用。单纯的提示级治理只能塑造模型行为，无法创建执行边界，因此需要一种运行时层面的机制来阻止危险操作实际发生。

Method: Aegis将模型输出视为操作提案，通过可信决策层在工具执行前进行调解。系统根据活动策略状态评估提案，在服务端解析来源信息，在不确定情况下自动失败关闭，并将选定案例路由至参议院式结算——一种基于法定人数的非单边授权路径。

Result: 在五个运行家族、42个任务、三种条件和每个家族十次重复的沙箱语料中：提示策略条件下产生79行危险比较器路径泄漏（6300行中）；Aegis治理下零受治理的模拟工具应用和零受治理的危险副作用完成（2100行中）；所有1832行Aegis尝试的治理行保持了可信来源解析；所有1019行参议院结算行具有法定人数和最终签名计数证据。

Conclusion: 结果不证明通用自主智能体的安全性，但支持更窄层面的系统论断：在所评估的沙箱语料中，运行时操作边界治理成功阻止了观察到的高风险提案演变为受治理的副作用。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [239] [The Price of Thinking: Reasoning Effort as a Model-Specific API Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16956)
*Yeabin Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过注册的配对对比实验，研究了API合约中显式"高推理努力"条款与省略该条款对Sonnet 5模型在AIME 2026任务上的成本与准确率影响。结果显示显式高努力合约的平均交付成本每调用高0.01031美元，准确率差异未检测到显著，成本/正确答案比率略高，所有结论均限定于特定模型、任务与日期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API买家购买的是带日期的合约（包含请求/服务的模型、推理努力条款、输出轨道、服务产品、提示词和价格表），而非仅模型名称。推理努力条款作为合约的重要组成部分，其显式指定与省略是否影响成本和性能，缺乏严格的实证对比研究。

Method: 注册配对对比实验：对Sonnet 5模型分别采用显式高推理努力与省略推理努力两种合约，使用30道AIME 2026题目、每题目5次调用。每个付费尝试分配一个冻结的终态类别，推理时对题目重新抽样但保留重复调用。使用Model-API元数据、预注册原始响应探针等多源数据进行档案普查，统计计划和分析流程在观察结果前冻结。

Result: 显式高努力合约的平均交付成本比省略合约高0.01031美元/调用（95%CI: +0.00204至+0.01974）；准确率差异为+0.0133（95%CI: -0.0267至+0.0467），未检测到显著准确率差异，但区间允许最多4.67个百分点的增益无法排除。高努力合约的每正确回答成本为0.08665美元，省略合约为0.07662美元。

Conclusion: 显式指定高推理努力会显著增加交付成本，但未检测到相应的准确率提升；不过设计无法排除高达4.67个百分点的准确率增益。研究还发现推理努力省略语义在不同模型甚至同一provider内存在差异。所有结论严格限定于所研究的模型、任务和采集日期，不能外推。

Abstract: API buyers purchase a dated contract, not a model name alone: the contract includes the requested and served model, reasoning-effort term or its omission, output rail, service product, prompt, and price schedule. We study the reasoning-effort term through a registered paired contrast of Sonnet 5 with explicit high effort against the same model with effort omitted, using 30 AIME 2026 items and five calls per item. Every paid attempt was assigned one frozen terminal category, and inference resampled items while retaining their repeated calls. Mean delivered cost was \$0.01031 per call higher under the explicit-high contract than under the omitted contract [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]. The corresponding accuracy contrast was +0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]; we did not detect an accuracy difference, and the interval permits a gain of up to 4.67 percentage points that this design cannot rule out. Cost per correct answer was \$0.08665 under the high-effort contract and \$0.07662 under the omitted contract, as registered point estimates. A dated contract census, Models-API metadata, and preregistered raw-response probes further documented model-specific omission semantics, including within a provider; claims remained at documentation grade when raw structure was indeterminate. The request registry, parser, terminal taxonomy, statistical plan, and analysis pipeline were frozen before outcomes were examined; the resulting claims are bounded to the model, task, and collection date studied.

</details>


### [240] [FedPref: Federated Preference Learning for Structured Radiology Report Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16971)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedPref框架，通过冻结的公共语言模型生成候选JSON抽取结果，本地标注进行排序，各机构仅共享模型更新（Qwen3-8B适配器）进行协作训练。在六家数据量不均的模拟医院上，相比各站点独立训练，客户端平均F1提升2.49点，最差站点F1提升9.10点；在400份人工验证的黄金测试集上达到68.68 F1，且无需共享任何报告或标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告以自由文本描述发现和位置，但下游搜索与分析需要固定模式的关系抽取。不同医疗机构标注数据分布不均（小型医院局部证据少），而集中汇总数据可能不可行（隐私/合规限制）。现有方法无法在不共享数据的情况下让机构从协作中获益，尤其对数据量最少的站点。

Method: FedPref包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用冻结的公共语言模型（异构教师池）为每个样本生成多个候选JSON提取结果；(2) 各站点利用本地标注对候选结果进行排序，形成偏好对；(3) 各站点仅共享Qwen3-8B适配器参数的模型更新进行联邦训练。异构教师池提供跨模型对比，解决单一模型重复采样时偏好对坍塌的问题。

Result: 在六家模拟医院（数据量和疾病分布不均）的开发集上，FedPref相比各站点独立训练在客户端平均F1上提升2.49点，最差站点F1提升9.10点，且数据量最少的站点受益最大。中心化联合训练客户端平均F1比FedPref高2.66点。在400份人工验证的锁定黄金测试集上，FedPref达到68.68 F1，中心化训练为71.67 F1，保持了相同的排序。

Conclusion: FedPref使得数据量不均、无法汇总数据的机构能够在不共享报告或标注的情况下通过协作受益，且在数据最稀缺的站点上带来最大提升，验证了联邦偏好学习的有效性。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings and locations in free text, but downstream search and analysis require these relations in a fixed schema. Learning this extraction requires labels that are unevenly distributed across institutions: smaller hospitals have less local evidence, and pooling data may be infeasible. We introduce FedPref: frozen public language models propose alternative JSON extractions, local annotations rank them, and sites collaboratively train compact Qwen3-8B adapters while sharing only model updates. A heterogeneous teacher pool provides cross-model contrast when repeated single-model samples collapse. On development data from six simulated hospitals with unequal data volume and disease prevalence, FedPref improves client-mean F1 by 2.49 points and worst-site F1 by 9.10 points compared with training each site in isolation, with the largest gains at the sites holding the least data. Central training on the pooled preference-pair union is 2.66 points higher on client-mean F1. On a locked, 400-report manually validated gold test set, FedPref reaches 68.68 F1 and pooled training 71.67, preserving that same ordering. FedPref thus lets institutions with unequal, unpooled data benefit from collaboration without ever sharing reports or annotations.

</details>


### [241] [The Problem Is the Problem: Towards Scalable Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16977)
*Zeyu Zheng,Shengtong Zhang,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的人机协作数学发现范式FAR（Find, Attempt, Recommend），通过文献到评审的级联流水线自动搜索候选问题并过滤，将稀缺的人类专家评审精力集中在高质量结果上。在组合学试点中，从5,245篇论文中筛选出77项供专家评审，发现了多个重要猜想的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助数学研究中，人类精力集中在问题选择和结果评审两个阶段，这两者正成为研究级数学的瓶颈。前沿模型推理和专家评审都是稀缺资源，需要高效分配。

Method: 提出FAR（Find, Attempt, Recommend）文献到评审级联流水线：人类提供研究方向而非单一问题；系统在文献语料库中搜索候选问题（Find）；对候选问题尝试推理（Attempt）；经过多阶段过滤后推荐给专家评审（Recommend）。流水线包括候选猜想/开放问题回收、适定性过滤、自动推理分诊和人工评审筛选。

Result: 在组合学试点中，从5,245篇论文中回收6,453个候选猜想或开放问题，过滤至4,717个适定且仍开放的猜想；自动分诊后产生598个潜在解决方案，最终选出77项供团队评审，发现了Davies-Jenssen-Perkins-Roberts、Erdős-Straus、Ikenmeyer-Pak-Panova和Lund-Saraf-Wolf等猜想的新结果。

Conclusion: FAR范式证明了将人类输入从预先选定问题转变为研究方向的可行性，通过级联自动化过滤有效分配稀缺推理和评审资源，展示了这种新的人机协作模式对数学发现的有效性。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of contributing to mathematical research. In research practice, frontier-model reasoning is a limited resource, and expert mathematical review is even more sharply constrained. Allocating these scarce resources well is therefore central to making AI-assisted mathematical discovery efficient. In most current AI-for-math workflows, human effort is concentrated at the beginning and end, in selecting suitable research problems and later reviewing the resulting artifacts. These two stages are becoming bottlenecks for research-level mathematics. We address them by proposing a new human-AI discovery paradigm. The human input is no longer a single problem selected in advance, but a research direction in which the experts have interest and expertise. The system then searches a broad literature corpus for candidate problems in that direction. Inspired by search and recommender systems, we build Find, Attempt, and Recommend (FAR), a literature-to-review cascade that automates the search for suitable problems and focuses human attention on artifacts that have passed several stages of filtering. In a combinatorics pilot, the pipeline starts from 5,245 combinatorics papers, recovers 6,453 candidate conjectures or open problems, and filters them to 4,717 apparently well-posed and still-open conjectures. Subsequent reasoning and automated triage stages surface 598 potential resolutions and select 77 items for author-team review. Among them, we identify many interesting discoveries, including results on conjectures and questions of Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts, Erdős--Straus, Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova, and Lund--Saraf--Wolf. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of this new mode of human-AI collaboration for mathematical discovery.

</details>


### [242] [SkillEffect: Checked Lowering for Memory-Bounded Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17007)
*Yinuo Wang,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillEffect 是一个用于智能体工具调用的"checked-lowering"（受检降级）运行时，通过独立的检查器、可恢复的源关系、有界的实现和输出的后置条件，确保将 Agent Skills 转化为代码时不会因加载整个输入而超出工具调用的内存限制。在六个算子族和六个插件上验证了该方法能显著降低峰值内存、提高固定内存上限下的完成率，并严格拒绝所有对抗性提案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型将 Agent Skills（代理技能）中的程序性和资源性义务实例化为具体程序时，即使语义正确，程序也可能加载整个输入数据，导致单次工具调用超出可用内存。需要一种机制来确保生成代码在资源上是有界的、可审计的。

Method: SkillEffect 采用架构化的检查-降级（checked-lowering）机制：每个关系插件提供源识别器、输入事实提取器、有界IR构造器、竞技场边界函数和后置条件；一个统一运行时负责检查选择、有界VM执行、原子容量租用和分阶段发布。在执行授权前，独立检查器从提交的程序和不可变输入中重建每个降级方案并验证其合法性。

Result: 在六个算子族上，有界访问显著降低了峰值内存并在外部固定内存上限下提高了完成率；六个插件跨五种执行模式（从流式归约到有界堆Top-k）实例化了同一契约。XLSX 入行研究和 Top-k 扩展表明新关系和新保留状态模式复用同一信任边界，检查器接受所有合法配置并拒绝所有对抗性提案。

Conclusion: 一个统一的 checked-lowering 架构可以在智能体工具调度时强制实施异构的注册内存关系约束，兼顾灵活性与安全性。

Abstract: Agent Skills can specify procedural and resource obligations for tool use, and language models instantiate them as concrete programs. However, when models turn this guidance into code for existing tool interfaces, even a semantically correct program may load an entire input and exceed the memory available to one tool call. We present SkillEffect, a checked-lowering runtime for computations with a recoverable source relation, an audited bounded implementation, and a registered output postcondition. Before granting execution authority, an independent checker rebuilds each proposed lowering from the submitted program and immutable input. Every relation plugin supplies a source recognizer, input-fact extractor, bounded-IR constructor, arena-bound function, and postcondition; one common runtime provides checked selection, bounded-VM execution, atomic capacity leasing, and staged publication. Generality in SkillEffect is architectural rather than automatic: each supported computation requires an audited relation plugin, while the dispatch, resource-control, execution, and publication mechanisms are shared across plugins. Across six operator families, bounded access substantially reduces peak memory and improves completion under externally fixed caps. Six plugins instantiate the same contract across five execution patterns, from streaming reduction to bounded-heap Top-k. The XLSX onboarding study and Top-k extension show that a new relation and a new retained-state pattern reuse the same trust boundary, while the checker accepts all evaluated legal configurations and rejects all adversarial proposals. Together, these results show that one checked-lowering architecture can enforce heterogeneous registered memory relations at Agent tool dispatch.

</details>


### [243] [Memory Is Communication: The Frontier Between Remembering and Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17053)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究有限资源的智能体如何在记忆（自身历史）与同伴交流（信号）之间分配信息预算以达成决策性能阈值。提出"记忆-信号边界"（remembering-signaling frontier）概念，刻画在固定任务与决策规则下，记忆与消息速率组合的可行区域。作者假设：当历史能带来更大的任务损失减少时，有界智能体所需同伴通信更少。初步指称游戏实验表明，目标重复与更短成功消息相关，但隐藏循环规则带来的可预测性并未缩短消息长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的决策者（包括生物与AI系统）同时受制于记忆容量与通信带宽。现有研究大多单独处理记忆或通信问题，缺乏一个统一框架来理解两者之间的权衡与替代关系。作者希望在信息论意义上刻画"记住多少"与"说多少"之间如何相互补偿，并提出可检验的经验预测。

Method: 理论层面，作者将记忆与消息速率视为双资源约束，在固定任务与决策规则下定义满足性能阈值的可达区域，并以其高效边界作为"记忆-信号边界"（frontier）。经验层面，通过初步指称游戏（referential games）实验，操作目标重复（history带来的损失减少）与隐藏循环规则（可预测性），测量对成功消息长度的差异化影响，并建议通过系统变化记忆与消息速率来估计边界并检验预测。

Result: 理论建构了记忆-信号边界的框架；初步实验显示目标重复与更短的成功消息并存（支持假设方向），而隐藏循环规则带来的可预测性并未缩短消息长度（不符合简单可预测性假说的预期），提示并非所有历史信息都能等量替代通信。

Conclusion: 历史信息减少任务损失的能力是决定通信需求的关键因素；并非所有可预测性都能降低通信成本。未来工作应通过多任务、多条件的系统实验来估计记忆-信号边界，以确证"更大历史损失减少→更少通信需求"的跨任务假设。

Abstract: A bounded agent may obtain information for a decision from its own past, from peers, or from both sources. Retaining task-relevant history can reduce later communication, while a peer message can supply what memory lacks. Under limits on both resources, how should an agent allocate its information budget? Given a fixed task and decision rule, the memory and message rate pairs attaining a performance threshold form an achievable region under specified rules for using history and peer observations. We call its efficient boundary the remembering--signaling frontier. Across conditions where history permits the same maximum reduction in task loss, we hypothesize that a bounded agent will need less peer communication when it obtains a larger loss reduction from history. In preliminary referential games, target repetition coincided with shorter successful messages, while predictability from a hidden cyclic rule did not shorten them. Experiments varying memory and message rates can estimate the frontier and test this prediction across cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [244] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DiSCO，一种零样本、纯黑盒的防御方法，通过在提示词层面进行分布引导的后缀扩展（beam search + 对比评分），有效降低文生图模型生成NSFW内容的成功率，可即插即用于任意模型而无需改动模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对文生图模型生成不安全（NSFW）内容的防御大多依赖白盒假设（如文本编码器优化、权重编辑、推理时干预），无法扩展到专有闭源模型。黑盒替代方案基于LLM提示重写虽通用性更广，但在"良性对抗"（benign adversarial）问题——即提示词在语言学层面安全但触发模型有害生成——这一关键场景下失效。

Method: DiSCO是一种零样本、严格黑盒的防御模块，完全在提示词层面运作。其核心流程包括：(1) 通过beam search进行分布引导的后缀扩展；(2) 利用目标模型自身生成的安全/不安全图像池进行对比打分（contrastive scoring）来优化；(3) 迭代自适应反馈直到生成安全内容。整个过程无需模型重训练、微调或访问模型内部。

Result: 在I2P基准测试上，DiSCO在多种红队攻击下分别使未防御模型和已防御模型的攻击成功率（ASR）降低37.7%和25.13%，同时保持语义保真度并提升图像一致性。

Conclusion: DiSCO作为一种黑盒、架构无关的即插即用模块，可以方便地应用于任何文生图系统，无需对模型本身做任何改动，为闭源/专有模型的NSFW内容防御提供了一种可行且有效的方案。

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{benign adversarial} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>


### [245] [EvoTS-Agent: A Self-Evolving LLM Agent for Financial Time Series Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17933)
*Lei Jiang,Ye Wei,Xinyu Xi,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Yifan Bao,Raad Khraishi,Yihao Ang,Anthony K. H. Tung,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoTS-Agent，一种基于验证反馈引导的自进化LLM智能体，用于金融时间序列变点检测，通过Revision、Alternative Strategy和Recombination三种算子自动进化实验流程，在四个基准数据集上优于现有LLM智能体且保持100%执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳性和异质性统计特征，单一无监督算法无法在所有资产和市场状态下保持一致的变点检测性能。传统工作流高度依赖专家进行模型选择、特征设计和超参数调优，限制了可扩展性和适应性。

Method: EvoTS-Agent采用自进化框架：首先通过探索性数据分析（EDA）刻画数据集特性并初始化候选检测模型；随后通过三种互补算子进化可执行的实验轨迹——Revision（利用当前最优解进行改良）、Alternative Strategy（在进展停滞时探索根本不同的建模方向）、Recombination（综合高绩效轨迹中的互补证据）；验证反馈在整个搜索过程中指导轨迹进化，使检测流程自适应每个数据集的统计特征。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，EvoTS-Agent在所有评估的后端LLM上均持续优于现有基于LLM的智能体，且保持100%的执行成功率。

Conclusion: 验证引导的自进化机制有效解决了金融时间序列变点检测中算法选择与自适应调优的难题，提升了检测流程的可扩展性和可靠性。

Abstract: Financial time series exhibit non-stationary and heterogeneous statistical properties, making change-point detection challenging because no single unsupervised algorithm performs consistently across assets and market regimes. Conventional workflows consequently depend heavily on expert-driven model selection, feature design, and hyperparameter tuning, limiting their scalability and adaptability. We propose EvoTS-Agent, a validation-guided self-evolving LLM agent for autonomous financial time-series change-point detection. EvoTS-Agent first performs curated exploratory data analysis to characterize dataset properties and initialize candidate detection models. It then evolves executable experiment trajectories through three complementary operators: \textit{Revision} exploits the current best solution, \textit{Alternative Strategy} explores fundamentally different modeling directions when progress stagnates, and \textit{Recombination} synthesizes complementary evidence from high-performing trajectories. Validation feedback guides trajectory evolution throughout the search, enabling the agent to adapt its detection pipeline to the statistical characteristics of each dataset while preserving reliable optimization. Experiments across four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EvoTS-Agent consistently outperforms existing LLM-based agents while maintaining a 100\% execution success rate across all evaluated backbone LLMs.

</details>


### [246] [KernelArc: A Multi-Agent Framework for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17071)
*Joyjit Kundu,Ben Stoffelen,Kaili Wang,Peter Vrancx,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KernelArc，一个用于异构工作负载下自主GPU内核优化的多智能体框架。智能体通过结论共享内存、确定性基准守卫和只读跨智能体状态进行并行协调，在NVIDIA H100和B200上使用SOL-ExecBench工作负载进行评估，在代表性L1、L2、Quantization和FlashInfer任务中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化是一个复杂的搜索问题，单一优化策略难以覆盖异构工作负载的多样性需求。作者推测共享的多智能体搜索可以拓宽探索空间，在固定候选预算内达到更强的优化效果（即更强的incumbent），这一动机推动了对多智能体协作框架的探索。

Method: KernelArc采用策略专业化智能体并行运行，通过三种协调机制进行协作：(1) 结论共享内存（conclusions-only shared memory）用于信息传递；(2) 确定性基准守卫（deterministic benchmark guard）确保评测可靠性；(3) 只读跨智能体状态配合平台触发草稿机制（plateau-triggered drafting）以促进智能体间知识复用。评估使用SOL-ExecBench工作负载，覆盖NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU。

Result: 生成的优化实现涵盖自定义BF16 GEMM、静态cuBLASLt Expert-API配置表、融合混合专家反向传播、形状门控解码器层融合、原生NVFP4分组查询注意力和分页预填充注意力等广泛内核类型。在2026年7月30日记录的公开SOL-ExecBench排行榜快照中，提交结果在代表性L1、L2、Quantization和FlashInfer任务中排名第一。

Conclusion: 实验轨迹支持核心论点：共享多智能体搜索能够在固定候选预算内拓宽探索并达到更强的incumbent；单个协调特征的价值取决于具体内核类型和优化阶段，说明需要根据任务特点动态调整协调策略。

Abstract: We present KernelArc, a multi-agent framework for autonomous GPU kernel optimization across heterogeneous workloads. Strategy-specialized agents run in parallel and coordinate through conclusions-only shared memory, a deterministic benchmark guard, and read-only cross-agent state with plateau-triggered drafting. We evaluate \kernelarc{} on NVIDIA H100 and B200 GPUs using category-representative SOL-ExecBench workloads. The resulting implementations span custom BF16 GEMM, static cuBLASLt Expert-API configuration tables, fused mixture-of-experts backward, shape-gated decoder-layer fusion, native NVFP4 grouped-query attention, and paged prefill attention. At the public SOL-ExecBench leaderboard snapshot recorded on July~30, 2026, these submissions ranked first on representative L1, L2, Quantization, and FlashInfer tasks. The trajectories support the paper's central motivation: shared multi-agent search can broaden exploration and reach stronger incumbents within a fixed candidate budget, while the value of individual coordination features depends on the kernel and optimization stage.

</details>


### [247] [A decodability criterion predicts when hidden-state selection beats majority voting in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17124)
*Zhixiang wang,Ziliang Hong,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE（Correctness-Axis SElection）方法，通过在答案token的隐藏状态上训练线性门控来动态选择候选答案。核心贡献是"可解码性"（decodability）指标，它能无泄漏地预测隐藏状态选择是否优于多数投票，为给定模型和任务提供提前选择决策策略的实用准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票在困难问题上不可靠，因为采样答案间存在相关性错误，导致即使增加采样数量反而使决策更差。从模型隐藏状态读取正确性信号来选择候选是可行替代方案，但其准确率在不同模型和任务间波动大，且缺乏衡量该方案何时可信的指标。

Method: CASE在答案token的隐藏状态上训练线性门控（linear gate），选择得分最高的候选。提出了"可解码性"（decodability）——一种无泄漏的度量，衡量门控将正确候选排在错误候选之上的能力。通过问题分组评估来消除问题身份泄漏（question-identity leakage）。

Result: 在留出数据上，可解码性预测选择优于投票的准确率增益，Pearson相关系数r=0.75，决策阈值接近AUC=0.60。在通用和医学LLM上，CASE在中等难度问题上比投票提高最多19个点，在困难问题上提高16.8个点。可解码性依赖于模型需回忆的对齐知识而非模型规模，其对未知科学领域的预测迁移误差在3.8个点以内。

Conclusion: 可解码性为给定模型和任务提供了可预先测量的实用准则，用于在学习式选择与多数投票之间做选择。该指标不依赖模型规模，且能跨领域迁移，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Combining the answers a large language model (LLM) samples for a question into one decision is a test-time information fusion problem, usually solved by majority voting. Voting is unreliable on difficult questions, where the sampled answers share correlated errors, so the wrong answer can win and drawing more samples makes the decision worse. Selecting a candidate by reading a correctness signal from the model's hidden states is a promising alternative, but its accuracy varies across models and tasks, and no measure indicates when it can be trusted. In this paper, we propose CASE (Correctness-Axis SElection), a dynamic selection combiner that trains a linear gate on the answer-token hidden state and selects the highest-scoring candidate. Its main contribution is decodability, a leakage-free measure of how well the gate ranks a question's correct candidates above its incorrect ones, which predicts whether hidden-state selection will outperform voting. A conventional probe appears accurate only because of question-identity leakage, which vanishes under question-grouped evaluation. On held-out data, decodability predicts the accuracy gain of selection over voting with a Pearson correlation r=0.75 and a decision threshold near AUC=0.60. Across general and medical LLMs, CASE improves over voting by up to 19 points on medium-difficulty questions and 16.8 points on hard questions. Decodability depends on the aligned knowledge a model must recall, not on its scale, and its prediction transfers to an unseen scientific domain within 3.8 points. It thus provides a practical criterion, measurable in advance for a given model and task, for choosing between learned selection and majority voting.

</details>


### [248] [Toward Personal Intelligence Through Cooperative Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17128)
*Yashar Talebirad,Osman Jime,Ali Parsaee,Eden Redman,Yongbin Kim,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"合作性观察"（cooperative observation）框架，指出个人AI系统面临观测瓶颈（observation bottleneck），其能力受限于系统能观测到什么。系统与用户之间存在反馈回路：系统构建用户部分生活模型，用户评估其行为，用户的同意与控制决定系统未来可观测的内容。通过有用且可检查的行为维持或扩展观测通道；失败则导致用户纠正、收窄、撤销或放弃。论文基于Organizm原型六个月的初步单被试报告，并概述评估方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI系统需要建模用户的目標、约束和持续承诺来代表用户行事，但模型质量受系统可观测内容的限制。然而，更广泛的观测本身并不能改善辅助效果，因为受限系统必须为当前任务选择和压缩信息。现有研究未充分理解观测瓶颈中的合作结构——即用户与系统之间的反馈循环如何决定观测通道的存续与扩展。

Method: 本文采用论证与实证结合的方法：首先从理论上论证观测瓶颈的合作结构，提出"合作性观察"框架描述有用性、信任与未来访问之间的反馈回路；随后通过Organizm（一个实验性个人AI原型）进行为期六个月的初步单被试纵向研究，作为框架的实证支撑；最后概述评估观测质量如何塑造个人AI的评估方向。

Result: 提出了"合作性观察"作为个人智能的研究框架，强调有用且可检查的行为能为用户提供维持或扩展观测通道的理由，而失败会导致用户纠正、收窄、撤销或放弃观测通道。基于Organizm六个月的初步单被试研究提供了初步实证支持，并勾勒出衡量观测质量对个人AI影响的评估方向。

Conclusion: 个人AI的观测瓶颈本质上是合作性的：有用性驱动信任，信任决定用户的控制意愿，进而决定系统未来的数据访问权限，形成闭合反馈回路。该框架为设计个人AI系统和评估其观测质量提供了新的视角，但需要更大规模的实证验证。

Abstract: A personal AI system needs a model of the user's goals, constraints, and ongoing commitments to plan and act on their behalf, and the quality of that model is bounded by what the system can observe. Broader observation does not by itself improve assistance because a bounded system must select and compress information for the task at hand. We argue that this observation bottleneck has a cooperative structure: the system builds a partial model of the user's changing life, the user evaluates its actions, and the user's consent and control shape what it can observe next. Useful and inspectable behavior can give users a reason to maintain or expand the observation channel, while failures can lead them to correct, narrow, revoke, or abandon it. We use the term cooperative observation for this feedback loop among usefulness, trust, and future access, and propose it as a framework for personal intelligence. We report a preliminary single-subject account from Organizm, a prototype used over six months, and outline evaluation directions for measuring how observation quality shapes personal AI.

</details>


### [249] [KnowSim: Evaluating Information Calibration in LLM Assistants with User Simulators that Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17150)
*Yoonjoo Lee,Hyoungwook Jin,Tae Soo Kim,Shaoyang Zhang,Philippe Laban,Q. Vera Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KNOWSIM，一个基于显式知识状态建模的用户模拟器评估框架，通过信息单元图谱和前置关系捕捉用户知识演变，并计算三个机制性指标，在705个人机对话会话中验证了其与人类判断的高度一致性，并揭示了大语言模型在不同知识水平用户上的能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用户模拟器不显式建模用户知识，既无法产生跨知识水平的真实交互，也无法反映知识随时间演变的动态过程，导致对LLM信息校准能力的评估存在根本性缺陷。

Method: KNOWSIM构建了一个维护显式知识状态（以信息单元图表示，含前置关系）的用户模拟器，知识状态依据学习理论中的更新规则动态演化，并据此计算知识增益、传递校准和认知过载三个指标。

Result: 在705个人机对话会话（跨两个领域、按知识水平分层）中，KNOWSIM的排名与人类判断有73-74%的一致性，优于三个基线模拟器；在9个LLM上的评估显示最佳模型随用户知识水平而变化，揭示了标准评估中不可见的aptitude-treatment交互效应。

Conclusion: 显式建模用户知识状态是评估LLM信息校准能力的关键，KNOWSIM不仅提供了更可靠的评估工具，还发现了"最优模型因用户而异"这一重要现象，表明当前标准评估范式可能掩盖了模型在不同用户群体上的真实表现差异。

Abstract: To effectively collaborate with users on knowledge-intensive tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) must perform information calibration: matching content to a user's evolving understanding and cognitive capacity. Yet user simulators used to evaluate and train LLMs do not explicitly model user knowledge so they neither produce realistic interactions across knowledge levels nor reflect how interactions unfold as that knowledge evolves. To close this gap, we introduce KNOWSIM, an evaluation framework built around a user simulator that maintains explicit knowledge states, represented as a graph of Information Units with prerequisite relationships, that evolve under update rules grounded in learning theory. KNOWSIM computes three metrics (Knowledge Gain, Delivery Calibration, Cognitive Overload) directly from the knowledge state trajectory, reflecting key mechanistic aspects of information calibration. We validate KNOWSIM against 705 human-AI sessions across two domains, stratified by knowledge level: its rankings align significantly with human judgments (73-74% sign agreement), outperforming three baseline simulators. Applied to 9 LLMs, KNOWSIM reveals that the best model shifts by user knowledge level, revealing aptitude-treatment interactions invisible to standard evaluation.

</details>


### [250] [Synthesizing Feature Extractors: An Agentic Approach for Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17170)
*Hai Xia,Carlos Ansótegui,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型（LLM）在代理式check-fix-verify循环中自动合成可执行的Python特征提取器的方法，用于约束满足问题的算法选择。该方法从MiniZinc模型生成类型化图表示并计算结构属性，在三个组合问题上优于专家手工设计的mzn2feat特征和基于transformer的trans2feat方法，同时保持特征提取器的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束满足问题的算法选择依赖手工设计特征提取器，这需要深厚的领域专业知识，并且当新问题类别出现时，手工设计成为瓶颈。现有方法如mzn2feat依赖专家经验，而transformer方法虽自动化但缺乏可解释性。因此需要一种自动化、可解释且能适应新问题类别的特征提取方法。

Method: 本文采用LLM代理在check-fix-verify循环中工作：给定高层MiniZinc模型和实例，LLM生成可执行的Python脚本，构建类型化图表示并计算结构属性（如图密度、变量聚类、约束紧度）。合成的特征提取器用于生成算法选择器，与专家设计的mzn2feat特征和transformer-based trans2feat变体进行比较评估。

Result: 在三个组合问题（车辆路由、汽车排产、固定长度纠错码）上，使用五个最先进求解器的组合进行测试。合成的特征提取器在测试集准确性上始终优于专家设计的mzn2feat特征，在FLECC上最多提升8.3个百分点，同时也优于基于transformer的trans2feat变体。

Conclusion: 自动化LLM驱动的特征提取器合成方法能够生成可解释、针对特定问题的特征，在算法选择任务上超越手工专家特征和深度学习方法，同时保持特征的可检查性和可理解性，为约束满足问题的算法端口folio提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Algorithm selection for constraint satisfaction problems requires extracting features that capture problem structure. Manually designing feature extractors demands deep domain expertise and quickly becomes a bottleneck when new problem classes appear. We present an automated approach that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) in an agentic check--fix--verify loop to synthesize executable Python scripts that act as interpretable, problem-specific feature extractors. Given a high-level MiniZinc model and an instance, the LLM agent generates code that constructs a typed graph representation and computes structural properties such as graph density, variable clustering, and constraint tightness. We evaluate our approach on three combinatorial problems (vehicle routing, car sequencing, fixed-length error-correcting codes) with a portfolio of five state-of-the-art solvers. The synthesized extractors yield algorithm selectors that consistently outperform both expert-curated mzn2feat features (up to $8.3$ percentage points (pp) test-set accuracy on FLECC) and the best transformer-based trans2feat variants. In the meanwhile, the synthesized feature extractors remain inspectable.

</details>


### [251] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Automated Safety Benchmarks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17183)
*Nyamtulla Shaik,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对小型语言模型（SLM），系统评估了五个主流安全/合规基准套件在26个开源SLM上的有效性和稳健性。研究发现LLM-centric安全基准在SLM上存在大量模糊判断，且与提示复杂度和模型架构相关，揭示出"能力-安全混杂"问题，并指出基于聚合均值分数的排行榜在模糊处理方式不同时排名会显著变化，因此这些基准不足以作为SLM安全评估的唯一证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM越来越多地部署在资源受限和隐私敏感的场景中，其安全和偏见失败会带来安全与社会风险。然而现有的AI安全/安全/合规基准是为大语言模型设计的，可能无法可靠迁移到SLM上。作者旨在回答这些基准能否有效可靠地评估SLM这一核心问题。

Method: 作者对五个广泛使用的基准套件在26个开源SLM上进行了大规模评估，采用统一的评判标准（0分=有害，1分=安全，0.5分=模糊/不相关），系统分析模糊判断的分布及其与提示复杂度、模型架构、词法密度、输出困惑度、输出长度、词法复杂度、自一致性和回复-提示相似度等因素的关系，并对模糊处理方式不同的情景下的排行榜稳健性进行数学分析。

Result: 研究发现：模糊判断在所有基准中占主导地位，并与提示复杂度和模型架构相关；模糊率随词法密度、输出困惑度和输出长度增加而升高，随词法复杂度、自一致性和回复-提示相似度增加而降低；这揭示了"能力-安全混杂"现象；此外，由于模糊判断普遍存在，聚合均值分数排行榜在数学上是脆弱的——在合理的模糊处理方式下模型排名会显著变化，即使底层输出不变。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：以LLM为中心的安全基准不足以作为SLM安全评估的独立证据，需要开发专门针对SLM特性的安全评估方法，以消除能力与安全之间的混杂效应并增强评估的稳健性。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in resource-constrained, privacy-sensitive settings, where safety and bias failures can cause security and societal risks. However, existing AI safety\slash security\slash compliance benchmarks are designed for large language models that may not transfer reliably to SLMs. We therefore ask: Can these benchmarks effectively and reliably evaluate SLMs? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale assessment of the effectiveness and robustness of these automated pipelines by evaluating five widely used benchmark suites across 26 open-source SLMs under a unified judging rubric, which assigns a score of 0, 1, or 0.5 to harmful, safe, or ambiguous/irrelevant responses, respectively. Across the benchmarks, ambiguous judgments dominate and correlate with prompt complexity and model architecture, indicating that {\em LLM-centric safety benchmarks are insufficient as standalone evidence for SLM safety assessment}. In general, the ambiguity rate increases with lexical density, output perplexity, and output length and decreases with lexical sophistication, self-coherence, and reply-prompt similarity. This reveals a capability-safety confound that mixes model capability with apparent safety. Since ambiguity is prevalent, aggregate mean-score leaderboards are mathematically brittle: model rankings change significantly under reasonable ambiguity treatments, even when the underlying outputs remain unchanged.

</details>


### [252] [Fool's Gold: Defensive Deception Against Safety-Removal Attacks on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17202)
*Mark Russinovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"愚人金"（Fool's Gold）的诱饵加固防御策略。该防御不试图阻止开源模型权重被移除拒答机制（abliteration），而是"承认失败"并主动毒化该攻击的收益：一旦拒答层被剥除，模型对危险操作请求的大多数回答都是自信流畅但关键要素被篡改的诱饵，使攻击者难以区分真假答案。该防御在七种模型（9B-122B，稠密与MoE）上实现，六种通过预注册效能门槛，在受攻击状态下产生了显著比例的诱饵回答，同时保持正常行为与能力不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源语言模型的安全对齐极易被移除——abliteration可以通过投影拒答中间方向在几分钟内剥离对齐，且现有的发布时防御均无法持久阻止。既然无法阻止攻击，本文转而采取"欺骗"策略：使攻击者的攻击行为变得无利可图甚至有害。

Method: 诱饵加固（Decoy Hardening）在可微分的攻击模拟器中训练诱饵，使其仅在受攻击状态下表达（即仅当拒答被剥离后才产生诱饵回答）。通过"拒答钉"（refusal pin）和"良性牵绳"（benign leash）保持干净状态下的原始行为。诱饵回答的关键要素均被伪造，但语气自信流畅，使外部验证者难以区分真伪。

Result: 在七个来自五个家族的模型（9B-122B，含稠密和MoE）上评估：六个通过预注册效能门槛的模型中，受攻击状态下0.51-0.90对保留提示的回答为诱饵（其中+0.27-0.84归因于本防御），且全部保持在良性行为和能力预算内；第七个较小模型未通过门槛（边界情况）。在外部红队基准的CBRNE相关切片上，受防御的122B模型在0.82-0.86的匹配质量回答中致命错误，而未受防御模型最多0.10。重复采样（K=64）的元素级共识也只能在0.083-0.625的提示中重建可用流程，而未经防御时为0.58-0.96，且没有无标签方式区分两种情形。

Conclusion: 该防御的声明是认识论层面的：在没有独立地面真值的情况下，任何测试过的观测面都无法区分伪造答案与正确回答。防御具有局限性：不支持上下文越狱防护，且仅保护最初发布的受防御权重。

Abstract: Safety alignment in open-weight language models is trivially removable: abliteration projects a refusal-mediating direction out of the weights in minutes, and no release-time defense we are aware of prevents it durably. What cannot be prevented can be deceived. Our defense, decoy hardening ("Fool's Gold"), concedes the refusal strip and poisons its payoff: once refusal is stripped, most answers to hazardous operational requests are confident, fluent decoys whose critical elements are falsified. Decoys are trained inside a differentiable simulation of the attack, expressing only in the attacked state; a refusal pin and benign leash hold clean-state behavior to the original. We instantiate it on seven models from five families (9B-122B, dense and mixture-of-experts). On the six models passing our pre-registered efficacy gate, 0.51-0.90 of attacked-state responses to held-out prompts are decoys, +0.27-0.84 attributable to the defense; all six stay within registered benign-behavior and capability budgets; the seventh (smaller) fails the gate (boundary case). Rates replicate on a frozen test split or untouched strata. The claim is epistemic: without independent ground truth, no observation surface we tested separates falsified answers from correct ones - on external red-team benchmarks' CBRNE-adjacent slice, the defended 122B is fatally wrong on 0.82-0.86 of matched-quality answers vs at most 0.10 undefended. Repeated sampling does not restore trust: element-wise consensus at K=64 reconstructs a fully usable procedure on 0.083-0.625 of prompts where the instrument validates, vs 0.58-0.96 undefended, with no label-free way to tell the regimes apart; on the weakest such model the claim is per-draw only. We evaluate chemical and biological hazards; the defense does not address in-context jailbreaks and protects only the initially released defended weights.

</details>


### [253] [Explicit State Elicitation Is Not Enough: A Controlled Audit of Memory-Policy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17247)
*Yihang Chen,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Chong Peng,Huan Xu,Shuaiting Li,Yiqi Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实施了一套结构化中间输出的实证审计协议，用于评估个性化代理中检索记忆的决策路由策略。通过构建受控反事实数据集和系列诊断，发现状态结构化提示带来的增益主要源于词法捷径而非真实语义机制，暴露状态定义可提升准确率但显式状态输出字段未带来显著增益，逐例准确率高估了反事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化代理需要判断检索到的用户记忆是否应当被使用、忽略、更新或查询，这直接影响当前任务。已有方法声称通过提示工程改善此类决策，但缺乏对结构化中间输出（如状态标签）是否真正反映内部机制的严格审计，需要解决"表面增益掩盖词法捷径"的评估陷阱。

Method: 提出五步审计协议：(1)审计数据集捷径（TF-IDF词法可分离性诊断）；(2)隔离捆绑的提示变更；(3)检查中间标签是否与答案关联；(4)测试分解语义证据；(5)审计提供端执行失败。构建480例合成开发集和160例受控反事实测试集（40组四路匹配家族），比较Llama-3.3-70B与GPT-OSS-120B上的多种提示/状态标签条件。

Result: 480例合成集看似显示状态结构化提示增益大，但TF-IDF诊断暴露词法可分离性且无正向独立Ignore案例，说明存在数据集捷径。160例受控集上，暴露四种状态定义提升准确率，但显式状态输出字段对Llama-3.3-70B无显著增益，对GPT-OSS-120B仅有边际非显著增益。基准关联状态标签会改变预测，但因其与策略确定性映射，仅属于标签条件化诊断而非内部机制证据。家族级与种子稳定性分析表明逐例准确率高估反事实一致性，完整四路家族成功罕见。探索性的分解语义证据引导未能改善路由，GPT-OSS条件因提供端请求验证不可用。

Conclusion: 结构化中间输出声称的改进需要通过受控反事实审计才能验证其忠实性；词法捷径、标签条件化效应和逐例聚合指标都会造成虚假增益假象。该协议为评估代理记忆决策路由提供了可复用的实证框架，且明确限定评估范围（策略分类而非下游响应、工具动作或存储变更）。

Abstract: Personalized agents must decide whether retrieved user memory should be used, ignored, updated, or queried before it affects a current task. We use this setting to develop an empirical audit protocol for structured intermediate outputs: first audit dataset shortcuts, then isolate bundled prompt changes, check whether intermediate labels are answer-associated, test decomposed semantic evidence, and audit provider-level execution failures. A 480-example synthetic development set initially suggested large gains from a state-structured prompt bundle, but TF-IDF diagnostics showed lexical separability and no positive standalone Ignore cases. We therefore construct a frozen 160-example controlled counterfactual set with 40 matched four-way families and rule-derived reference policies. On this set, exposing the four state definitions improves accuracy, but an isolated explicit state-output field does not significantly improve policy accuracy for Llama-3.3-70B and gives only a marginal, non-significant gain for GPT-OSS-120B. Supplying benchmark-associated state labels shifts policy predictions, but because those labels deterministically map to policies, this is a label-conditioning diagnostic rather than evidence of a faithful internal mechanism. Family-level and seed-stability analyses further show that example-level accuracy overstates counterfactual consistency: complete four-way family success is rare. An exploratory follow-up that elicits decomposed semantic evidence also fails to improve routing for the cleanly evaluated endpoint; the corresponding GPT-OSS condition was unavailable because of provider-side request validation. We evaluate policy classification only, not downstream responses, tool actions, or memory-store mutation.

</details>


### [254] [Do LLMs Know a Good Hypothesis When They See One? Logit-Based Energy Scoring Outperforms Prompted LLM-as-Judge for Scientific Hypothesis Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17270)
*Swati Rajwal,Sanjay Das,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于logit能量的评分方法，利用语言模型的内在置信度来评估科学假设，而非传统的比较式评判或语义相似度。在1,323篇论文、12个学科上的基准测试中，内在评分在Hit@1上达到33.0%，优于提示式列表排序的16.6%；最强的配置（1B参数模型）达到53.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被用于科学假设生成，但如何评估生成的假设仍然是可信AI科学工作流中的挑战。现有方法通常使用LLM作为评判者或依赖语义相似度，这可能会偏向熟悉而非新颖的想法，缺乏对假设质量的可靠评估手段。

Method: 提出基于logit能量的评分方法，利用语言模型的内在置信度（intrinsic confidence）而非比较式判断来评估假设。基准测试涵盖7个语言模型、1,323篇论文、12个学科，每篇论文配对一个正确假设和15个错误备选，比较内在评分与提示式列表排序的效果。

Result: 内在评分在Hit@1上达到33.0%（两个评分器合并），而提示式列表排序仅为16.6%。最强配置为一个10亿参数模型使用logit能量评分达到53.1%，但该结果是14种模型-评分器组合事后选择的最大值。

Conclusion: 内在模型置信度在科学假设评估中展现出潜力，并激励未来在可信AI驱动的科学发现中进一步研究基于置信度的方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for scientific hypothesis generation. However, evaluating generated hypotheses remains a challenge for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific workflows. Existing approaches often use LLMs as judges or rely on semantic similarity, which can favor familiar ideas over novel ones. We propose a logit-based energy scoring method that evaluates hypotheses using a language model's intrinsic confidence rather than comparative judgment. We benchmarked seven language models on 1,323 papers across 12 disciplines. Each paper was paired with its hypothesis and fifteen incorrect alternatives. Intrinsic scoring reached 33.0% Hit@1 pooled across both scorers, compared with 16.6% for prompted listwise ranking. The strongest configuration, a 1-billion-parameter model using logit-based energy scoring, reached 53.1%, though this was the maximum across 14 model-by-scorer combinations selected post hoc. Overall, intrinsic model confidence shows potential for scientific hypothesis evaluation. This study also motivates future research on confidence-based methods for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific discovery.

</details>


### [255] [ASI-Bench: At the Dawn of Artificial Superintelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17271)
*Junwei Zhou,Zhen Sun,Binyu Li,Jiangyu Zhou,Yuexi Pan,Hengyu Wang,Honghe Ren,Xiaohan Jia,Xueyang Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Yongchao Chen,Yuanning Feng,Junhao Wu,Cheng Zhang,Sijia Chen,Haoyu Xue,Chengsong You,Huan Wang,Koutian Wu,Peigan Gao,Jiakun Wu,Wenzhe Li,Ergan Shang,Qingyuan Zheng,Jingjing Zhou,Ruixuan Jia,Yan Xu,Hongrui Zhang,Xiao-Han Ma,Zhengxiang Cheng,Yuexing Hao,Liting Mai,Xianglin Ji,Wenjun Zhang,Zhuofan Chen,Yixiao Huang,Chi Wang,Wenyue Hua,Yilun Hao,Yuantao Zhai,Ziyan Zhao,Jingyan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASI-Bench，首个联合评估AI系统创新探索与自主科学执行能力的基准，通过逐步撤回方法论指导来测试AI独立开展研究的能力。在18种先进智能体-模型配置上，平均得分从完整指导的50.91骤降至仅指定方法的29.10，以及自主确定方法的26.62，表明当前AI仍高度依赖人类指导，远未实现端到端的自主科学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统的能力主要建立在学习、压缩和应用既有人类知识之上，现有基准也主要测试AI基于已知知识给出正确答案或在大量人类指导下完成任务的能力，缺乏对AI创新探索和自主科学执行能力的联合评估。为迈向人工超级智能，需要一个新的基准来衡量AI能否探索未知、创造新知识并产出可验证的结果。

Method: ASI-Bench由超过40位专家构建，投入31,000+人工小时，包含覆盖11个科学领域的60个项目级研究任务。基准采用渐进式撤回人类方法论指导的设计：从完整方法指导到仅指定方法再到要求智能体自行确定方法，测试AI能否独立选择方法、开展研究并产生可验证结果。所有任务经过专家审查、AI辅助审计、沙盒执行和评分者验证。

Result: 在18种先进的智能体-模型配置上，随着方法论指导的逐步撤回，平均得分从50.91（完整方法指导）降至29.10（仅指定方法），再降至26.62（完全自主确定方法），呈现显著下降趋势，反映了AI系统对人类指导的高度依赖。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，当前最先进的AI系统在消除人类方法论指导后表现大幅下滑，尚无法自主进行端到端的项目级科学研究，距离人工超级智能仍存在显著差距。ASI-Bench已对外开放，邀请全球研究者贡献新任务、挑战当前AI的极限，加速通向人工超级智能的进程。

Abstract: Artificial superintelligence (ASI) requires AI to move beyond mastering existing knowledge toward exploring the unknown, creating new knowledge, and turning new ideas into verifiable results. However, the capabilities of today's AI systems are still largely built on learning, compressing, and applying existing human knowledge. Accordingly, existing benchmarks primarily test whether AI can produce correct answers based on learned knowledge, or whether it can complete tasks under extensive human guidance. We therefore introduce ASI-Bench, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate AI systems' capabilities of innovative exploration and autonomous scientific execution across general research domains, and the first to progressively withdraw human methodological guidance within the same research project to test how far AI can proceed on its own. Built by over 40 experts with the cost of 31,000+ human hours, ASI-Bench contains 60 project-level research tasks across 11 scientific domains and progressively reduces methodological guidance to test whether AI can independently select methods, conduct research, and produce verifiable results. All tasks undergo expert review, AI-assisted auditing, sandbox execution, and scorer validation. Across 18 state-of-the-art agent--model configurations, the average score drops from 50.91 with full methodological guidance to 29.10 with only the method specified and 26.62 when agents must determine the method themselves. This sharp decline shows that current systems remain heavily dependent on human guidance and are still far from autonomously conducting end-to-end, project-level scientific research. ASI-Bench is open to the world. We invite researchers and builders everywhere to contribute new tasks, challenge the limits of today's AI, and help accelerate humanity's collective path toward artificial superintelligence at https://asibench.apexin.ai/submit.

</details>


### [256] [DeAR: Decentralized Agentic Reasoning via Capability Grounding and Collaborative Thought Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17282)
*Xing Wei,Changmeng Zheng,XiaoYong Wei,Xiufen Ye,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeAR提出了一种去中心化的智能体推理框架，通过自主对等协作替代传统集中式协议，在9个多模态推理和文本问答基准上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推理系统依赖集中式协议，存在路由瓶颈和静态角色分配问题，在处理复杂多模态查询时表现不佳，亟需更灵活的自适应协作机制。

Method: DeAR框架包含三个核心机制：(1) 去中心化能力定位，实现基于查询的智能体专业化分工；(2) 思维图导航，用于目标导向的对等智能体交互；(3) 拓扑更新，支持自适应的错误修正。

Result: 在9个多样化的多模态推理和基于文本的问答基准测试中，DeAR一致性地优于近期基线方法，验证了去中心化和自适应智能体协作能提升知识密集型推理任务的准确性。

Conclusion: 去中心化的自适应智能体协作框架（DeAR）能有效提升复杂多模态推理任务的性能，为智能体推理系统的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Existing agentic reasoning systems typically rely on centralized protocols. This design introduces routing bottlenecks and static role allocations that often fail when handling complex multimodal queries. We propose DeAR (Decentralized Agentic Reasoning), a framework that shifts from central control to autonomous peer-to-peer collaboration. DeAR is built on three mechanisms: (1) decentralized capability grounding for query-dependent agent specialization, (2) thought map navigation for targeted peer interactions, and (3) topology update for adaptive error correction. Evaluations across 9 diverse multimodal reasoning and text-based QA benchmarks indicate that DeAR consistently outperforms recent baseline methods, validating that decentralized and adaptive collaboration among agents enhances accuracy in knowledge-intensive reasoning tasks. The source code will be available at https://open_upon_acceptance.

</details>


### [257] [PlanPO: Group Planning-Aware Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17289)
*Dayang Liang,Liyuan He,Xuan Feng,Shuxin Li,Bo An,Yunlong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanPO是一种基于组相对策略优化的强化学习方法，通过粗到细的优势信号（显式区分轨迹级和回合级长度差异）来学习通用规划能力，在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld等多轮交互基准上平均超越GRPO 27.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组相对策略优化变体无法区分成功轨迹之间的优势差异，即使这些轨迹在交互效率上差异显著（例如绕远路的成功结果与直接的成功结果获得相同奖励），导致优势坍缩和性能瓶颈。

Method: PlanPO引入粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的优势信号：在轨迹级别捕获长度相对差异，在回合级别识别响应长度差异，两者均在成功轨迹条件下计算。在组相对优化结构内，同时编码交互规划与文本生成维度的优势信号，且避免退化为朴素的长度最小化。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld三个多轮交互基准上，PlanPO平均比GRPO提升27.2%，优于近期强基线方法，且训练额外开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过区分成功轨迹间的效率差异，PlanPO能够促使智能体从高质量rollout中主动学习通用且深思熟虑的行为，涵盖交互规划与文本生成两个层面，实现超越任务特定行为模式的泛化规划能力。

Abstract: Group-relative policy optimization has emerged as a key paradigm for training agentic large language models (LLMs) on multi-turn interactive tasks. However, most existing variants fail to distinguish advantages among successful trajectories even when these trajectories differ substantially in their interaction efficiency. For instance, circuitous successes are often assigned the identical outcome reward, causing advantage collapse and severe performance bottlenecks. To this end, we propose Group Planning-aware Policy Optimization (PlanPO), a simple yet effective RL method for learning generalizable planning abilities beyond task-specific high-quality behavior patterns. Specifically, PlanPO introduces coarse-to-fine advantage signals, which capture the relative differences in trajectory-level lengths and turn-level response lengths conditioned on successful trajectories sampled for the same task. Within the group-relative optimization structure, this enables agents to actively learn generalizable and deliberate behaviors spanning interaction planning and textual generation from high-quality rollouts, without degenerating into vanilla length minimization. Experimentally, PlanPO improves over GRPO by 27.2\% on average across the challenging multi-turn benchmarks ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, outperforming recent powerful baselines while incurring negligible additional training cost.

</details>


### [258] [LiveHouse-TS: An Open-world Living Benchmark for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17299)
*Haomin Wen,Ziyu Zhou,Qingxiang Liu,Siru Zhong,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LiveHouse-TS，首个面向时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）的开放世界实时基准测试基础设施，通过前序式（prequential）评估在真实未来数据上持续检验模型表现，取代传统静态快照式基准，以捕捉模型在季节变化、分布偏移和突发事件等动态环境中的长期时效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSFM评估协议依赖固定历史测试窗口的静态基准，仅能评估模型在固定历史数据上的平均性能，无法反映模型在持续演变的真实世界环境中的行为，包括季节变化、分布偏移和突发事件等动态因素。

Method: 构建LiveHouse-TS开放世界实时基准基础设施，采用前序式（prequential）评估协议，在开放世界环境中对真实未来数据进行持续流式评估，将基准从快照精度转向连续时间有效性，而非一次性排行榜。

Result: 在11个领域、17个数据集上的大规模流式评估表明，静态排名在实时协议下会发生剧烈重排，说明静态基准无法反映模型在动态环境中的真实鲁棒性和长期表现。

Conclusion: LiveHouse-TS作为一个连续的时间序列基础设施，旨在探索长期科学问题（如模型排名能否长期维持、哪些模型在分布偏移下真正鲁棒），弥补静态基准与真实世界动态环境之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have recently emerged as a highly promising paradigm for cross-domain zero-shot forecasting. However, existing evaluation protocols predominantly rely on static benchmarks with fixed historical test windows. While these benchmarks provide a valuable baseline snapshot, they evaluate an average performance on a fixed history, failing to capture how models behave in continuously evolving real-world environments characterized by seasonal variations, distribution shifts, and unexpected events. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiveHouse-TS, the first open-world living benchmark infrastructure for TSFMs. By evaluating models prequentially on real future data in open-world environments, LiveHouse-TS shifts time series benchmarking from snapshot accuracy to continuous temporal validity. Rather than acting as a one-off leaderboard, our infrastructure serves as a continuous time series infrastructure designed to explore vital, long-term scientific questions: Can model rankings be maintained over the long term? Which models remain genuinely robust under distribution shifts? Extensive streaming evaluations across 11 domains with 17 datasets demonstrate that static rankings undergo a dramatic reshuffling under a live protocol.

</details>


### [259] [SignalReasoner: Assessing the Upper Bound of 3B Models for Signal Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17301)
*Guozheng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究将强化微调策略应用于无线通信领域的数学推理问题。在WirelessMATHBench-XL基准上，对比了两种训练范式（直接RL vs SFT+RL）和三种策略优化算法（GRPO、GSPO、GMPO），最佳模型准确率达39.12%，较未训练的基座模型提升三倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然监督式思维链微调和基于可验证奖励的强化学习已显著提升LLM的数学推理能力，但这些方法在信号处理问题上的应用仍相对未被充分探索。作者希望填补这一空白，考察强化微调策略在领域特定数学推理任务上的效果。

Method: 以Qwen2.5-3B-Base为基座模型，在WirelessMATHBench-XL基准上开展两种训练范式：(i) 直接基于可验证奖励的强化学习；(ii) 先在蒸馏的无线领域思维链语料上进行监督微调，再进行同领域的RL。同时对比GRPO、GSPO和GMPO三种策略优化算法在稳定性与准确率上的表现。

Result: 最佳模型取得39.12%的总体准确率，相较未训练的基座模型（12.37%）实现了超过三倍的提升。

Conclusion: 领域感知的思维链SFT有望作为后续RL的有效初始化，且需要进一步评估GSPO与GMPO在信号推理任务上的稳定性与准确性优势。

Abstract: Post-training with supervised chain-of-thought fine-tuning and reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards has substantially improved the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, their application to signal processing problems remains relatively under-explored. This report investigates reinforcement fine-tuning strategies for adapting Qwen2.5-3B-Base to graduate-level signal mathematical problems from WirelessMATHBench-XL, a comprehensive benchmark for mathematical reasoning in this domain. We examine two training paradigms: (i) direct reinforcement learning (RL) on WirelessMATHBench-XL with verifiable rewards; and (ii) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a distilled wireless-domain chain-of-thought corpus, followed by the same domain-specific RL stage. Across both paradigms, we benchmark Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), and Geometric-Mean Policy Optimization (GMPO). We aim to assess whether domain-aware CoT SFT serves as an effective initialization for subsequent RL, and whether GSPO or GMPO offer advantages in stability or accuracy over GRPO for signal reasoning tasks. Our best model achieves an overall accuracy of 39.12\%, representing a more than threefold improvement over the untrained Base model (12.37\%).

</details>


### [260] [Wuying-Browser-Agent: Real-World Centric Fundamental Long-Horizon Browser Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17319)
*AIMAE Team,Tianxiang Chen,Yan Cheng,Zhangye Han,Xiaowei Li,Chang Liu,Cheng Liu,Zhongqiang Ma,Long Peng,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Hongliang Wei,Chen Wu,Daiping Xin,Kunyu Zhou,Pengyang Zhou,Peiyuan Chen,Ziyuan Chen,Yutao Deng,Chunyu Dong,Xiangyu Fu,Yicheng Feng,Ruian He,Haochen Li,Miancan Liu,Zhengqin Liu,Wei Peng,Jinkui Ren,Haoyu Tan,Dong Xiao,Rongkun Xue,Shujian Yang,Xianhang Ye,Ziqi Yuan,Ziyang Yu,Linghan Zhang,Xiantao Zhang,Xuanpu Zhao,Yinan Zhao,Zhenghui Zhao,Bin Zhu,Likai Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wuying-Browser-Agent框架，从执行、监督、优化和评估四个层面系统性地对齐真实浏览器部署场景，并引入BrowserBench全新基准，在多个浏览器基准上达到开源SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浏览器智能体在短小干净的演示任务上表现良好，但真实部署中需要持续数十步决策、从错误中恢复并应对复杂UI。现有方法仅依赖规模扩展不足以弥合这一差距，需要在整个pipeline中对齐真实部署场景。

Method: 提出统一框架Wuying-Browser-Agent，包含四个层面的对齐：(1) 执行层：结构化浏览器harness提供稳定执行原语和面向决策的上下文管理；(2) 监督层：Reflection和UI特化的Curriculum SFT（RUIC-SFT）显式训练恢复轨迹和复杂UI交互；(3) 优化层：Divergence-Aware Online GRPO（DAO-GRPO），通过基于势能的奖励塑形和分歧感知的步骤加权改善长程信用分配；(4) 评估层：提出BrowserBench，包含350个双语真实网页任务，平均37.9步。

Result: Wuying-Browser-Agent-27B在WebVoyager上达到80.6%，在Online-Mind2Web上达到66.7%，在BrowserBench上达到65.1%，建立开源SOTA。该pipeline还展现出超越浏览器场景的通用agent能力，在Tau2-Bench、Claw-Eval和BFCL-v4上平均得分73.8。

Conclusion: 在浏览器智能体pipeline的每个层面（执行、监督、优化、评估）进行系统性对齐，而非仅依赖规模扩展，是提升真实部署性能的关键。该框架不仅在浏览器基准上达到开源SOTA，且展现出可迁移的通用agent能力。

Abstract: Browser agents perform well on short, clean demonstrations, but real deployment is fundamentally different: agents must sustain dozens of decisions on live websites while recovering from mistakes and navigating complex UIs. We argue that closing this gap requires alignment at every level of the pipeline, including execution, supervision, optimization, and evaluation, rather than scale alone. We present Wuying-Browser-Agent, a unified framework that addresses each of these levels. A structured browser harness provides stable execution primitives and decision-oriented context management. Reflection and UI-specialized Curriculum SFT (RUIC-SFT) explicitly trains on recovery trajectories and complex-UI interactions. Divergence-Aware Online GRPO (DAO-GRPO) improves long-horizon credit assignment through potential-based reward shaping and divergence-aware step weighting. Finally, we introduce BrowserBench, a bilingual real-web benchmark of 350 tasks averaging 37.9 steps, because most existing benchmarks are too short to expose long-horizon failure modes. Wuying-Browser-Agent-27B achieves 80.6\% on WebVoyager, 66.7\% on Online-Mind2Web, and 65.1\% on BrowserBench, establishing a new open-source state of the art on browser-use benchmarks. The same pipeline also transfers beyond browser use, demonstrating strong general agentic ability and reaching an average score of 73.8 on Tau2-Bench, Claw-Eval, and BFCL-v4.

</details>


### [261] [LLMs for Medical Consultation Are Evaluated Too Late: The Preformulation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17330)
*Yining Hua,Cyrus Ayubcha,Hongbin Na,Levi Lian,Alon Gorenshtein,Yiftach Barash,Eyal Klang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了三个API大语言模型在医疗咨询场景中的"预表述阶段"（preformulation）表现，通过基线条件和"进入照护"指令条件对照，发现指令能显著改变模型行为：减少在患者未回答前就给出自我照护建议（从9/12降到0/12），增加结构化交接摘要生成（从0/12提升到10/12），但指令并未可靠确保关键事实的引出。研究者主张应通过可观察的首触行为直接评估预表述差距，而非通过诊断准确性或最终回答质量间接推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型医疗咨询评估通常假设临床问题已明确陈述，但真实咨询常以模糊、弱化或错误框架的主诉开始。这种"预表述差距"（preformulation gap）被现有评估方法忽视，研究者希望直接评估这一关键阶段的行为表现。

Method: 研究使用三个API模型，基于四个医生撰写、多轮对话的临床场景，设置基线和"进入照护"指令两种条件。共生成24个固定脚本转录（3模型×4场景×2条件）；其中两个场景还使用自适应标准化患者模拟，额外产生12个转录。比较两种条件下模型在患者给出任何回答之前提供自我照护/家庭管理建议的频率，以及结构化交接摘要的出现率。

Result: 基线条件下，12个基线病例-模型单元中有9个在患者回答前即给出自我照护或家庭管理建议；指令条件下该数字降至0/12。结构化交接摘要在基线条件下为0/12，指令条件下升至10/12。然而，指令并未可靠地确保模型引出决定性临床事实。

Conclusion: "进入照护"指令能显著改善模型在初诊阶段的交互顺序和文档记录，说明通过指令可以调控模型的首触行为。但预表述差距仍需通过可观察的首触行为直接评估，因为最终诊断准确性或回答质量无法可靠反映这一阶段的缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models for medical consultation are often evaluated after a clinical problem has already been made clear, although real consultations may begin with a vague, minimized, or misframed concern. We evaluated three API models across four physician-authored, multi-turn vignettes under baseline and entry-to-care instruction conditions, yielding 24 fixed-script transcripts; two cases also used adaptive standardized-patient simulation, yielding 12 transcripts. Self-care or home-management advice before any patient answer appeared in 9 of 12 baseline case-model cells and 0 of 12 instruction cells, while structured handoff summaries appeared in 0 of 12 and 10 of 12 cells, respectively. The instruction changed sequencing and documentation, although it did not reliably ensure elicitation of decisive facts. The preformulation gap should therefore be evaluated directly through observable first-contact behavior rather than inferred from diagnostic accuracy or final-answer quality.

</details>


### [262] [TileMix: Tile-Centric Mixed-Precision Attention for LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17336)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TileMix是一种面向融合密集注意力（fused dense attention）的基于tile粒度的精度路由内核，将数值精度作为可执行的空间决策，按硬件对齐的score tile组在FP16和INT8两条计算路径之间动态分配，在保持密集token连接、无需训练的前提下，恢复长上下文质量并提升prefill吞吐，形成可控的精度-效率权衡前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM长上下文prefill阶段因密集自注意力的二次方query-key计算而面临巨大的计算量和内存流量。现有方法要么使用统一的低精度路径，要么选择性地忽略部分token交互，均未能在融合密集注意力内部对硬件对齐的score tile实施空间粒度的精度路由。这导致精度分配缺乏灵活性，难以在保持质量的同时提升吞吐。

Method: TileMix将注意力矩阵划分为硬件对齐的score tile，将路由决策压缩为紧凑的bitmask，每个tile组通过FP16或INT8两条路径计算score，且两条路径共享在线softmax状态。通过可扩展的精度分组，每个路由bit可控制多个相邻key tile，在长上下文中保持硬件对齐的计算tile和紧凑元数据。TileMix路由所有合法tile组以保持密集token连接，无需训练，支持GQA、变长批处理以及INT8 key/value缓存。

Result: 在LongEval、LV-Eval和A100 prefill基准上，针对LLaMA、Qwen和Vicuna模型，TileMix恢复了在统一INT8下丢失的长上下文质量，并相比FP16提升了prefill吞吐，在不同模型家族上实现了可控的精度-效率权衡前沿。

Conclusion: TileMix首次在融合密集注意力内实现了patch级（tile-centric）的精度路由，证明精度可以被视为一种可执行的空间决策而非全局参数。该方法在保持密集注意力语义、无需训练的前提下同时获得质量恢复和吞吐提升，为长上下文LLM的高效推理提供了新的优化维度。

Abstract: Long-context prefill in large language models (LLMs) incurs substantial computation and memory traffic because dense self-attention computes quadratic query-key scores. Existing methods either use a uniform low-precision path or select token interactions, leaving spatial precision routing over hardware-aligned score tiles outside fused dense attention. We introduce TileMix, a tile-centric precision-routing kernel that makes numerical precision an executable spatial decision over score-tile groups within fused dense attention. TileMix partitions the attention matrix into hardware-aligned score tiles, packs routing decisions into compact bitmasks, and dispatches each tile group through FP16 or INT8 score computation while both paths update a shared online-softmax state. Scalable precision grouping lets each routing bit govern multiple adjacent key tiles, preserving hardware-aligned compute tiles and compact metadata at long contexts. By routing all legal tile groups, TileMix preserves dense token connectivity, requires no training, and supports grouped-query attention, variable-length batches, and INT8 key/value caches. Across LongEval, LV-Eval, and A100 prefill benchmarks on LLaMA, Qwen, and Vicuna, TileMix recovers long-context quality lost under uniform INT8 and improves prefill throughput over FP16, yielding a controllable accuracy-efficiency frontier across model families. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TileMix.

</details>


### [263] [LLM-Only PDDL Domain Repair with Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17341)
*Nader Karimi Bavandpour,Pascal Bercher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了开源大语言模型在PDDL模型自动修复任务上的能力。实验表明，最强LLM在F1分数上达到0.87，相比符号基线（0.49）有明显提升，但在测试通过率上仅达0.82，且在最难的Thoughtful域上暴跌至0.06，即便加入测试轨迹的最佳设置也仅达0.92，说明当前开源模型无法保证满足测试约束，难以实现可靠的自动化模型修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI规划依赖PDDL等显式世界模型，而模型中的错误需要被检测和修复。已有自动化修复方法研究，但作者希望评估近年开源大语言模型能否独立完成这一模型修复任务。

Method: 采用LLM-only方法，让多个开源权重的大语言模型直接执行PDDL模型修复任务，并与符号基线方法进行对比；实验设置了不同推理努力程度及是否包含测试轨迹等多种条件进行系统评估。

Result: 符号基线F1为0.49；最佳LLM在高推理努力下F1达到0.87，绝对提升0.38。但该设置的测试通过率均值仅0.82，在Thoughtful域上降至0.06；包含测试轨迹的最佳设置测试通过率也仅0.92。

Conclusion: 尽管开源大语言模型在F1分数上大幅超越符号基线，但在关键的安全指标（测试约束满足率）上表现不足，无法保证可靠地完成自动化PDDL模型修复，仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: AI planning is concerned with finding a sequence of actions that achieves a specified goal. It relies on explicit models of the world, commonly represented in the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL). An active line of research investigates how errors in such models can be detected and repaired. For example, users may provide positive test plans that are solutions, and negative test plans that fail during execution. Automated repair methods then modify the PDDL model to satisfy these constraints. In this paper, we evaluate the ability of recent open-weight large language models to perform this repair task using an LLM-only approach. Our experiments show that the symbolic baseline achieves an $F_1$ score of $.49$, while the best-performing LLM reaches $.87$ with high reasoning effort, an absolute improvement of $.38$. However, that setting has a mean test pass rate of only $.82$, falling to $.06$ on the Thoughtful domain; even the best setting that includes the test traces reaches only $.92$. Thus, current open-weight models cannot guarantee satisfaction of the test constraints required for reliable automated model repair.

</details>


### [264] [Cognitive Graph Intelligence for Adaptive and Robust DDoS Attack Detection in Next Generation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17352)
*Mohammad Arif Hossain,Yeahia Sarker,Md Jafrin Hossain,Most. Humayra Khanom Rime,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图的生成对抗网络（GraphGAN），作为DDoS攻击的认知检测引擎。GraphGAN通过滑动窗口将时序流量转换为k近邻图，利用生成器合成少数类样本以解决类不平衡问题，并采用GCN判别器和分类器实现检测。在四个基准数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDoS攻击威胁网络可用性，需要具备感知流量、推断意图和自适应响应的认知检测过程。然而，该类检测面临严重的类别不平衡和非平稳条件，现有方法在数据稀缺场景下难以有效建模协调攻击行为。

Method: GraphGAN由三部分构成：（1）利用滑动窗口将时序流量流转换为k近邻图，以保留特征相似性和时间依赖关系；（2）生成器学习DDoS攻击的数据分布以合成逼真的少数类样本，缓解类别不平衡；（3）基于GCN的判别器区分真实图与合成图数据；（4）另设一个在平衡数据集上训练的GCN分类器执行最终检测决策。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的评估表明，GraphGAN相比现有最先进方法在准确率、精确率和召回率上均表现更优，尤其在数据稀缺场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: GraphGAN通过整合时序图构建、对抗性数据增强和GCN分类，有效建模了协调攻击行为并缓解了类别不平衡，为数据受限环境中的入侵检测提供了鲁棒且拓扑感知的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks threaten network availability, requiring a cognitive detection process that senses traffic, infers intent, and supports an adaptive response under severe class imbalance and non-stationary conditions. This paper proposes a Graph-based Generative Adversarial Network (GraphGAN) that serves as the cognitive detection engine for this task. GraphGAN captures the relational structure among traffic flows while addressing imbalance through adversarial generation of synthetic samples. Sequential flows are converted into $k$-nearest neighbor graphs using sliding windows to preserve feature-similarity and temporal dependencies among flows. The generator learns the distribution of DDoS attacks to synthesize realistic minority samples, while a Graph Convolutional Network (GCN)-based discriminator distinguishes real from synthetic graph data. A separate GCN classifier, trained on the balanced dataset, performs the final detection decision. Evaluations on four benchmark datasets show that GraphGAN achieves superior accuracy, precision, and recall compared to state-of-the-art approaches, particularly in data-scarce scenarios. By integrating temporal graph construction, adversarial augmentation, and GCN classification, GraphGAN effectively models coordinated attack behaviors and mitigates class imbalance, providing a robust and topology-aware solution for intrusion detection in data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [265] [LEGO-RL: Harness-Native Reinforcement Learning for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17393)
*Yiming Du,Yuxin Jiang,Tao Yuan,Jianbo Dai,Shaowei Wang,Jierun Chen,Chaofan Tao,Xianzhi Yu,Lifeng Shang,Kam-Fai Wong,Xiaohui Li,Haoli Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEGO-RL是一个连接原生编码Agent框架与可扩展策略梯度优化的框架，通过进程内LLM代理、可扩展沙箱编排和可观测训练三大支柱，在不修改内部控制流的前提下解决环境崩溃、奖励黑客和训练推理不一致问题，在SWE-bench Verified上显著提升Qwen3.5-35B-A3B在三个编码框架上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码Agent的强化学习越来越依赖长时间运行的Agent框架来管理工具集成、仓库上下文和执行反馈，但这些框架的原生执行环境与策略梯度训练存在本质错配：环境崩溃和奖励黑客会污染结果信号，训练推理差异导致rollout行为与策略更新脱节，阻碍了高效可靠的政策梯度优化。

Method: LEGO-RL基于三大支柱：(1) 忠实优化：通过进程内LLM代理捕获原始生成流，实现token级对齐和在训练端稳健的对数概率重计算，即使框架侧有压缩或重新序列化也能保持一致性；(2) 可靠执行：可扩展沙箱编排，具备镜像缓存和分阶段防御机制以缓解奖励黑客；(3) 可观测训练：集成插件自动化验证与监控，配合Live UI进行细粒度轨迹诊断。

Result: 使用GSPO训练稀疏MoE模型Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，在三个原生编码Agent框架上均取得显著提升：OpenHands SDK从64.0%提升至70.4%，Claude Code从62.4%提升至68.2%，OpenCode从57.2%提升至66.6%（SWE-bench Verified），同时保持rollout训练概率相关性高于0.99。

Conclusion: LEGO-RL有效弥合了原生编码Agent框架与可扩展策略梯度优化之间的鸿沟，在不修改框架内部控制流的前提下实现了忠实的强化学习优化、可靠的执行环境和透明的训练观测，显著提升了编码Agent在多个框架上的代码生成与问题解决能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for coding agents increasingly relies on long-running agent harnesses to manage tool integration, repository contexts, and execution feedback. However, the native execution environments of these harnesses are inherently misaligned with policy-gradient training: environmental crashes and reward hacking corrupt outcome signals, while train-inference discrepancies decouple rollout behavior from policy updates. To address this, we present LEGO-RL, a framework that bridges native coding-agent harnesses with scalable policy-gradient optimization without modifying their internal control flow. LEGO-RL is built upon three pillars: (1) faithful optimization via in-process LLM proxying that captures raw generation streams for token-level alignment and robust trainer-side log-probability recomputation, even under harness-side compaction or re-serialization; (2) reliable execution via scalable sandbox orchestration featuring image caching and stage-wise defenses to mitigate reward hacking; and (3) observable training through an integrated plugin that automates validation and monitoring, paired with a Live UI for granular trajectory diagnostics. We evaluate LEGO-RL by training the sparse MoE model Qwen3.5-35B-A3B with GSPO across three native coding-agent harnesses. LEGO-RL improves Qwen3.5-35B-A3B across OpenHands SDK (64.0% to 70.4%), Claude Code (62.4% to 68.2%), and OpenCode (57.2% to 66.6%) on SWE-bench Verified, while maintaining a rollout-training probability correlation above 0.99.

</details>


### [266] [Task-Aware Harness Provisioning for LLM Agents in Mission-Critical Infrastructure Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17433)
*Liangtao Lin,Qingang Zhang,Zhaomeng Zhu,Tianwei Zhang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体在关键基础设施（MCI）中的最佳harness配置问题，将其建模为任务需求与harness提供的资源匹配问题，并提出了基于映射的渐进式harness配置算法（map-guided escalation），在液冷和电网两个MCI任务中验证了该方法在准确率和成本之间的权衡优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
