Large Language Models (LLMs), which excel at language reasoning, bring a promising solution to this issue. However, applying LLMs in this domain presents key challenges such as modal inconsistency, limited classification ability, scarcity of task-specific data for fine-tuning, and lack of domain knowledge. To overcome these challenges, we propose FlightLLM, a prior-guided semantic LLM-based approach for interpretable flight safety analysis. Specifically, we first perform feature engineering to address modal inconsistency, combining statistical descriptors with physically meaningful flight indicators. This representation is further processed by a Semantic Discretization module, which converts abstract numerical patterns into qualitative descriptions that are more compatible with language reasoning. In addition, since LLMs are not inherently strong classifiers, CatBoost is incorporated as a statistical expert, and its prediction results are injected into the prompt as prior guidance. A contrastive few-shot learning strategy is further adopted to compensate for limited data. Finally, we design structured prompts to embed aviation-specific knowledge into the inference process. Using hard landing, a representative risk event with complex causal mechanisms, as an anchor point, we evaluate FlightLLM on a dataset of 704 real-world A320 flight samples. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves competitive classification performance while generating direct and reasonable explanations for event causes.

</details>


### [299] [On the Fragility of Self-Improving Agents: Variance, Task Order, and Underspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18066)
*Qinyuan Ye,Yu Li,Yada Pruksachatkun,Jiaxin Zhang,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了基于记忆的自我改进智能体（memory-based self-improving agents），发现当前方法存在两方面脆弱性：评估噪声大且随任务顺序波动。研究通过多轮运行和任务随机打乱实验揭示问题，并假设任务与环境的欠规格化（underspecification）是根本原因，通过引入更详细的规格信息（如评分标准和环境反馈）部分缓解了性能退化，但差距仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆型自我改进智能体虽然在文献中表现优异，但其可靠性方面被严重忽视。作者旨在系统性地研究这些方法是否稳定可靠，而非仅仅关注其平均性能提升。

Method: 作者沿两个维度扩展评估范围：(1) 多次运行以量化方差；(2) 随机打乱任务顺序以研究任务顺序的影响。随后手动检查智能体的记忆内容，提出任务与环境欠规格化假设，并通过在记忆构建过程中引入详细评分标准和环境反馈等信息来验证该假设。

Result: 实验揭示两个关键发现：一是智能体评估在复杂环境和多步骤任务中本质上是嘈杂的，自我改进循环会进一步放大噪声；二是智能体的改进高度依赖任务顺序，先前的默认顺序隐含了一个课程学习式的隐藏前提条件。引入更好的规格信息部分弥补了性能退化，但仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁对自我改进智能体采用更严格的评估协议（跨多次运行报告结果、在挑战性条件下进行压力测试），并强调欠规格化问题需要有效的系统与接口支持人类监督，以防止智能体以不可预见的方式失败。

Abstract: Memory-based self-improving agents--those that learn from an online stream of tasks and improve over time by maintaining a textual memory bank--have shown great promise in recent literature. However, the reliability aspects of these methods have been critically overlooked. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive re-evaluation of two memory-based methods, broadening the scope of evaluation along two axes: (1) including multiple runs to quantify variance, and (2) randomly shuffling the tasks to investigate the effect of task order. Through these experiments, we make two observations that expose the fragility of current methods: First, agent evaluation is inherently noisy in complex environments and on multi-step tasks, and stacking a self-improving loop on top can further amplify this noise. Second, the agent's improvement is highly dependent on task order. Prior works often adopt default orderings that impose an implicit curriculum, acting as a hidden prerequisite for success.
  To better understand this fragility, we manually examine the agents' memory and hypothesize that task and environment underspecification contribute to this fragility. We validate this hypothesis by incorporating information that enables better specification, such as detailed rubrics and environment feedback, into the memory construction process. While this added information partially closes the performance degradation in previous experiments, significant gaps still remain, suggesting that other uncharacterized factors contribute to this fragility. Looking ahead, our work advocates for more rigorous evaluation protocols for self-improving agents by reporting results across multiple runs and stress-testing them under challenging conditions. Moreover, our findings on underspecification call for systems and interfaces that enable effective human oversight, preventing agents from failing in unforeseeable ways.

</details>


### [300] [StagedWorkspace: A Versioned Workspace for Knowledge-Work Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18050)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Yifan Zhou,Akshay Kalose,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StagedWorkspace，一个针对知识工作智能体（处理代码库、文档、电子表格、幻灯片等持久化数字产物的agent）的版本化工作空间。该工作空间将解析后的视图和差异审查绑定到原生文件的内容哈希上，解决"视图引用的版本与实际编辑的版本不一致"这一工作空间状态契约问题。实验表明双解析/原生访问在OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents上均优于单一视图，SW-AGENT分别达到63.9%（Gemini 3.1 Pro）和42.1（GPT-5.4 Nano），远超已发布的同模型基线（29.3%和25.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识工作智能体在执行任务时，搜索使用的解析视图、编辑的原生文件、审查的变更以及提交的产物可能引用同一工作产品的不同版本，导致工作空间状态不一致。编程智能体已通过代码仓库契约（search/diffs/tests）部分解决此问题，但PDF、电子表格、幻灯片、笔记本和混合格式项目文件夹缺乏类似的显式契约。

Method: 提出StagedWorkspace版本化工作空间，将解析记录（parsed records）和审查差异（review diffs）绑定到原生文件的内容哈希。当原生文件变化时，哈希随之更新，确保所有视图显式关联到工作空间状态的特定版本。在固定框架消融实验中比较双解析/原生访问与单一视图，并在57个文件编辑任务上进行配对审查轴消融实验验证diff可见性的效果。

Result: 双解析/原生访问在所有测试模型上取得最高点估计：相对于单一视图，OfficeQA Pass@1提升8.3-12.1个点，APEX平均rubric得分提升4.7-9.2个点。SW-AGENT在OfficeQA上达到63.9%（Gemini 3.1 Pro），在APEX上达到42.1（GPT-5.4 Nano），对比已发布的同模型基线29.3%和25.5。57个文件编辑任务的配对消融显示diff可见时得分更高。

Conclusion: 工作空间状态（workspace state）应被视为知识工作智能体的一个实验变量。研究建议基准测试应将对证据（evidence）、分批编辑（staged edits）和提交产物（submitted artifacts）的评分作为显式的状态转换来设计。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform knowledge work (i.e., produce and modify persistent digital artifacts such as code repositories, documents, spreadsheets, slides, reports), yet the parsed views they search, the native files they edit, the changes they review, and the artifacts they submit can refer to different versions of the same work product. We formulate this as a workspace-state contract: every view should be explicitly tied to a version of the evolving workspace state. Coding agents partly address this need through repository contracts for search, diffs, and tests, whereas an analogous contract is less explicit for PDFs, spreadsheets, slides, notebooks, and mixed-format project folders. We propose StagedWorkspace, a versioned workspace for knowledge-work agents. The workspace binds parsed records and review diffs to content hashes of the native files as they change. In fixed-harness ablations on OfficeQA Pro and APEX-Agents, dual parsed/native access has the highest point estimate for every tested model; relative to the more limiting single view, it improves OfficeQA Pass@1 by 8.3-12.1 points and APEX mean rubric score by 4.7-9.2 points. SW-AGENT scores 63.9% with Gemini 3.1 Pro on OfficeQA and 42.1 with GPT-5.4 Nano on APEX, compared with published same-model scores of 29.3% and 25.5, respectively. A paired review-axis ablation on 57 file-editing tasks further finds higher observed scores when diffs are visible. These results identify workspace state as an experimental variable in knowledge-work agents and motivate benchmarks that score evidence, staged edits, and submitted artifacts as explicit state transitions.

</details>


### [301] [HLSR: Hybrid Live Forecast Selective Dynamic Vehicle Rerouting for Real-Time Congestion Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18056)
*Xiao Wang,Shun Ren Yang,Hui Nien Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HLSR框架，一种选择性混合实时-预测车辆重路由方法，通过融合实时边速度和短期预测，在有限干预范围内缓解城市交通拥堵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全网络实时最短路径重路由在仿真中效果显著，但假设每辆道路车辆在每个决策周期都被重新规划，这在实际中不现实。需要在有限干预范围内实现有效的网络级重路由。

Method: HLSR框架包含：双阈值拥堵检测、校准的上游路段选择、驾驶员定制化的行程时间预测，以及三个新组件：接近车辆扩展、行程时间加权的k最短路径生成、以及基于时域依赖的混合实时-预测段速度用于多成本路径分配。

Result: 通过选择性干预和混合速度预测，在有限重路由范围内接近全网络重路由的效果，同时降低对全部车辆进行重规划的依赖性。

Conclusion: HLSR证明了在有限的干预范围内通过融合实时与预测交通信息进行选择性重路由，可以有效缓解城市交通拥堵，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion reduces productivity and increases travel cost and emissions. Network-wide live travel-time shortest-path rerouting can be highly effective in simulation, but assumes that essentially every on-road vehicle is replanned every decision period. We propose HLSR, a selective hybrid live--forecast vehicle rerouting framework that fuses live edge speeds with short-horizon forecasts under limited intervention scope. Building on dual-threshold congestion detection, calibrated upstream selection, and driver-tailored travel-time prediction, HLSR further introduces approaching-vehicle expansion, travel-time-weighted k-shortest-path generation, and a horizon-dependent hybrid live--forecast segment speed used in multi-cost route allocation.

</details>


### [302] [Delegation Asymmetry in Agentic Recommender Systems: Measuring Two-Sided Receptivity in Online Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18058)
*Daria Leshchikova,Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Valerii Klimov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过两项大规模调查（N=2,894和N=2,617）研究了约会平台上用户对自主LLM代理代聊与接收代理通信的双向接受度，开发了基于分级响应模型和潜回归的潜变量测量模型，发现"发送意愿"与"接收意愿"是高度相关但可分离的构念，并量化了系统性委托不对称性：部署自己的代理所需接受阈值远低于与对方代理互动的阈值。随机配对反事实显示仅4-13%的有向二元组同时具备部署与接收意愿，而设计反事实表明基于接收接受度的路由可使每联系人参与度提高三倍（AUC 0.88）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM代理代表用户进行对话正在成为匹配平台的新兴设计模式，但其可行性取决于一个很少被检验的条件：用户不仅要接受将对话委托给代理，还要接受接收来自他人的代理中介通信。现有研究忽视了这一双向接受条件，本文旨在系统测量用户的"代理接受度"并量化其影响。

Method: 作者在一家大型约会平台对活跃用户开展两项大规模调查（分别针对生成式档案特征和自主对话代理，覆盖两种语言）。采用基于分级响应模型（graded response model）和潜回归（latent regression）的潜变量测量模型，通过模型比较验证"发送意愿"与"接收意愿"的结构可分性；进一步构建随机配对反事实和设计反事实（如互惠要求、基于接收接受度的路由）来量化不同机制的影响，并进行样本外验证（留出目标项、受访者级交叉验证，AUC 0.88）。

Result: 发送与接收意愿高度相关（rho=0.92）但可分离（Delta BIC=52），且跨语言存在部分测量不变性。系统量化了委托不对称性：部署自己代理的接受阈值（-0.38）远低于与对方代理互动的阈值（+0.32，完全参与+1.39），平均部署倾向约为参与倾向的三倍。随机配对下仅4-13%的有向二元组同时满足部署与接收条件，且存在明显的性别方向性失衡。互惠要求会使互动量减少一半以上（排除近三分之二的潜在部署），而基于接收接受度的路由使每联系人参与度提升三倍，该提升在样本外验证中稳健。

Conclusion: 自主代理代聊系统的可行性取决于双向接受度，而非仅单向的部署意愿。平台设计应纳入揭示机制、选择性加入（opt-in）机制以及接受度感知的匹配策略，以弥合委托-接收之间的不对称差距，从而提升代理中介通信的整体参与效率。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents that converse on a user's behalf are an emerging design pattern in matching platforms, yet their viability depends on a condition rarely examined: users must accept not only delegating conversation to an agent, but also receiving agent-mediated communication from others. We study this condition using two large-scale surveys of active users of a major dating platform (N=2,894 on generative profile features; N=2,617 on autonomous conversational agents, fielded in two languages). We develop a latent-variable measurement model of agent receptivity based on graded response models with latent regression, and show via model comparison that willingness to send and willingness to receive agent communication are distinct constructs: highly correlated (rho=0.92) but separable (Delta BIC=52), with partial measurement invariance across languages. The model quantifies a systematic delegation asymmetry: deploying one's own agent requires far lower receptivity (threshold -0.38) than engaging a counterpart's agent (+0.32; full engagement +1.39), and mean deployment propensity exceeds engagement propensity roughly threefold. Under a random-pairing counterfactual derived from stated receptivity, only 4-13% of directed dyads combine agent deployment with receiver engagement, with a pronounced gender-directional imbalance. Design counterfactuals quantify the levers: a reciprocity requirement cuts interaction volume by half or more by excluding nearly two-thirds of would-be deployment, while routing agent contacts on receive receptivity triples per-contact engagement, a lift that survives out-of-sample validation with the target item held out (AUC 0.88, 3.1x quartile lift under respondent-level cross-validation). We discuss implications for agentic recommender design, including disclosure, opt-in mechanics, and receptivity-aware matchmaking.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [303] [Expected free energy as an information constraint on the Bethe Lagrangian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17167)
*Wouter M. Kouw*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Bethe自由能泛函的主动推理替代框架，通过引入信息约束（要求未来观测、状态和参数与目标先验之间的互信息至少达到目标先验熵）来代替期望自由能（EFE）中缺乏Kullback-Leibler结构的问题，从而完全支持基于消息传递的推理。在该约束Bethe Lagrangian的KKT乘子取特定值时，其驻点可恢复EFE解。作者展示了随信息需求变化，乘子经历非激活、内部和饱和三个区间，并在三个任务上与EFE和Q-MDP进行了性能对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准主动推理通过最小化期望自由能泛函来选择动作，但对未观测结果取期望后，自由能函数失去Kullback-Leibler结构，阻碍了基于消息传递的推理过程求解。作者旨在提出一种新的公式化方法，在保持认知驱动（epistemic drive）的同时，恢复消息传递友好的结构。

Method: 提出基于Bethe自由能泛函的公式化，在标准化、边缘化和形式约束之外，增加一条信息约束：未来观测、状态和参数之间的互信息必须至少达到目标先验的熵。由此构建约束Bethe Lagrangian，研究其KKT乘子在不同信息需求下的行为，识别非激活、内部和饱和三个乘子机制。

Result: 当KKT乘子取特定值时，约束Bethe Lagrangian的驻点与EFE解一致。随信息需求变化，乘子在非激活（认知驱动完全关闭）、内部（部分驱动）和饱和（认知驱动最大化）三个区间移动。在三个任务上与EFE和Q-MDP基准进行了性能对比。

Conclusion: 该约束Bethe自由能公式化提供了一种支持完全消息传递推理的主动推理方案，能够保持认知驱动并通过KKT乘子的调节实现从无认知驱动力到最大认知驱动力之间的连续过渡，为主动推理的消息传递计算开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Active inference selects actions by minimising an expected free energy functional over predicted futures. However, adding an expectation over yet-unobserved outcomes means the free energy functional no longer has a Kullback-Leibler structure, which hinders message passing treatments of inference procedures. We propose an alternative formulation based on a Bethe free energy functional, fully supporting inference by message passing. The epistemic drive is maintained by imposing an information constraint, next to normalisation, marginalisation and form constraints, insisting that the mutual information between future observations, states and parameters given actions must be at least as large as the entropy of the goal prior. For a specific value of the corresponding Karush-Kuhn-Tucker multiplier, the stationary point of this constrained Bethe Lagrangian recovers the expected free energy solution. We show that, as the information demand is varied, the solved multiplier moves through its inactive, interior, and saturated regimes. In the inactive regime the agent's epistemic drive switches off entirely, while in the saturated regime it is maximal. We compare the performance of the constrained Bethe agent on three tasks against EFE and Q-MDP.

</details>


### [304] [Chi-Squared Geometry for Robust Finite-Blocklength Information and Dispersion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17305)
*Hassan Tavakoli,Thinh Nguyen,Bella Bose*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对离散无记忆信道(DMC)提出了一种列式卡方几何方法，利用关键参数η（转移概率相对于输出边缘的最坏相对偏差）推导出互信息、信道色散和有限块长编码速率的紧致无对数界，仅需基本算术运算即可完成认证计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的互信息、信道色散和有限块长编码速率界限通常需要计算信道的对数函数，增加了计算复杂度和数值不稳定性。特别是当信道接近完全噪声信道时，需要一种不依赖对数、且能提供紧致的封闭式界限的工具。为离散无记忆信道提供一种仅基于算术运算的、严格认证的界，是信道容量分析和极化码等应用中亟待解决的问题。

Method: 论文发展了一种列式卡方几何框架，核心参数η定义为转移概率与其输出边缘概率的最坏相对偏差，当信道接近完全噪声信道t_ij=s_j时η很小。基于此框架证明了三个主要结果：(1) 三阶比展开式，表明当η→0时I(X;Y)/χ²(X;Y)→1/2，并带有O(η)的偏度修正项；(2) 双向色散等价关系，用χ²(X;Y)以显式常数c±(η)→1上下夹逼V(X;Y)；(3) 确定性鲁棒设计速率R_cert(n,ε)，总认证间隙为O(η)+O(η/√n)+O(log n/n)。该方法的认证计算仅使用加法、乘法、除法和平方根，最终速率计算额外使用Q⁻¹(ε)。

Result: 证明了三个核心定理：互信息与卡方散度之比以O(η)偏度修正收敛到1/2；信道色散可由卡方散度以趋于1的显式常数上下夹逼；设计鲁棒速率的总认证间隙为O(η)+O(η/√n)+O(log n/n)。所有界限均无需计算信道矩阵的对数。

Conclusion: 该工作为近完全噪声信道提供了全新的、计算友好的分析框架，摆脱了对数运算依赖，使得认证界可以仅通过基本算术操作（以及Q⁻¹函数）获得，为有限块长信道编码的设计和分析提供了实用的紧致工具。

Abstract: We develop a column-wise chi-squared geometry for discrete memoryless channels (DMCs) yielding tight, logarithm-free bounds on mutual information, channel dispersion, and finite-blocklength coding rates without evaluating logarithms of the channel matrix. The key parameter is~\(η\)---the worst-case relative deviation of a transition probability from its output marginal, which is small precisely when the channel is close to the fully noisy channel $t_{ij}=s_j$. We prove three main results: (1) a third-order ratio expansion showing \(I(X;Y)/χ^2(X;Y)\to 1/2\) as \(η\to 0\) with an \(O(η)\) skewness correction; (2) a two-sided dispersion equivalence bounding \(V(X;Y)\) above and below by \(χ^2(X;Y)\) with explicit constants \(c_{\pm}(η)\to 1\); and (3) a certified robust design rate \(R_{\mathrm{cert}}(n,\varepsilon)\) with total certification gap \(O(η)+O(η/\sqrt{n})+O(\log n/n)\). The certified bounds on \(I\) and \(V\) require only addition, multiplication, division, and square roots; the final rate also uses \(Q^{-1}(\varepsilon)\).

</details>


### [305] [Universal CKM for Environment-Aware Wireless Networks: Enabling Cross-Device and Cross-Task Channel Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17382)
*Haiquan Lu,Yong Zeng,Cheng-Xiang Wang,Xiqi Gao,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出通用信道知识图（uCKM）的概念，作为一种基础无线环境先验，旨在解决现有CKM与设备和任务紧密耦合、可扩展性和可重用性受限的问题，实现跨设备、跨任务的信道知识迁移，并提出了"All for uCKM"和"uCKM for All"的构建与利用愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CKM大多与特定无线设备和下游任务紧密耦合，导致其在无线网络中的可扩展性和可重用性受限，无法有效支持环境感知的6G无线网络。

Method: 首先回顾代表性CKM并讨论其局限性；然后提出uCKM-enabled新范式，从构建和利用两个阶段强调其优势，提出"All for uCKM"和"uCKM for All"愿景；接着讨论主要挑战并提出潜在解决方案；最后通过仿真验证其可行性和性能增益，并展望未来研究方向。

Result: 仿真结果表明uCKM能够为环境感知无线网络带来性能增益，验证了其可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: uCKM作为基础无线环境先验，是实现跨设备和跨任务信道知识迁移的有力途径，为6G环境感知无线网络提供了新的发展方向。

Abstract: Channel knowledge map (CKM) is a promising technology for environment-aware sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks. However, most existing CKMs are tightly coupled with wireless devices and downstream tasks, which limit their scalability and reusability in wireless networks. To address these limitations, this article proposes the concept of universal CKM (uCKM) as a foundational wireless environment prior, which aims to enable cross-device and cross-task channel knowledge transfer for environment-aware wireless networks. We first revisit the representative CKMs and discuss their limitations. Then, the uCKM-enabled new paradigm for environment-aware wireless networks is introduced, and its benefits are highlighted from the perspectives of uCKM construction and utilization phases, for which we propose the visions of ``All for uCKM'' and ``uCKM for All'', i.e., the data acquired by all devices and tasks should contribute to the construction of uCKM, and vice versa. Subsequently, we discuss the main challenges of uCKM and propose potential solutions. Last, we provide simulation results to demonstrate the feasibility and performance gains brought by uCKM and outline future research directions.

</details>


### [306] [Dynamic Question Design for Efficient Estimation of Aggregate Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17459)
*Kazuyoshi Fukuda,Masaki Inoue*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态自适应问答策略来高效估计群体人类偏好，通过贝叶斯估计（粒子滤波）进行偏好估计，并采用基于期望信息增益最大化的epsilon-greedy策略进行问题设计，在理论上给出偏好估计误差下界和搜索效率分析，并通过数值模拟验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效估计群体人类偏好是一个重要问题，传统静态问卷方法效率低下。通过动态调整问题以适应受访者的回答，可以提高估计效率，但这一问题尚未被系统解决。

Method: 将问题分解为两个子问题：(1)偏好估计：使用贝叶斯估计框架，以粒子滤波作为计算高效的近似方法；(2)问题设计：将问题设计建模为期望信息增益最大化问题，采用epsilon-greedy策略进行高效求解。

Result: 在理论层面，通过信息论方法推导了偏好估计误差的理论下界；证明了epsilon-greedy策略的搜索效率优于随机搜索；通过数值模拟验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过动态自适应问题设计结合贝叶斯偏好估计，为高效估计群体偏好提供了一种兼顾计算效率与理论保证的方法，理论上证明了误差下界及搜索效率优势，并通过仿真验证了实际效果。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of efficiently estimating aggregate human preferences by dynamically adapting questions based on respondents'answers. To this end, we formulate and address two sub-problems: preference estimation and question design. First, regarding preference estimation, we model respondents' preferences and estimate them using Bayesian estimation, employing a particle filter as a computationally efficient approximation. The main theoretical contribution to this sub-problem is to analyze the preference estimation error using an information-theoretic approach, deriving a theoretical lower bound for the error. Second, regarding question design, we formulate the design problem as an Expected Information Gain maximization problem and employ an epsilon-greedy strategy to solve the problem in a computationally efficient way. We theoretically analyze the search efficiency of the approach, demonstrating that it achieves higher efficiency than a random search. Finally, we verify the effectiveness of the proposed method through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [307] [Learnware for CSI Feedback: Scene-specific Small Models Can Do Big](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17760)
*Xiangyi Li,Jiajia Guo,Chao-Kai Wen,Xin Geng,Shi Jin,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型仓库和Learnware框架的智能CSI反馈部署方案，通过集中式AI数据中心维护场景特定模型目录，基站仅提交本地统计规范即可检索最相关的预训练模型，实现超过90%的选模准确率，在LOS和NLOS场景下分别比通用模型提升18.8%和57.7%的性能，同时将本地微调减少至多1000个样本和100个epoch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习CSI反馈方案面临模型泛化能力与场景特定性能之间的权衡：大型神经网络泛化好但计算和调参成本高，小型模型在特定环境表现优异但需要为每个基站重复进行昂贵的端到端训练，缺乏高效且隐私友好的模型复用机制。

Method: 提出基于模型仓库的部署框架：(1) 集中式AI数据中心维护场景特定CSI模型目录；(2) 采用Learnware框架，每个模型关联包含语义部分（网络架构参数）和统计部分（codebook指纹嵌入训练数据分布）的规范；(3) 基站仅提交本地统计规范以检索最相关预训练模型，避免传输原始CSI数据；(4) 开发数据驱动的搜索策略，将codebook指纹与模型性能匹配。

Result: 所提方案在预训练模型选择上达到超过90%的准确率；仿真表明在LOS和NLOS场景下分别比通用模型提升18.8%和57.7%的性能；同时将本地微调需求减少至多1000个样本和100个epoch。

Conclusion: 该Learnware-based方法最小化了冗余训练，最大化模型复用，支持快速且隐私增强的CSI反馈模型部署，为6G系统中智能CSI反馈的实际落地提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Intelligent channel state information (CSI) feedback is essential for realizing the high capacity and spectral efficiency goals of future 6G systems, yet existing deep learning solutions face a trade-off between model generalization and scenario-specific performance. Large neural networks generalize well but incur high computational and tuning costs, while small models excel in particular environments but require repetitive costly end-to-end training for each base station (BS). To address these challenges, we introduce a model repository-based deployment framework in which a centralized AI data center maintains a catalog of scene-specific CSI models. The repository is enhanced with a Learnware-based framework, where each model is associated with a specification including semantic part (network architecture parameters) and statistical part (codeboo-fingerprint embeddings of training-data distributions). A BS submits only its local statistical specifications to retrieve the most relevant pre-trained model, enhancing data privacy by avoiding raw CSI transmission and drastically reducing retrieval latency and communication overhead. We further develop a data-driven search strategy that matches codebook fingerprints to model performance, achieving over 90% selection accuracy. In simulations, our scheme yields 18.8% and 57.7% performance improvements over the General Model in LOS and NLOS scenarios, respectively while reducing local fine-tuning by up to 1000 samples and 100 epochs. This Learnware-based approach minimizes redundant training, maximizes model reuse, and supports rapid,privacy-enhancing deployment of CSI feedback models.

</details>


### [308] [On the Estimation of Chernoff Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17916)
*Kadircan Aksoy,Peter Jung*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于k近邻的非参数Chernoff信息估计方法。通过将Chernoff信息的优化问题转化为Rényi散度导数的求根问题，直接利用k近邻方法估计导数，并配合二分法精确定位最优混合参数，从而获得Chernoff信息的估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chernoff信息是贝叶斯二元假设检验中最优误差指数的基础散度度量，在信息融合、时间序列分析和统计学习理论中都有重要应用。然而，其闭式表达式仅存在于简单参数族中，非参数估计一直很困难，因为该量被定义为未归一化Rényi散度在阶数上的优化问题。

Method: 本文通过导数条件重新表述该优化问题（其零点定位最优混合参数），并利用k近邻方法直接估计该导数。结合二分法程序将最优参数定位到任意精度，最终得到Chernoff信息的估计器。

Result: 在密度及其定义域的温和正则条件下，证明了导数估计器的L2一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法为非参数情境下的Chernoff信息估计提供了一种可行且具有理论保证的方案，克服了传统闭式表达式仅适用于简单参数族的局限。

Abstract: Chernoff information is a fundamental divergence measure characterizing the optimal error exponent in Bayesian binary hypothesis testing, with applications in information fusion, time-series analysis, and statistical learning theory. However, closed-form expressions exist only for simple parametric families, and nonparametric estimation remains difficult because the quantity is defined as an optimization of the unnormalized Rényi divergence over its order. We reformulate this optimization via a derivative condition, whose zero locates the optimal mixture parameter, and estimate the derivative directly using a $k$-nearest-neighbor method. We prove the $L_2$-consistency of the derivative estimator under mild regularity conditions on the densities and their domain. Coupled with a bisection procedure that locates the optimal parameter up to arbitrary precision, this yields an estimator for Chernoff information.

</details>


### [309] [Tail exponents of conditional guesswork via the method of types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17949)
*Adway Girish,Andreina Patrizia Motter,Emre Telatar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在元素级相关边信息下的独立同分布随机序列猜测问题，通过类型计数方法估计无边信息情况下猜测次数的尾概率，并将计数论证扩展到条件设置，得到以条件倾斜分布散度表达的新猜测工作指数，最后应用于带边信息的暴力密码猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上猜测序列的尾概率估计依赖大偏差技术，作者希望采用类型计数这种更直接的方法来估计，并将该方法推广到带条件边信息的情形，以获得新的显式表达式。

Method: 使用类型计数（type-counting）方法：首先在没有边信息的场景中估计猜测次数尾概率；然后将同样的计数论证扩展到条件设置，推导出对应的猜测工作指数，其形式为涉及条件倾斜分布的散度表达式。

Result: 在没有边信息的情况下复现了此前通过大偏差技术得到的估计结果；在条件设置下获得了新的显式猜测工作指数表达式，以条件倾斜分布之间的散度形式给出。

Conclusion: 类型计数方法是一种有效的工具，既能统一处理有/无边信息的猜测问题，又能导出新的显式指数表达式，并可直接应用于带边信息的暴力密码猜测场景。

Abstract: We study the problem of guessing a realization of an i.i.d. random sequence given element-wise correlated side-information. We use type-counting to provide estimates of the tail probabilities of the number of guesses for the case without side-information, which was shown earlier through large-deviation techniques. We then extend the same counting argument to the conditional setting, obtaining new explicit expressions for the corresponding guesswork exponents as divergences involving conditional tilted distributions. Finally, we provide an application of these exponents to brute-force password guessing with side-information.

</details>
